First and foremost, it is paramount to highlight that the predictive content inherently embedded in our CAs-estimated factors is particularly pronounced with target variables that play a key role in the design of political, fiscal or monetary policies. These variables are economic growth, inflation (excluding energy and food), unemployment and wages. Since monitoring inflation is at the core of the European Central Bank’s mandate and policymaking, it is very valuable to observe that the estimated factors have formidable forecasting power for consumer prices under all tests and types of persistence. Focusing on individual tests, we see that
s_{\widehat{f},1}
exhibits statistical significance across the largest collection of target variables. While this statistic exhibits power, we know from our simulations that it tends to overreject the null. Therefore, the support from other statistics, especially
s_{\widehat{f},2}^{P}
and
s_{\widehat{f},3}^{P}
, strengthens the evidence for factor predictive power. Unsurprisingly to our theory, our statistics detect better overall forecast accuracy using stationary data, which is still against the popular belief that stationary data incurs a material loss of information content compared to non-stationary data. In fact, this finding was already anticipated by the fact that the convergence rates of our statistics slow down with increasing persistence in the data. Further, while the ultimate objective of our tests is to uncover the predictive specifications where the estimated factors improve upon the (autoregressive) baseline, it is also important to identify those situations where the factors are not informative about the future and should therefore be excluded from our forecasting considerations, as is rightfully and conventionally the case for the changes in, and the level of, the long-term interest rate, the exchange rate and the stock market returns. In fact, these three variables are notoriously difficult, if not impossible, to predict because - assuming the efficient market hypothesis holds - the expectations of rational, informed traders must already be priced into financial markets.

Nevertheless, we acknowledge that our results may inadvertedly be affected by structural breaks in the coefficients of the factor-augmented regression. Although we have dedicated considerable attention to breaks in the factor loadings, there exist no statistical procedure to detect structural instability à la
Corradi and Swanson (2014)
under the CAs framework. In fact, these authors propose a test for the joint hypothesis of structural stability of both factor loadings and regression coefficients in the factor-augmented forecasting model in a PC setting. As previously argued, the period associated with the Covid-19 pandemic should be treated by including an additional factor in the specification, which will by construction capture a portion of the shift in the level of the target variables. Hence, overestimating the number of CAs makes us robust to: 1) the possibility of breaks in the factor loadings, and consequently 2) an increase in the number of factors resulting from a large shock, but possibly also 3) a large break in the factor-augmented regression coefficients. However, it is not unreasonable to think that some concerns about the last point have not been dispelled once and for all. At the same time, our empirical results would not be interpretable fairly under the tests of
Clark and McCracken (2001)
and
McCracken (2007)
due to their strong foundational assumptions about factor properties.

We repeat the same forecasting exercise with data on Germany, France, Italy and Spain. The target variables are the same, with the exception of the 3-month and 6-month interest rates since they are not available at the country level. The results of our tests are reported in Table

.

Although the institutions of these countries are structurally distinct, the results will naturally be heterogeneous across countries. That is, the factors extracted from each country dataset are found to be informative about the future evolution of different variables. In sum, it seems from our results that the macroeconomic factors are still able to offer consistent forecasting insight into economic growth, wages and (possibly) consumer prices across the four countries, with the notable exceptions of Spain and Italy. With respect to Spain, the factors appear to show quite weak predictive ability while, for Italy, they anticipate the bulk of economic activity across most variables, excluding the stock market. As before,
s_{\widehat{f},1}
detects the greatest number of specifications where our macroeconomic factors yield forecasting power. All things considered, we conclude that - although the CAs method may appear deceivingly simplistic at first glance - predictive regressions augmented with the estimated factors offer powerful predictive insight in the context of macroeconomic forecasting in both levels and differences, especially in the presence of possibly big structural breaks.

6
Conclusion

We present four novel tests of equal predictive accuracy and encompassing for factor-augmented regressions under a CAs setup by revisiting
Stauskas and Westerlund (2022)
. While their results in the context of tests by
Clark and McCracken (2001)
bring important practical implications, merging CAs with a novel class of tests by
Pitarakis (2023)
and
Pitarakis (2025)
opens new possibilities. The overspecification of the number of factors has an asymptotically negligible influence, thereby eliminating the need to know the number of factors (as long as it is bounded from above by the number of averages). This goes against the usual trend in this literature according to which the excess CAs result in bias even asymptotically. In practice, the suggested upper bound is often substantial, as can be seen from our empirical application on the novel EA-MD-QD dataset. Further, observed predictors and factors can be persistent, and the location of structural breaks in the factor loadings need not be known. Ultimately, Monte Carlo simulations reveal an excellent small sample performance across many empirically relevant scenarios.

7
Appendix

7.1
Auxiliary Results

In the auxiliary lemmas and Lemma 1, we will use the stacked notation, such that
\mathbf{A}\in\mathbb{R}^{(t-1)\times p}
stacks
p\times 1
vectors over time in a recursive fashion.

7.1.1
General Dependence vs. Uncorrelatedness in the Idiosyncratic Component

Lemma A.1

Under Assumptions 1-4, we have that

\displaystyle\left\|\frac{1}{T^{1+\tau}}\mathbf{Z}^{\prime}\overline{\mathbf{E}}\right\|=O_{p}(N^{-1/2}T^{-\tau/2})

(A.1)

as
(N,T)\to\infty
.

Proof.
To begin with, we can write
\frac{1}{T^{1+\tau}}\mathbf{Z}^{\prime}\overline{\mathbf{E}}=\frac{1}{T^{1+\tau}}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{z}_{t}\overline{\mathbf{e}}_{t}^{\prime}
. For this and the upcoming auxiliary results, we use the total sample for
t=1,\ldots,T
, however, by Assumption 2 (c), the same rates hold in a recursive setup for each
t
. This is so, because by stacking over
s=1,\ldots,t
, we can write
\frac{1}{T^{1+\tau}}\sum_{s=1}^{t}\mathbf{z}_{s}\overline{\mathbf{e}}_{s}^{\prime}=\left(\frac{t}{T}\right)^{1+\tau}\frac{1}{t^{1+\tau}}\sum_{s=1}^{t}\mathbf{z}_{s}\overline{\mathbf{e}}_{s}^{\prime}
, where
t=\lfloor T\eta\rfloor
for
\eta\in(0,1)
. Then for some positive
\epsilon
,

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\left(\left\|\frac{1}{T^{1+\tau}}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{z}_{t}\overline{\mathbf{e}}_{t}^{\prime}\right\|>\epsilon\right)

\displaystyle\leq\epsilon^{-1}\mathbb{E}\left(\left\|\frac{1}{T^{1+\tau}}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{z}_{t}\overline{\mathbf{e}}_{t}^{\prime}\right\|\right)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\epsilon\sqrt{N}}\mathbb{E}\left(\left\|\frac{1}{T^{1+\tau}}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{z}_{t}(\sqrt{N}\overline{\mathbf{e}}_{t}^{\prime})\right\|\right)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{\epsilon\sqrt{N}}\mathbb{E}\left(\frac{1}{T^{1+\tau}}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left\|\mathbf{z}_{t}\right\|\left\|(\sqrt{N}\overline{\mathbf{e}}_{t})\right\|\right)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\epsilon\sqrt{N}}\frac{1}{T^{1+\tau}}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbb{E}(\left\|\mathbf{z}_{t}\right\|)\mathbb{E}\left(\left\|(\sqrt{N}\overline{\mathbf{e}}_{t})\right\|\right)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{\epsilon\sqrt{N}T^{\tau/2}}\left(\frac{1}{T^{1+\tau}}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbb{E}(\left\|\mathbf{z}_{t}\right\|)^{2}\right)^{1/2}\underbrace{\left(\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbb{E}\left(\left\|(\sqrt{N}\overline{\mathbf{e}}_{t})\right\|\right)^{2}\right)^{1/2}}_{O(1)}

\displaystyle=O(N^{-1/2}T^{-\tau/2}),

(A.2)

because

\displaystyle\frac{1}{T^{1+\tau}}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbb{E}(\left\|\mathbf{z}_{t}\right\|)^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{T^{1+\tau}}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\sqrt{(\mathrm{tr}(\mathbf{z}_{t}\mathbf{z}_{t}^{\prime}))}\right)^{2}\right]

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{T^{1+\tau}}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbb{E}(\mathrm{tr}[\mathbf{z}_{t}\mathbf{z}_{t}^{\prime}])=O(1)

(A.3)

by the concavity of function appearing in Jensen’s inequality. Note that this result gives a different rate than for similar terms in
Pitarakis (2023)
, which is brought down exactly by time dependence in
\{\mathbf{e}_{i,t}\}
. Also note that independence between the predictors and idiosyncratics is not strictly necessary, and we use it for simplicity. Indeed,

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\left(\left\|\frac{1}{T^{1+\tau}}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{z}_{t}\overline{\mathbf{e}}_{t}^{\prime}\right\|>\epsilon\right)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{\epsilon\sqrt{N}}\mathbb{E}\left(\frac{1}{T^{1+\tau}}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left\|\mathbf{z}_{t}\right\|\left\|(\sqrt{N}\overline{\mathbf{e}}_{t})\right\|\right)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{\epsilon\sqrt{N}}\frac{1}{T^{1+\tau}}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbb{E}\left(\left\|\mathbf{z}_{t}\right\|^{2}\right)^{1/2}\mathbb{E}\left(\left\|\sqrt{N}\overline{\mathbf{e}}_{t}\right\|^{2}\right)^{1/2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\sup_{t}\mathbb{E}\left(\left\|\sqrt{N}\overline{\mathbf{e}}_{t}\right\|^{2}\right)^{1/2}}{\epsilon\sqrt{N}T^{\tau/2}}\sup_{t}\mathbb{E}\left(\left\|T^{-\tau/2}\mathbf{z}_{t}\right\|^{2}\right)^{1/2}

\displaystyle=O(N^{-1/2}T^{-\tau/2}),

(A.4)

because
\sup_{t}\mathbb{E}\left(\left\|T^{-\tau/2}\mathbf{z}_{t}\right\|^{2}\right)^{1/2}=O(1)
by Lemma 3.1 in
Magdalinos and Phillips (2009)
.

Corollary A.1

Under Assumptions 1-4, but
\mathbf{e}_{i,t}
is uncorrelated over time, we have that

\displaystyle\left\|\frac{1}{T^{1+\tau}}\mathbf{Z}^{\prime}\overline{\mathbf{E}}\right\|=O_{p}(N^{-1/2}T^{-(1+\tau)/2})

(A.5)

as
(N,T)\to\infty
.

Proof.
Assume that
\mathbf{e}_{i,t}
is uncorrelated over time. Then by Markov’s inequality we obtain

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\left(\left\|\frac{1}{T^{1+\tau}}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{z}_{t}\overline{\mathbf{e}}_{t}^{\prime}\right\|>\epsilon\right)

\displaystyle\leq\epsilon^{-2}\mathbb{E}\left(\left\|\frac{1}{T^{1+\tau}}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{z}_{t}\overline{\mathbf{e}}_{t}^{\prime}\right\|^{2}\right)

\displaystyle=\epsilon^{-2}\frac{1}{T^{1+\tau}}\mathbb{E}\left(\mathrm{tr}\left[\frac{1}{T^{1+\tau}}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\sum_{s=1}^{T}\mathbf{z}_{t}\overline{\mathbf{e}}_{t}^{\prime}\overline{\mathbf{e}}_{s}\mathbf{z}_{s}^{\prime}\right]\right)

\displaystyle=\epsilon^{-2}\frac{1}{T^{1+\tau}}\left(\mathrm{tr}\left[\frac{1}{T^{1+\tau}}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\sum_{s=1}^{T}\mathbb{E}\left(\mathbf{z}_{s}^{\prime}\mathbf{z}_{t}\overline{\mathbf{e}}_{t}^{\prime}\overline{\mathbf{e}}_{s}\right)\right]\right)

\displaystyle=\epsilon^{-2}\frac{1}{T^{1+\tau}}\left(\mathrm{tr}\left[\frac{1}{T^{1+\tau}}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\sum_{s=1}^{T}\mathbb{E}\left(\mathbf{z}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{z}_{s}\right)\mathbb{E}\left(\overline{\mathbf{e}}_{t}^{\prime}\overline{\mathbf{e}}_{s}\right)\right]\right)

\displaystyle=\epsilon^{-2}\frac{1}{T^{1+\tau}}\left(\mathrm{tr}\left[\frac{1}{T^{1+\tau}}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbb{E}\left(\mathbf{z}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{z}_{t}\right)\mathbb{E}\left(\overline{\mathbf{e}}_{t}^{\prime}\overline{\mathbf{e}}_{t}\right)\right]\right)

\displaystyle=\epsilon^{-2}\frac{1}{NT^{1+\tau}}\left(\mathrm{tr}\left[\frac{1}{T^{1+\tau}}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbb{E}\left(\mathbf{z}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{z}_{t}\right)\mathbb{E}\left(N\overline{\mathbf{e}}_{t}^{\prime}\overline{\mathbf{e}}_{t}\right)\right]\right)

\displaystyle\leq\epsilon^{-2}\frac{\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T}\mathbb{E}\left(N\overline{\mathbf{e}}_{t}^{\prime}\overline{\mathbf{e}}_{t}\right)}{NT^{1+\tau}}\left(\mathrm{tr}\left[\frac{1}{T^{1+\tau}}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbb{E}\left(\mathbf{z}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{z}_{t}\right)\right]\right)

\displaystyle\leq O(N^{-1}T^{-1-\tau})\times\underbrace{\mathrm{tr}\left[\frac{1}{T^{1+\tau}}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbb{E}\left(\mathbf{z}_{t}\mathbf{z}_{t}^{\prime}\right)\right]}_{O(1)}

\displaystyle=O(N^{-1}T^{-1-\tau}),

(A.6)

which implies that
\left\|\frac{1}{T^{1+\tau}}\mathbf{Z}^{\prime}\overline{\mathbf{E}}\right\|=O_{p}(N^{-1/2}T^{-(1+\tau)/2})
if
\mathbf{e}_{i,t}
is uncorrelated over time.

Lemma A.2

Under Assumptions 1-4, we have that

\displaystyle\left\|\frac{1}{T^{1+\tau}}\mathbf{Z}^{\prime}\mathbf{u}\right\|=O_{p}(T^{-(1+\tau)/2})

as
T\to\infty
.

Proof.
By using similar steps as in Lemma A.1, we get

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\left(\left\|\frac{1}{T^{1+\tau}}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{z}_{t}u_{t+1}\right\|>\epsilon\right)

\displaystyle\leq\epsilon^{-2}\mathbb{E}\left(\left\|\frac{1}{T^{1+\tau}}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{z}_{t}u_{t+1}\right\|^{2}\right)

\displaystyle=\epsilon^{-2}\frac{1}{T^{1+\tau}}\mathbb{E}\left(\frac{1}{T^{1+\tau}}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\sum_{s=1}^{T}\mathbf{z}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{z}_{s}u_{t+1}u_{s+1}\right)

\displaystyle=\epsilon^{-2}\frac{1}{T^{1+\tau}}\mathbb{E}\left(\frac{1}{T^{1+\tau}}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{z}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{z}_{t}\mathbb{E}(u_{t+1}^{2}|\mathcal{F}_{t})\right)

\displaystyle=\frac{\sigma^{2}}{\epsilon^{2}T^{1+\tau}}\frac{1}{T^{1+\tau}}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathrm{tr}\left[\mathbb{E}(\mathbf{z}_{t}\mathbf{z}_{t}^{\prime})\right]

\displaystyle=O(T^{-(1+\tau)}),

(A.7)

which implies that
\left\|\frac{1}{T^{1+\tau}}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{z}_{t}u_{t+1}\right\|=O_{p}(T^{-(1+\tau)/2})
. Note that this rate is effectively assumed in
Pitarakis (2023)
and
Pitarakis (2025)
. However, it naturally follows from our more primitive assumptions.

7.1.2
Extension to
MA(p)
Idiosyncratics

We can extend our analysis and proceed to develop a moving average analog of (
A.6
) by assuming that
\mathbf{e}_{i,t}
follows an
MA(p)
process.

Lemma A.3

Under Assumptions 1-4 and idiosyncratics following and
MA(p)
process, we have that

\displaystyle\left\|\frac{1}{T^{1+\tau}}\mathbf{Z}^{\prime}\overline{\mathbf{E}}\right\|=O_{p}(N^{-1/2}T^{-(1+\tau)/2})

(A.8)

as
(N,T)\to\infty
.

Proof.
By applying the same chain of inequalities, we obtain

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}

\displaystyle\left(\left\|\frac{1}{T^{1+\tau}}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{z}_{t}\overline{\mathbf{e}}_{t}^{\prime}\right\|>\epsilon\right)\leq\epsilon^{-2}\frac{1}{T^{1+\tau}}\left(\mathrm{tr}\left[\frac{1}{T^{1+\tau}}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\sum_{s=1}^{T}\mathbb{E}\left(\mathbf{z}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{z}_{s}\right)\mathbb{E}\left(\overline{\mathbf{e}}_{t}^{\prime}\overline{\mathbf{e}}_{s}\right)\right]\right)

\displaystyle=\epsilon^{-2}\frac{1}{T^{1+\tau}}\left(\frac{1}{T^{1+\tau}}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\sum_{s=\max\{1,t-p\}}^{\min\{T,t+p\}}\mathbb{E}\left(\mathbf{z}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{z}_{s}\right)\mathbb{E}\left(\overline{\mathbf{e}}_{t}^{\prime}\overline{\mathbf{e}}_{s}\right)\right)

\displaystyle\leq\epsilon^{-2}\frac{1}{T^{1+\tau}}\left(\frac{1}{T^{1+\tau}}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\sum_{s=\max\{1,t-p\}}^{\min\{T,t+p\}}\mathbb{E}\left(|\mathbf{z}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{z}_{s}|\right)\left|\mathbb{E}\left(\overline{\mathbf{e}}_{t}^{\prime}\overline{\mathbf{e}}_{s}\right)\right|\right)

\displaystyle\leq\epsilon^{-2}\frac{1}{NT^{1+\tau}}\sup_{t,s}\left|\mathbb{E}\left(N\overline{\mathbf{e}}_{t}^{\prime}\overline{\mathbf{e}}_{s}\right)\right|\left(\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\sum_{s=\max\{1,t-p\}}^{\min\{T,t+p\}}\mathbb{E}\left(\left\|T^{-\tau/2}\mathbf{z}_{t}\right\|\left\|T^{-\tau/2}\mathbf{z}_{s}\right\|\right)\right)

\displaystyle\leq\epsilon^{-2}\frac{1}{NT^{1+\tau}}\sup_{t,s}\left|\mathbb{E}\left(N\overline{\mathbf{e}}_{t}^{\prime}\overline{\mathbf{e}}_{s}\right)\right|\left(\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\sum_{s=\max\{1,t-p\}}^{\min\{T,t+p\}}\left[\mathbb{E}\left(\left\|T^{-\tau/2}\mathbf{z}_{t}\right\|^{2}\right)\right]^{1/2}\left[\mathbb{E}\left(\left\|T^{-\tau/2}\mathbf{z}_{s}\right\|^{2}\right)\right]^{1/2}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\epsilon^{-2}\frac{1}{NT^{1+\tau}}\sup_{t,s}\left|\mathbb{E}\left(N\overline{\mathbf{e}}_{t}^{\prime}\overline{\mathbf{e}}_{s}\right)\right|\sup_{t}\mathbb{E}\left(\left\|T^{-\tau/2}\mathbf{z}_{t}\right\|^{2}\right)\times B

\displaystyle=O(N^{-1}T^{-1-\tau})

(A.9)

for some
B>0
. Here we used the fact that
\sup_{t}\left[\mathbb{E}\left(\left\|T^{-\tau/2}\mathbf{z}_{t}\right\|^{2}\right)\right]^{1/2}\times\sup_{s}\left[\mathbb{E}\left(\left\|T^{-\tau/2}\mathbf{z}_{s}\right\|^{2}\right)\right]^{1/2}=\sup_{t}\mathbb{E}\left(\left\|T^{-\tau/2}\mathbf{z}_{t}\right\|^{2}\right)=O(1)
, and the fact that the sum over an index
s
is necessarily bounded in case of
MA(h-1)
. This implies that
\left\|\frac{1}{T^{1+\tau}}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{z}_{t}\overline{\mathbf{e}}_{t}^{\prime}\right\|=O_{p}(N^{-1/2}T^{-(1+\tau)/2})
.

7.2
Using the Rotation Matrix

The case when
m>r
is problematic. One reason for this is that
\overline{\boldsymbol{\Lambda}}
is no longer invertible. However, we still need an equivalent of

\displaystyle\overline{\boldsymbol{\Lambda}}^{-1\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{f}}_{t}=\mathbf{f}_{t}+O_{p}(N^{-1/2}),

(A.10)

because it determines the object that is being estimated. The way we approach this issue is by introducing the following rotation matrix, which is chosen such that
\overline{\boldsymbol{\Lambda}}\overline{\mathbf{H}}=[\mathbf{I}_{r},\mathbf{0}_{r\times(m-r)}]
and that is going to play the same role as
\overline{\boldsymbol{\Lambda}}^{-1}
under
m=r
:

\overline{\mathbf{H}}=\left[\begin{array}[]{cc}\overline{\boldsymbol{\Lambda}}_{r}^{-1}&-\overline{\boldsymbol{\Lambda}}_{r}^{-1}\overline{\boldsymbol{\Lambda}}_{-r}\\
\mathbf{0}_{(m-r)\times r}&\mathbf{I}_{m-r}\end{array}\right]=[\overline{\mathbf{H}}_{r},\,\overline{\mathbf{H}}_{-r}]\in\mathbb{R}^{m\times m},

(A.11)

where
\overline{\mathbf{H}}_{r}=[\overline{\boldsymbol{\Lambda}}_{r}^{-1\prime},\mathbf{0}_{r\times(m-r)}]^{\prime}\in\mathbb{R}^{m\times r}
and
\overline{\mathbf{H}}_{-r}=[-\overline{\boldsymbol{\Lambda}}_{-r}^{\prime}\overline{\boldsymbol{\Lambda}}_{r}^{-1\prime},\mathbf{I}_{m-r}]^{\prime}\in\mathbb{R}^{m\times(m-r)}
. If
m=r
, we define
\overline{\mathbf{H}}=\overline{\mathbf{H}}_{r}=\overline{\boldsymbol{\Lambda}}_{r}^{-1}=\overline{\boldsymbol{\Lambda}}^{-1}
. We further introduce
\mathbf{D}_{N}=\mathrm{diag}(\mathbf{I}_{r},\sqrt{N}\mathbf{I}_{m-r})\in\mathbb{R}^{m\times m}

with
\mathbf{D}_{N}=\mathbf{I}_{m}
if
m=r
. By pre-multiplying
\widehat{\mathbf{f}}_{t}
by
\mathbf{D}_{N}\overline{\mathbf{H}}^{\prime}
, we obtain

\mathbf{D}_{N}\overline{\mathbf{H}}^{\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{f}}_{t}=\widehat{\mathbf{f}}^{0}_{t}=\mathbf{D}_{N}\overline{\mathbf{H}}^{\prime}\overline{\boldsymbol{\Lambda}}^{\prime}\mathbf{f}_{t}+\mathbf{D}_{N}\overline{\mathbf{H}}^{\prime}\overline{\mathbf{e}}_{t}=\mathbf{f}^{0}_{t}+\overline{\mathbf{e}}^{0}_{t},

(A.12)

where
\mathbf{f}^{0}_{t}=[\mathbf{f}^{\prime}_{t},\mathbf{0}_{(m-r)\times 1}^{\prime}]^{\prime}\in\mathbb{R}^{m}
and
\overline{\mathbf{e}}^{0}_{t}=[\overline{\mathbf{e}}_{r,t}^{\prime}\overline{\boldsymbol{\Lambda}}_{r}^{-1},\sqrt{N}(\overline{\mathbf{e}}_{-r,t}-\overline{\boldsymbol{\Lambda}}_{-r}^{\prime}\overline{\boldsymbol{\Lambda}}_{r}^{-1\prime}\overline{\mathbf{e}}_{r,t})^{\prime}]^{\prime}=[\overline{\mathbf{e}}^{0\prime}_{r,t},\overline{\mathbf{e}}^{0\prime}_{-r,t}]^{\prime}

\in\mathbb{R}^{m}
with
\overline{\mathbf{e}}_{r,t}\in\mathbb{R}^{r}
and
\overline{\mathbf{e}}_{-r,t}\in\mathbb{R}^{m-r}
being the partitions of
\overline{\mathbf{e}}_{t}=[\overline{\mathbf{e}}_{r,t}^{\prime},\overline{\mathbf{e}}_{-r,t}^{\prime}]^{\prime}
. If
m=r
, then
\widehat{\mathbf{f}}^{0}_{t}=\overline{\boldsymbol{\Lambda}}^{-1\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{f}}_{t}
,
\mathbf{f}^{0}_{t}=\mathbf{f}_{t}
and
\overline{\mathbf{e}}^{0}_{t}=\overline{\boldsymbol{\Lambda}}^{-1\prime}\overline{\mathbf{e}}_{t}
, and so we are back in (
A.10
). Hence, since
\|\overline{\mathbf{e}}_{r,t}^{0}\|=O_{p}(N^{-1/2})
and
\|\overline{\mathbf{e}}_{-r,t}^{0}\|=O_{p}(1)
, when
m>r
we are no longer estimating
\mathbf{f}_{t}
but rather
[\mathbf{f}_{t}^{\prime},\overline{\mathbf{e}}_{-r,t}^{0\prime}]^{\prime}
.
8
8

8

The scaling by
\sqrt{N}
in
\mathbf{D}_{N}
and hence in
\overline{\mathbf{e}}_{-r,t}^{0}
is necessary, for without it
\overline{\mathbf{H}}^{\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{f}}_{t}
is estimating
[\mathbf{f}_{t}^{\prime},\mathbf{0}_{(m-r)\times 1}^{\prime}]^{\prime}
, whose second moment matrix is singular.
The fact that
\mathbf{f}_{t}
is included in this object suggests that asymptotically, CAs should be able to account for the unknown factors even if
m>r
. By ensuring the existence of
\overline{\mathbf{H}}
, Assumption 5 makes this possible. However, we also note that because of the presence of
\overline{\mathbf{e}}_{-r,t}^{0}
, the asymptotic distribution theory will depend on whether
m=r
or
m>r
.

7.3
Proofs of the Main Results

7.3.1
Proof of Lemma 1

To prove Lemma 1, let us begin from the decomposition in
Stauskas and Westerlund (2022)
(proof of Lemma 1, Appendix A):

\displaystyle\mathbf{D}_{T}(\mathbf{Q}_{N}^{-1}\boldsymbol{\widehat{\delta}}_{t}-\boldsymbol{\widetilde{\delta}_{t}^{0}})

(A.13)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\begin{bmatrix}\mathbf{D}_{\mathbf{F}}\left((\widehat{\mathbf{F}}^{0\prime}\mathbf{M}_{\mathbf{W}}{\widehat{\mathbf{F}}^{0}})^{+}\widehat{\mathbf{F}}^{0\prime}\mathbf{M}_{\mathbf{W}}-\begin{bmatrix}({\mathbf{F}^{\prime}}\mathbf{M}_{\mathbf{W}}{\mathbf{F}})^{+}{\mathbf{F}^{\prime}}\mathbf{M}_{\mathbf{W}}\\
\mathbf{0}_{(m-r)\times(t-1)}\end{bmatrix}\right)\widehat{\mathbf{Z}}^{0}\boldsymbol{\delta}^{0}\\
T^{\frac{1}{4}+\frac{\tau}{2}}\mathbf{I}_{q}\bigl[({\mathbf{W}^{\prime}}\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{\mathbf{F}}^{0}}{\mathbf{W}})^{+}{\mathbf{W}^{\prime}}\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{\mathbf{F}}^{0}}-({\mathbf{W}^{\prime}}\mathbf{M}_{\mathbf{F}}{\mathbf{W}})^{+}{\mathbf{W}^{\prime}}\mathbf{M}_{\mathbf{F}}\bigr]\widehat{\mathbf{Z}}^{0}\boldsymbol{\delta}^{0}\end{bmatrix}

\displaystyle-

\displaystyle\begin{bmatrix}\mathbf{D}_{\mathbf{F}}\left((\widehat{\mathbf{F}}^{0\prime}\mathbf{M}_{\mathbf{W}}{\widehat{\mathbf{F}}^{0}})^{+}\widehat{\mathbf{F}}^{0\prime}\mathbf{M}_{\mathbf{W}}-\begin{bmatrix}({\mathbf{F}^{\prime}}\mathbf{M}_{\mathbf{W}}{\mathbf{F}})^{+}{\mathbf{F}^{\prime}}\mathbf{M}_{\mathbf{W}}\\
\mathbf{0}_{(m-r)\times(t-1)}\end{bmatrix}\right)\overline{\mathbf{E}}_{r}^{0}\boldsymbol{\alpha}\\
T^{\frac{1}{4}+\frac{\tau}{2}}\mathbf{I}_{q}\bigl[({\mathbf{W}^{\prime}}\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{\mathbf{F}}^{0}}{\mathbf{W}})^{+}{\mathbf{W}^{\prime}}\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{\mathbf{F}}^{0}}-({\mathbf{W}^{\prime}}\mathbf{M}_{\mathbf{F}}{\mathbf{W}})^{+}{\mathbf{W}^{\prime}}\mathbf{M}_{\mathbf{F}}\bigr]\overline{\mathbf{E}}_{r}^{0}\boldsymbol{\alpha}\end{bmatrix}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\begin{bmatrix}\mathbf{D}_{\mathbf{F}}\left((\widehat{\mathbf{F}}^{0\prime}\mathbf{M}_{\mathbf{W}}{\widehat{\mathbf{F}}^{0}})^{+}\widehat{\mathbf{F}}^{0\prime}\mathbf{M}_{\mathbf{W}}-\begin{bmatrix}({\mathbf{F}^{\prime}}\mathbf{M}_{\mathbf{W}}{\mathbf{F}})^{+}{\mathbf{F}^{\prime}}\mathbf{M}_{\mathbf{W}}\\
\mathbf{0}_{(m-r)\times(t-1)}\end{bmatrix}\right)\mathbf{u}\\
T^{\frac{1}{4}+\frac{\tau}{2}}\mathbf{I}_{q}\bigl[({\mathbf{W}^{\prime}}\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{\mathbf{F}}^{0}}{\mathbf{W}})^{+}{\mathbf{W}^{\prime}}\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{\mathbf{F}}^{0}}-({\mathbf{W}^{\prime}}\mathbf{M}_{\mathbf{F}}{\mathbf{W}})^{+}{\mathbf{W}^{\prime}}\mathbf{M}_{\mathbf{F}}\bigr]\mathbf{u}\end{bmatrix}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\begin{bmatrix}\mathrm{\mathbf{I}}\\
\mathrm{\mathbf{I}\mathbf{I}}\end{bmatrix}-\begin{bmatrix}\mathrm{\mathbf{I}\mathbf{I}\mathbf{I}}\\
\mathrm{\mathbf{I}V}\end{bmatrix}+\begin{bmatrix}\mathrm{\mathbf{V}}\\
\mathrm{\mathbf{V}\mathbf{I}}\end{bmatrix},

where
\mathbf{D}_{T}=\mathrm{diag}(\mathbf{D}_{\mathbf{F}},T^{\frac{1}{4}+\frac{\tau}{2}}\mathbf{I}_{q})
with
\mathbf{D}_{\mathbf{F}}=\mathrm{diag}(T^{\frac{1}{4}+\frac{\tau}{2}}\mathbf{I}_{r},T^{1/4}\mathbf{I}_{m-r})
and the definitions of
\mathrm{\mathbf{I}}-\mathrm{\mathbf{V}I}
are implicit. WE assume that
\mathbf{e}_{i,t}
is uncorrelated over time. Note that the matrices above stack observations over
s=1,\ldots,t-1
, therefore, we conduct the proof for a fixed
t
. However, under our assumptions, the rates will hold uniformly.

Now we can work out the order of each of the terms by applying simple adjustments to the proofs of and by using the results in
Stauskas and Westerlund (2022)
. Indeed, starting with
\mathrm{\mathbf{I}}
, and recalling that
\widehat{\mathbf{Z}}^{0}\boldsymbol{\delta}^{0}=\widehat{\mathbf{F}}^{0}{(\mathbf{D}_{N}\overline{\mathbf{H}}^{\prime}\overline{\boldsymbol{\Lambda}}^{\prime})^{+\prime}}\boldsymbol{\alpha}+\mathbf{W}\boldsymbol{\theta}
,
\boldsymbol{\alpha}=T^{-\frac{1}{4}-\frac{\tau}{2}}\boldsymbol{\alpha}^{0}
,
\mathbf{M}_{\mathbf{W}}\mathbf{W}=\mathbf{0}_{(t-1)\times n}
, and the fact that
\widehat{\mathbf{F}}^{0\prime}\mathbf{M}_{\mathbf{W}}{\widehat{\mathbf{F}}^{0}}
is a full rank matrix wp1, then

\displaystyle\mathbf{D}_{\mathbf{F}}(\widehat{\mathbf{F}}^{0\prime}\mathbf{M}_{\mathbf{W}}{\widehat{\mathbf{F}}^{0}})^{+}\widehat{\mathbf{F}}^{0\prime}\mathbf{M}_{\mathbf{W}}\widehat{\mathbf{Z}}^{0}\boldsymbol{\delta}^{0}

(A.14)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathbf{D}_{\mathbf{F}}(\widehat{\mathbf{F}}^{0\prime}\mathbf{M}_{\mathbf{W}}{\widehat{\mathbf{F}}^{0}})^{+}\widehat{\mathbf{F}}^{0\prime}\mathbf{M}_{\mathbf{W}}\widehat{\mathbf{F}}^{0}{(\mathbf{D}_{N}\overline{\mathbf{H}}^{\prime}\overline{\boldsymbol{\Lambda}}^{\prime})^{+\prime}}\boldsymbol{\alpha}+\mathbf{D}_{\mathbf{F}}(\widehat{\mathbf{F}}^{0\prime}\mathbf{M}_{\mathbf{W}}{\widehat{\mathbf{F}}^{0}})^{+}\widehat{\mathbf{F}}^{0\prime}\mathbf{M}_{\mathbf{W}}\mathbf{W}\boldsymbol{\theta}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{(\mathbf{D}_{N}\overline{\mathbf{H}}^{\prime}\overline{\boldsymbol{\Lambda}}^{\prime})^{+\prime}}\boldsymbol{\alpha}^{0}=[\boldsymbol{\alpha}^{0\prime},\mathbf{0}_{1\times(m-r))}]^{\prime},

which holds wp1. Further recall that
({\mathbf{F}^{\prime}}\mathbf{M}_{\mathbf{W}}{\mathbf{F}})^{+}{\mathbf{F}^{\prime}}\mathbf{M}_{\mathbf{W}}=(\mathbf{D}_{N}\overline{\mathbf{H}}^{\prime}\overline{\boldsymbol{\Lambda}}^{\prime})^{\prime}({{{\mathbf{F}}^{0}}^{\prime}}\mathbf{M}_{\mathbf{W}}{{\mathbf{F}}^{0}})^{+}{{{\mathbf{F}}^{0}}^{\prime}}\mathbf{M}_{\mathbf{W}}
,
\overline{\mathbf{E}}^{0}(\mathbf{D}_{N}\overline{\mathbf{H}}^{\prime}\overline{\boldsymbol{\Lambda}}^{\prime})^{\prime}\boldsymbol{\alpha}=T^{\frac{1}{4}+\frac{\tau}{2}}\overline{\mathbf{E}}_{r}^{0}\boldsymbol{\alpha}^{0}
and the fact that
{\mathbf{F}^{\prime}}\mathbf{M}_{\mathbf{W}}{\mathbf{F}}
is full rank and positive definite wp1. Then

\displaystyle t^{-1-\tau}{\mathbf{F}^{\prime}}\mathbf{M}_{\mathbf{W}}{\mathbf{F}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle t^{-1-\tau}{\mathbf{F}^{\prime}}{\mathbf{F}}-t^{-1-\tau}{\mathbf{F}^{\prime}}\mathbf{W}(t^{-1-\tau}{\mathbf{W}^{\prime}}{\mathbf{W}})^{+}t^{-1-\tau}{\mathbf{W}^{\prime}}{\mathbf{F}}

(A.15)

\displaystyle\to_{p}

\displaystyle\boldsymbol{\Sigma}_{ff}-\boldsymbol{\Sigma}_{wf}^{\prime}\boldsymbol{\Sigma}_{ww}^{-1}\boldsymbol{\Sigma}_{fw}=\boldsymbol{\Sigma}_{f.w},

which is positive definite by assumption because
\boldsymbol{\Sigma}_{ZZ}
is (Assumption 2). In addition, the rank of
T^{-1-\tau}{\mathbf{F}^{\prime}}\mathbf{M}_{\mathbf{W}}{\mathbf{F}}
is invariant to changes in
T\to\infty
, so

\displaystyle\big\|(t^{-1-\tau}{\mathbf{F}^{\prime}}\mathbf{M}_{\mathbf{W}}{\mathbf{F}})^{+}-\boldsymbol{\Sigma}_{f.w}^{+}\big\|=o_{p}(1).

Thus, it follows that

\displaystyle\big\|(t^{-1-\tau}{\mathbf{F}^{\prime}}\mathbf{M}_{\mathbf{W}}{\mathbf{F}})^{+}\big\|=O_{p}(1).

(A.16)

In a similar manner,
\big\|(t^{-1-\tau}{\mathbf{W}^{\prime}}{\mathbf{W}})^{+}\big\|=O_{p}(1)
. Turning now to
T^{-1-\tau}{\mathbf{F}^{\prime}}\mathbf{M}_{\mathbf{W}}\overline{\mathbf{E}}_{r}^{0}
,

\displaystyle\big\|T^{-1-\tau}{\mathbf{F}^{\prime}}\mathbf{M}_{\mathbf{W}}\overline{\mathbf{E}}_{r}^{0}\boldsymbol{\alpha}^{0}\big\|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\big\|T^{-1-\tau}{\mathbf{F}^{\prime}}\overline{\mathbf{E}}_{r}^{0}\big\|\big\|\boldsymbol{\alpha}^{0}\big\|-\big\|T^{-1-\tau}{\mathbf{F}^{\prime}}\mathbf{W}\big\|\big\|(T^{-1-\tau}{\mathbf{W}^{\prime}}{\mathbf{W}})^{+}\big\|\big\|T^{-1-\tau}{\mathbf{W}^{\prime}}\overline{\mathbf{E}}_{r}^{0}\big\|\big\|\boldsymbol{\alpha}^{0}\big\|

(A.17)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle O_{p}(N^{-\frac{1}{2}}T^{-\frac{1}{2}-\frac{\tau}{2}}).

By using these results,

\displaystyle\mathbf{T}^{\frac{1}{4}+\frac{\tau}{2}}\mathbf{I}_{r}({\mathbf{F}^{\prime}}\mathbf{M}_{\mathbf{W}}{\mathbf{F}})^{+}{\mathbf{F}^{\prime}}\mathbf{M}_{\mathbf{W}}\widehat{\mathbf{Z}}^{0}\boldsymbol{\delta}^{0}

(A.18)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle T^{\frac{1}{4}+\frac{\tau}{2}}\boldsymbol{\alpha}+T^{\frac{1}{4}+\frac{\tau}{2}}({\mathbf{F}^{\prime}}\mathbf{M}_{\mathbf{W}}{\mathbf{F}})^{+}{\mathbf{F}^{\prime}}\mathbf{M}_{\mathbf{W}}\overline{\mathbf{E}}_{r}^{0}\boldsymbol{\alpha}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\boldsymbol{\alpha}^{0}+(T^{-1-\tau}{\mathbf{F}^{\prime}}\mathbf{M}_{\mathbf{W}}{\mathbf{F}})^{+}T^{-1-\tau}{\mathbf{F}^{\prime}}\mathbf{M}_{\mathbf{W}}\overline{\mathbf{E}}_{r}^{0}\boldsymbol{\alpha}^{0}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\boldsymbol{\alpha}^{0}+O_{p}(N^{-\frac{1}{2}}T^{-\frac{1}{2}-\frac{\tau}{2}}).

By combining the results in (
A.14
) and (
A.18
), we obtain

\displaystyle\mathrm{\mathbf{I}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathbf{D}_{\mathbf{F}}\left((\widehat{\mathbf{F}}^{0\prime}\mathbf{M}_{\mathbf{W}}{\widehat{\mathbf{F}}^{0}})^{+}\widehat{\mathbf{F}}^{0\prime}\mathbf{M}_{\mathbf{W}}-\begin{bmatrix}({\mathbf{F}^{\prime}}\mathbf{M}_{\mathbf{W}}{\mathbf{F}})^{+}{\mathbf{F}^{\prime}}\mathbf{M}_{\mathbf{W}}\\
\mathbf{0}_{(m-r)\times(t-1)}\end{bmatrix}\right)\widehat{\mathbf{Z}}^{0}\boldsymbol{\delta}^{0}

(A.19)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\begin{bmatrix}\boldsymbol{\alpha}^{0}\\
\mathbf{0}_{(m-r)\times 1}\end{bmatrix}-\left(\begin{bmatrix}\boldsymbol{\alpha}^{0}\\
\mathbf{0}_{(m-r)\times 1}\end{bmatrix}+O_{p}(N^{-\frac{1}{2}}T^{-\frac{1}{2}-\frac{\tau}{2}}))\right)=O_{p}(N^{-\frac{1}{2}}T^{-\frac{1}{2}-\frac{\tau}{2}}).

Consider now
\mathrm{\mathbf{I}I}
. Since
\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{\mathbf{F}}^{0}}\widehat{\mathbf{F}}^{0}=\mathbf{0}_{(t-1)\times m}
then it can be shown that

\displaystyle T^{\frac{1}{4}+\frac{\tau}{2}}({\mathbf{W}^{\prime}}\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{\mathbf{F}}^{0}}{\mathbf{W}})^{+}{\mathbf{W}^{\prime}}\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{\mathbf{F}}^{0}}\widehat{\mathbf{Z}}^{0}\boldsymbol{\delta}^{0}

(A.20)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle T^{\frac{1}{4}+\frac{\tau}{2}}({\mathbf{W}^{\prime}}\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{\mathbf{F}}^{0}}{\mathbf{W}})^{+}{\mathbf{W}^{\prime}}\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{\mathbf{F}}^{0}}(\widehat{\mathbf{F}}^{0}{(\mathbf{D}_{N}\overline{\mathbf{H}}^{\prime}\overline{\boldsymbol{\Lambda}}^{\prime})^{+\prime}}\boldsymbol{\alpha}+\mathbf{W}\boldsymbol{\theta})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle T^{\frac{1}{4}+\frac{\tau}{2}}({\mathbf{W}^{\prime}}\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{\mathbf{F}}^{0}}{\mathbf{W}})^{+}{\mathbf{W}^{\prime}}\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{\mathbf{F}}^{0}}\widehat{\mathbf{F}}^{0}{(\mathbf{D}_{N}\overline{\mathbf{H}}^{\prime}\overline{\boldsymbol{\Lambda}}^{\prime})^{+\prime}}\boldsymbol{\alpha}+T^{\frac{1}{4}+\frac{\tau}{2}}({\mathbf{W}^{\prime}}\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{\mathbf{F}}^{0}}{\mathbf{W}})^{+}{\mathbf{W}^{\prime}}\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{\mathbf{F}}^{0}}\mathbf{W}\boldsymbol{\theta}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle T^{\frac{1}{4}+\frac{\tau}{2}}\boldsymbol{\theta},

whereas using the definition and properties of
\mathbf{F}^{0}
together with previous results gives

\displaystyle T^{\frac{1}{4}+\frac{\tau}{2}}({\mathbf{W}^{\prime}}\mathbf{M}_{\mathbf{F}}{\mathbf{W}})^{+}{\mathbf{W}^{\prime}}\mathbf{M}_{\mathbf{F}}\widehat{\mathbf{Z}}^{0}\boldsymbol{\delta}^{0}

(A.21)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle T^{\frac{1}{4}+\frac{\tau}{2}}({\mathbf{W}^{\prime}}\mathbf{M}_{\mathbf{F}}{\mathbf{W}})^{+}{\mathbf{W}^{\prime}}\mathbf{M}_{\mathbf{F}}\overline{\mathbf{E}}_{r}^{0}\boldsymbol{\alpha}+T^{\frac{1}{4}+\frac{\tau}{2}}\boldsymbol{\theta}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle(T^{-1-\tau}{\mathbf{W}^{\prime}}\mathbf{M}_{\mathbf{F}}{\mathbf{W}})^{+}T^{-1-\tau}{\mathbf{W}^{\prime}}\mathbf{M}_{\mathbf{F}}\overline{\mathbf{E}}_{r}^{0}\boldsymbol{\alpha}^{0}+T^{\frac{1}{4}+\frac{\tau}{2}}\boldsymbol{\theta}.

Observe that
{\mathbf{W}^{\prime}}\mathbf{M}_{\mathbf{F}}{\mathbf{W}}
is positive definite wp1 and

\displaystyle t^{1+\tau}T^{-1-\tau}t^{-1-\tau}{\mathbf{W}^{\prime}}\mathbf{M}_{\mathbf{F}}{\mathbf{W}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle(t^{1+\tau}T^{-1-\tau})[t^{-1-\tau}\mathbf{W}^{\prime}\mathbf{W}-t^{-1-\tau}{\mathbf{W}^{\prime}}\mathbf{F}(t^{-1-\tau}{\mathbf{F}^{\prime}}{\mathbf{F}})^{+}t^{-1-\tau}{\mathbf{F}^{\prime}}{\mathbf{W}}]

(A.22)

\displaystyle\to_{p}

\displaystyle s^{1+\tau}[\boldsymbol{\Sigma}_{ww}-\boldsymbol{\Sigma}_{fw}^{\prime}\boldsymbol{\Sigma}_{ff}^{-1}\boldsymbol{\Sigma}_{wf}]=s^{1+\tau}\boldsymbol{\Sigma}_{w.f}

in light of the fact that

\displaystyle\sup_{1\leq t\leq T}\quad\sup_{(t-1)T^{-1}\leq s\leq tT^{-1}}|(tT^{-1})^{k}-s^{k}|=O(T^{-1}),\quad\forall k>0.

(A.23)

Since the rank of
T^{-1-\tau}{\mathbf{W}^{\prime}}\mathbf{M}_{\mathbf{F}}{\mathbf{W}}
does not change as
T\to\infty
, then

\displaystyle\big\|(T^{-1-\tau}{\mathbf{W}^{\prime}}\mathbf{M}_{\mathbf{F}}{\mathbf{W}})^{+}-s^{-1-\tau}\boldsymbol{\Sigma}_{w.f}^{+}\big\|=o_{p}(1).

(A.24)

Thus, it follows that

\displaystyle\big\|(T^{-1-\tau}{\mathbf{W}^{\prime}}\mathbf{M}_{\mathbf{F}}{\mathbf{W}})^{+}\big\|=O_{p}(1).

(A.25)

Further, since

\displaystyle\big\|T^{-1-\tau}{\mathbf{W}^{\prime}}\mathbf{M}_{\mathbf{F}}\overline{\mathbf{E}}_{r}^{0}\boldsymbol{\alpha}^{0}\big\|

\displaystyle\leq\big\|T^{-1-\tau}{\mathbf{W}^{\prime}}\overline{\mathbf{E}}_{r}^{0}\big\|\big\|\boldsymbol{\alpha}^{0}\big\|+\big\|T^{-1-\tau}{\mathbf{W}^{\prime}}\mathbf{F}\big\|\big\|(T^{-1-\tau}{\mathbf{F}^{\prime}}{\mathbf{F}})^{+}\big\|\big\|T^{-1-\tau}{\mathbf{F}^{\prime}}\overline{\mathbf{E}}_{r}^{0}\big\|\big\|\boldsymbol{\alpha}^{0}\big\|

\displaystyle=O_{p}(N^{-\frac{1}{2}}T^{-\frac{1}{2}-\frac{\tau}{2}}),

(A.26)

implying that

\displaystyle\big\|(T^{-1-\tau}{\mathbf{W}^{\prime}}\mathbf{M}_{\mathbf{F}}{\mathbf{W}})^{+}T^{-1-\tau}{\mathbf{W}^{\prime}}\mathbf{M}_{\mathbf{F}}\overline{\mathbf{E}}_{r}^{0}\boldsymbol{\alpha}^{0}\big\|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\big\|(T^{-1-\tau}{\mathbf{W}^{\prime}}\mathbf{M}_{\mathbf{F}}{\mathbf{W}})^{+}\big\|\big\|T^{-1-\tau}{\mathbf{W}^{\prime}}\mathbf{M}_{\mathbf{F}}\overline{\mathbf{E}}_{r}^{0}\big\|\big\|\boldsymbol{\alpha}^{0}\big\|=O_{p}(N^{-\frac{1}{2}}T^{-\frac{1}{2}-\frac{\tau}{2}})

so that

\displaystyle T^{\frac{1}{4}+\frac{\tau}{2}}({\mathbf{W}^{\prime}}\mathbf{M}_{\mathbf{F}}{\mathbf{W}})^{+}{\mathbf{W}^{\prime}}\mathbf{M}_{\mathbf{F}}\widehat{\mathbf{Z}}^{0}\boldsymbol{\delta}^{0}

(A.27)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle({\mathbf{W}^{\prime}}\mathbf{M}_{\mathbf{F}}{\mathbf{W}})^{+}{\mathbf{W}^{\prime}}\mathbf{M}_{\mathbf{F}}\overline{\mathbf{E}}_{r}^{0}\boldsymbol{\alpha}^{0}+T^{\frac{1}{4}+\frac{\tau}{2}}\boldsymbol{\theta}=T^{\frac{1}{4}+\frac{\tau}{2}}\boldsymbol{\theta}+O_{p}(N^{-\frac{1}{2}}T^{-\frac{1}{2}-\frac{\tau}{2}})

Combining the results in (
A.20
) and (
A.27
) yields

\displaystyle\mathrm{\mathbf{II}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle T^{\frac{1}{4}+\frac{\tau}{2}}\bigl[({\mathbf{W}^{\prime}}\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{\mathbf{F}}^{0}}{\mathbf{W}})^{+}{\mathbf{W}^{\prime}}\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{\mathbf{F}}^{0}}-({\mathbf{W}^{\prime}}\mathbf{M}_{\mathbf{F}}{\mathbf{W}})^{+}{\mathbf{W}^{\prime}}\mathbf{M}_{\mathbf{F}}\bigr]\widehat{\mathbf{Z}}^{0}\boldsymbol{\delta}^{0}

(A.28)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle T^{\frac{1}{4}+\frac{\tau}{2}}\boldsymbol{\theta}-\bigl[T^{\frac{1}{4}+\frac{\tau}{2}}\boldsymbol{\theta}+O_{p}(N^{-\frac{1}{2}}T^{-\frac{1}{2}-\frac{\tau}{2}})\bigr]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle O_{p}(N^{-\frac{1}{2}}T^{-\frac{1}{2}-\frac{\tau}{2}}).

We now continue onto
\mathrm{\mathbf{I}II}
and
\mathrm{\mathbf{I}V}
. It is useful to recall that
\widehat{\mathbf{F}}^{0}=\mathbf{F}^{0}+\overline{\mathbf{E}}^{0}
is correlated with both
\overline{\mathbf{E}}_{r}^{0}
and
\mathbf{W}
so that
\big\|T^{-1-\tau}{\mathbf{W}^{\prime}}\widehat{\mathbf{F}}^{0}\big\|=\big\|T^{-1-\tau}{{\widehat{\mathbf{F}}^{0\prime}}}{\mathbf{W}}\big\|=O_{p}(1)
. Similarly, define
\mathbf{S}
to be

\displaystyle\mathbf{S}=\begin{bmatrix}\mathbf{F}^{\prime}{\mathbf{F}}&\mathbf{0}_{r\times(m-r)}\\
\mathbf{0}_{(m-r)\times r}&\overline{\mathbf{E}}_{-r}^{0\prime}\overline{\mathbf{E}}_{-r}^{0}.\end{bmatrix}

(A.29)

Note that
T^{-1-\tau}\mathbf{F}^{\prime}{\mathbf{F}}
is positive definite. Further,
T^{-1}\overline{\mathbf{E}}_{-r}^{0\prime}\overline{\mathbf{E}}_{-r}^{0}
is positive definite wp1, as well
(
Stauskas and Westerlund, 2022
)
. Similarly to
Karabiyik et al. (2017)
, define
\mathbf{G}=\mathrm{diag}(T^{\frac{1}{2}+\frac{\tau}{2}}\mathbf{I}_{r},T^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathbf{I}_{m-r})
and consider the decomposition

\displaystyle\mathbf{G}^{-1}\widehat{\mathbf{F}}^{0\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{F}}^{0}\mathbf{G}^{-1}=\mathbf{G}^{-1}\mathbf{F}^{0\prime}\mathbf{F}^{0}\mathbf{G}^{-1}+\mathbf{G}^{-1}\mathbf{F}^{0\prime}\overline{\mathbf{E}}^{0}\mathbf{G}^{-1}+\mathbf{G}^{-1}\overline{\mathbf{E}}^{0\prime}\mathbf{F}^{0}\mathbf{G}^{-1}+\mathbf{G}^{-1}\overline{\mathbf{E}}^{0\prime}\overline{\mathbf{E}}^{0}\mathbf{G}^{-1}.

(A.30)

In detail,

\displaystyle\mathbf{G}^{-1}\mathbf{F}^{0\prime}\mathbf{F}^{0}\mathbf{G}^{-1}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\begin{bmatrix}T^{-1-\tau}\mathbf{F}^{\prime}{\mathbf{F}}&\mathbf{0}_{r\times(m-r)}\\
\mathbf{0}_{(m-r)\times r}&\mathbf{0}_{(m-r)\times(m-r)}\end{bmatrix},

(A.31)

\displaystyle\mathbf{G}^{-1}\mathbf{F}^{0\prime}\overline{\mathbf{E}}^{0}\mathbf{G}^{-1}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\begin{bmatrix}T^{-1-\tau}\mathbf{F}^{\prime}\overline{\mathbf{E}}_{r}\boldsymbol{\overline{\Lambda}}_{r}^{-1}&N^{\frac{1}{2}}T^{-1-\frac{\tau}{2}}(\mathbf{F}^{\prime}\overline{\mathbf{E}}_{-r}-\mathbf{F}^{\prime}\overline{\mathbf{E}}_{r}\boldsymbol{\overline{\Lambda}}_{r}^{-1}\boldsymbol{\overline{\Lambda}}_{-r})\\
\mathbf{0}_{(m-r)\times r}&\mathbf{0}_{(m-r)\times(m-r)}\end{bmatrix},

(A.32)

\displaystyle\mathbf{G}^{-1}\overline{\mathbf{E}}^{0\prime}\mathbf{F}^{0}\mathbf{G}^{-1}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\begin{bmatrix}T^{-1-\tau}(\boldsymbol{\overline{\Lambda}}_{r}^{-1})^{\prime}\overline{\mathbf{E}}_{r}^{\prime}\mathbf{F}&\mathbf{0}_{(m-r)\times r}\\
N^{\frac{1}{2}}T^{-1-\frac{\tau}{2}}(\overline{\mathbf{E}}_{-r}^{\prime}\mathbf{F}-\boldsymbol{\overline{\Lambda}_{-r}^{\prime}}(\boldsymbol{\overline{\Lambda}}_{r}^{-1})^{\prime}\overline{\mathbf{E}}_{r}^{\prime}\mathbf{F})&\mathbf{0}_{(m-r)\times(m-r)}\end{bmatrix},

(A.33)

and

\displaystyle\mathbf{G}^{-1}\overline{\mathbf{E}}^{0\prime}\overline{\mathbf{E}}^{0}\mathbf{G}^{-1}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\begin{bmatrix}T^{-1-\tau}\overline{\mathbf{E}}_{r}^{0\prime}\overline{\mathbf{E}}_{r}^{0}&T^{-1-\frac{\tau}{2}}\overline{\mathbf{E}}_{r}^{0\prime}\overline{\mathbf{E}}_{-r}^{0}\\
T^{-1-\frac{\tau}{2}}\overline{\mathbf{E}}_{-r}^{0\prime}\overline{\mathbf{E}}_{r}^{0}&T^{-1}\overline{\mathbf{E}}_{-r}^{0\prime}\overline{\mathbf{E}}_{-r}^{0}\end{bmatrix}

(A.34)

where

\displaystyle\overline{\mathbf{E}}_{r}^{0\prime}\overline{\mathbf{E}}_{r}^{0}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle(\boldsymbol{\overline{\Lambda}}_{r}^{-1})^{\prime}\overline{\mathbf{E}}_{r}^{\prime}\overline{\mathbf{E}}_{r}(\boldsymbol{\overline{\Lambda}}_{r}^{-1})

\displaystyle\overline{\mathbf{E}}_{r}^{0\prime}\overline{\mathbf{E}}_{-r}^{0}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle N^{\frac{1}{2}}((\boldsymbol{\overline{\Lambda}}_{r}^{-1})^{\prime}\overline{\mathbf{E}}_{r}^{\prime}\overline{\mathbf{E}}_{-r}-(\boldsymbol{\overline{\Lambda}}_{r}^{-1})^{\prime}\overline{\mathbf{E}}_{r}^{\prime}\overline{\mathbf{E}}_{r}\boldsymbol{\overline{\Lambda}}_{r}^{-1}\boldsymbol{\overline{\Lambda}}_{-r})

\displaystyle\overline{\mathbf{E}}_{-r}^{0\prime}\overline{\mathbf{E}}_{r}^{0}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle N^{\frac{1}{2}}(\overline{\mathbf{E}}_{-r}^{\prime}\overline{\mathbf{E}}_{r}\boldsymbol{\overline{\Lambda}}_{r}^{-1}-\boldsymbol{\overline{\Lambda}_{-r}^{\prime}}(\boldsymbol{\overline{\Lambda}}_{r}^{-1})^{\prime}\overline{\mathbf{E}}_{r}^{\prime}\overline{\mathbf{E}}_{r}\boldsymbol{\overline{\Lambda}}_{r}^{-1})

\displaystyle\overline{\mathbf{E}}_{-r}^{0\prime}\overline{\mathbf{E}}_{-r}^{0}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle N(\overline{\mathbf{E}}_{-r}^{\prime}\overline{\mathbf{E}}_{-r}-\overline{\mathbf{E}}_{-r}^{\prime}\overline{\mathbf{E}}_{r}\boldsymbol{\overline{\Lambda}}_{r}^{-1}\boldsymbol{\overline{\Lambda}}_{-r}-\boldsymbol{\overline{\Lambda}_{-r}^{\prime}}(\boldsymbol{\overline{\Lambda}}_{r}^{-1})^{\prime}\overline{\mathbf{E}}_{r}^{\prime}\overline{\mathbf{E}}_{-r}+\boldsymbol{\overline{\Lambda}_{-r}^{\prime}}(\boldsymbol{\overline{\Lambda}}_{r}^{-1})^{\prime}\overline{\mathbf{E}}_{r}^{\prime}\overline{\mathbf{E}}_{r}\boldsymbol{\overline{\Lambda}}_{r}^{-1}\boldsymbol{\overline{\Lambda}}_{-r})

Now notice that
\big\|T^{-1-\frac{\tau}{2}}\mathbf{F}^{\prime}\overline{\mathbf{E}}\big\|
is of order
O_{p}(N^{-\frac{1}{2}}T^{-\frac{1}{2}})
, which implies that
\big\|\mathbf{G}^{-1}\mathbf{F}^{0\prime}\overline{\mathbf{E}}^{0}\mathbf{G}^{-1}\big\|
and
\big\|\mathbf{G}^{-1}\overline{\mathbf{E}}^{0\prime}\mathbf{F}^{0}\mathbf{G}^{-1}\big\|
are both of order
O_{p}(T^{-\frac{1}{2}})
by symmetry arguments by Corollary A.1. However, by Lemma A.1
\big\|\mathbf{G}^{-1}\overline{\mathbf{E}}^{0\prime}\mathbf{F}^{0}\mathbf{G}^{-1}\big\|=O_{p}(1)
if the idiosyncratics are serially correlated. Finally, it remains to address the terms in
T^{-1}\overline{\mathbf{E}}^{0\prime}\overline{\mathbf{E}}^{0}
:

\displaystyle\big\|T^{-1-\tau}(\boldsymbol{\overline{\Lambda}}_{r}^{-1})^{\prime}\overline{\mathbf{E}}_{r}^{\prime}\overline{\mathbf{E}}_{r}(\boldsymbol{\overline{\Lambda}}_{r}^{-1})\big\|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\big\|\boldsymbol{\overline{\Lambda}}_{r}^{-1}\big\|^{2}\big\|T^{-1-\tau}\overline{\mathbf{E}}_{r}^{\prime}\overline{\mathbf{E}}_{r}\big\|

(A.35)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle O_{p}(N^{-1}T^{-\tau}),

by virtue of the fact that
\big\|T^{-1}\overline{\mathbf{E}}^{\prime}\overline{\mathbf{E}}\big\|
is
O_{p}(N^{-1})
while
\big\|\boldsymbol{\overline{\Lambda}}_{r}\big\|
and
\big\|\boldsymbol{\overline{\Lambda}}_{r}^{-1}\big\|
are
O_{p}(1)
. In turn, (
A.35
) implies

\displaystyle\big\|N^{\frac{1}{2}}T^{-1-\frac{\tau}{2}}((\boldsymbol{\overline{\Lambda}}_{r}^{-1})^{\prime}\overline{\mathbf{E}}_{r}^{\prime}\overline{\mathbf{E}}_{-r}-(\boldsymbol{\overline{\Lambda}}_{r}^{-1})^{\prime}\overline{\mathbf{E}}_{r}^{\prime}\overline{\mathbf{E}}_{r}\boldsymbol{\overline{\Lambda}}_{r}^{-1}\boldsymbol{\overline{\Lambda}}_{-r})\big\|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle N^{\frac{1}{2}}\big\|\boldsymbol{\overline{\Lambda}}_{r}^{-1}\big\|\big\|T^{-1-\frac{\tau}{2}}\overline{\mathbf{E}}_{r}^{\prime}\overline{\mathbf{E}}_{-r}\big\|

(A.36)

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle N^{\frac{1}{2}}\big\|\boldsymbol{\overline{\Lambda}}_{r}^{-1}\big\|^{2}\big\|T^{-1-\frac{\tau}{2}}\overline{\mathbf{E}}_{r}^{\prime}\overline{\mathbf{E}}_{r}\big\|\big\|\boldsymbol{\overline{\Lambda}}_{-r}\big\|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle O_{p}(N^{-\frac{1}{2}}T^{-\frac{\tau}{2}})

By symmetry, it also follows that
\big\|N^{\frac{1}{2}}T^{-1-\frac{\tau}{2}}(\overline{\mathbf{E}}_{-r}^{\prime}\overline{\mathbf{E}}_{r}\boldsymbol{\overline{\Lambda}}_{r}^{-1}-\boldsymbol{\overline{\Lambda}_{-r}^{\prime}}(\boldsymbol{\overline{\Lambda}}_{r}^{-1})^{\prime}\overline{\mathbf{E}}_{r}^{\prime}\overline{\mathbf{E}}_{r}\boldsymbol{\overline{\Lambda}}_{r}^{-1})\big\|=O_{p}(N^{-\frac{1}{2}}T^{-\frac{\tau}{2}})
. Moreover,

\displaystyle\big\|NT^{-1}(\overline{\mathbf{E}}_{-r}^{\prime}\overline{\mathbf{E}}_{-r}-\overline{\mathbf{E}}_{-r}^{\prime}\overline{\mathbf{E}}_{r}\boldsymbol{\overline{\Lambda}}_{r}^{-1}\boldsymbol{\overline{\Lambda}}_{-r}-\boldsymbol{\overline{\Lambda}_{-r}^{\prime}}(\boldsymbol{\overline{\Lambda}}_{r}^{-1})^{\prime}\overline{\mathbf{E}}_{r}^{\prime}\overline{\mathbf{E}}_{-r}+\boldsymbol{\overline{\Lambda}_{-r}^{\prime}}(\boldsymbol{\overline{\Lambda}}_{r}^{-1})^{\prime}\overline{\mathbf{E}}_{r}^{\prime}\overline{\mathbf{E}}_{r}\boldsymbol{\overline{\Lambda}}_{r}^{-1}\boldsymbol{\overline{\Lambda}}_{-r})\big\|

\displaystyle\leq N\big\|T^{-1}\overline{\mathbf{E}}_{-r}^{\prime}\overline{\mathbf{E}}_{-r}\big\|+N\big\|T^{-1}\overline{\mathbf{E}}_{-r}^{\prime}\overline{\mathbf{E}}_{r}\big\|\big\|\boldsymbol{\overline{\Lambda}}_{r}^{-1}\big\|\big\|\boldsymbol{\overline{\Lambda}}_{-r}\big\|+N\big\|\boldsymbol{\overline{\Lambda}}_{r}^{-1}\big\|\big\|\boldsymbol{\overline{\Lambda}}_{-r}\big\|\big\|T^{-1}\overline{\mathbf{E}}_{r}^{\prime}\overline{\mathbf{E}}_{-r}\big\|

\displaystyle+N\big\|\boldsymbol{\overline{\Lambda}}_{r}^{-1}\big\|^{2}\big\|\boldsymbol{\overline{\Lambda}}_{-r}\big\|^{2}\big\|T^{-1}\overline{\mathbf{E}}_{-r}^{\prime}\overline{\mathbf{E}}_{-r}\big\|

\displaystyle=O_{p}(1).

(A.37)

Thus, the probability limit of
\mathbf{G}^{-1}\widehat{\mathbf{F}}^{0\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{F}}^{0}\mathbf{G}^{-1}
is then derived by combining the above results:

\displaystyle\big\|\mathbf{G}^{-1}\widehat{\mathbf{F}}^{0\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{F}}^{0}\mathbf{G}^{-1}-\mathbf{G}^{-1}\mathbf{S}\mathbf{G}^{-1}\big\|=O_{p}(N^{-\frac{1}{2}}T^{-\frac{\tau}{2}})+O_{p}(T^{-\frac{1}{2}})

(A.38)

That is, we obtain

\displaystyle\mathbf{G}^{-1}\widehat{\mathbf{F}}^{0\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{F}}^{0}\mathbf{G}^{-1}=\mathbf{G}^{-1}\mathbf{S}\mathbf{G}^{-1}+O_{p}(N^{-\frac{1}{2}}T^{-\frac{\tau}{2}})+O_{p}(T^{-\frac{1}{2}}),

(A.39)

Since
T^{-1-\tau}\mathbf{F}^{\prime}{\mathbf{F}}
and
T^{-1}\overline{\mathbf{E}}_{-r}^{0\prime}\overline{\mathbf{E}}_{-r}^{0}
are both positive definite wp1, then so is
\boldsymbol{\Sigma}_{\mathbf{F}^{0}}
. Further, the rank of
\mathbf{G}^{-1}\mathbf{S}\mathbf{G}^{-1}
is
m
, which is also the rank of
\mathbf{G}^{-1}\widehat{\mathbf{F}}^{0\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{F}}^{0}\mathbf{G}^{-1}
. It then follows that as
N,T\to\infty
,

\displaystyle rk[\mathbf{G}^{-1}\widehat{\mathbf{F}}^{0\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{F}}^{0}\mathbf{G}^{-1}]\to rk\mathbf{G}^{-1}\mathbf{S}\mathbf{G}^{-1}

(A.40)

almost surely. Following
Karabiyik et al. (2017)
, (
A.39
) and (
A.40
) lead to the key result

\displaystyle\big\|(\mathbf{G}^{-1}\widehat{\mathbf{F}}^{0\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{F}}^{0}\mathbf{G}^{-1})^{+}-(\mathbf{G}^{-1}\mathbf{S}\mathbf{G}^{-1})^{+}\big\|=O_{p}(N^{-\frac{1}{2}}T^{-\frac{\tau}{2}})+O_{p}(T^{-\frac{1}{2}}).

(A.41)

An important implication of this result is that

\displaystyle\big\|(\mathbf{G}^{-1}\widehat{\mathbf{F}}^{0\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{F}}^{0}\mathbf{G}^{-1})^{+}\big\|=O_{p}(1).

(A.42)

Further, note that
\left\|\mathbf{G}^{-1}\right\|\leq MT^{-1/2}
for some positive
M
and

\displaystyle\left\|T^{-\frac{1}{2}-\frac{\tau}{2}}\mathbf{W}^{\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{F}}^{0}\mathbf{G}^{-1}\right\|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left\|T^{-\frac{1}{2}-\frac{\tau}{2}}\mathbf{W}^{\prime}\mathbf{F}^{0}\mathbf{G}^{-1}\right\|+\left\|T^{-\frac{1}{2}-\frac{\tau}{2}}\mathbf{W}^{\prime}\overline{\mathbf{E}}^{0}\mathbf{G}^{-1}\right\|

(A.43)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left\|T^{-\frac{1}{2}-\frac{\tau}{2}}\mathbf{W}^{\prime}\mathbf{F}^{0}\mathbf{G}^{-1}\right\|+\left\|T^{-\frac{1}{2}-\frac{\tau}{2}}\mathbf{W}^{\prime}\overline{\mathbf{E}}^{0}\right\|\left\|\mathbf{G}^{-1}\right\|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left\|T^{-1-\tau}\mathbf{W}^{\prime}\mathbf{F}\right\|+MT^{-\frac{1}{2}}\left\|T^{-\frac{1}{2}-\frac{\tau}{2}}\mathbf{W}^{\prime}\overline{\mathbf{E}}^{0}\right\|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle O_{p}(1)+O_{p}(T^{-\frac{1}{2}})=O_{p}(1).

In turn, it follows by using (A.77) in the Supplement of
Stauskas and Westerlund (2022)
and (
A.43
) that

\displaystyle\big\|T^{-1-\tau}{\mathbf{W}^{\prime}}\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{\mathbf{F}}^{0}}{\mathbf{W}}-t^{-1-\tau}{\mathbf{W}^{\prime}}\mathbf{M}_{\mathbf{F}^{0}}{\mathbf{W}}\big\|

\displaystyle=\big\|T^{-1-\tau}{\mathbf{W}^{\prime}}(\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{\mathbf{F}}^{0}}-\mathbf{M}_{\mathbf{F}^{0}}){\mathbf{W}}\big\|

\displaystyle\leq\big\|T^{-1-\frac{\tau}{2}}\mathbf{W}^{\prime}\overline{\mathbf{E}}_{-r}^{0}(T^{-1}\overline{\mathbf{E}}_{-r}^{0\prime}\overline{\mathbf{E}}_{-r}^{0})^{+}T^{-1-\frac{\tau}{2}}\overline{\mathbf{E}}_{-r}^{0\prime}\mathbf{W}\big\|+\big\|T^{-1-\tau}\mathbf{W}^{\prime}\overline{\mathbf{E}}_{r}^{0}(T^{-1-\tau}\mathbf{F}^{\prime}\mathbf{F})^{+}T^{-1-\tau}\overline{\mathbf{E}}_{r}^{0\prime}\mathbf{W}\big\|

\displaystyle+\big\|T^{-1-\tau}\mathbf{W}^{\prime}\overline{\mathbf{E}}_{r}^{0}(T^{-1-\tau}\mathbf{F}^{\prime}\mathbf{F})^{+}T^{-1-\tau}\mathbf{F}^{\prime}\mathbf{W}\big\|+\big\|T^{-1-\tau}\mathbf{W}^{\prime}\mathbf{F}(T^{-1-\tau}\mathbf{F}^{\prime}\mathbf{F})^{+}T^{-1-\tau}\overline{\mathbf{E}}_{r}^{0\prime}\mathbf{W}\big\|

\displaystyle+\big\|T^{-\frac{1}{2}-\frac{\tau}{2}}\mathbf{W}^{\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{F}}^{0}\mathbf{G}^{-1}[(\mathbf{G}^{-1}\widehat{\mathbf{F}}^{0\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{F}}^{0}\mathbf{G}^{-1})^{+}-(\mathbf{G}^{-1}\mathbf{S}\mathbf{G}^{-1})^{+}]\mathbf{G}^{-1}T^{-\frac{1}{2}-\frac{\tau}{2}}\widehat{\mathbf{F}}^{0\prime}\mathbf{W}\big\|

\displaystyle=\big\|T^{-1-\frac{\tau}{2}}\mathbf{W}^{\prime}\overline{\mathbf{E}}_{-r}^{0}\big\|\big\|(T^{-1}\overline{\mathbf{E}}_{-r}^{0\prime}\overline{\mathbf{E}}_{-r}^{0})^{+}\big\|\big\|T^{-1-\frac{\tau}{2}}\overline{\mathbf{E}}_{-r}^{0\prime}\mathbf{W}\big\|+\big\|T^{-1-\tau}\mathbf{W}^{\prime}\overline{\mathbf{E}}_{r}^{0}\big\|\big\|(T^{-1-\tau}\mathbf{F}^{\prime}\mathbf{F})^{+}\big\|\big\|T^{-1-\tau}\overline{\mathbf{E}}_{r}^{0\prime}\mathbf{W}\big\|

\displaystyle+\big\|T^{-1-\tau}\mathbf{W}^{\prime}\overline{\mathbf{E}}_{r}^{0}\big\|\big\|(T^{-1-\tau}\mathbf{F}^{\prime}\mathbf{F})^{+}\big\|\big\|T^{-1-\tau}\mathbf{F}^{\prime}\mathbf{W}\big\|+\big\|T^{-1-\tau}\mathbf{W}^{\prime}\mathbf{F}\big\|\big\|(T^{-1-\tau}\mathbf{F}^{\prime}\mathbf{F})^{+}\big\|\big\|T^{-1-\tau}\overline{\mathbf{E}}_{r}^{0\prime}\mathbf{W}\big\|

\displaystyle+\big\|T^{-\frac{1}{2}-\frac{\tau}{2}}\mathbf{W}^{\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{F}}^{0}\mathbf{G}^{-1}\big\|\big\|(\mathbf{G}^{-1}\widehat{\mathbf{F}}^{0\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{F}}^{0}\mathbf{G}^{-1})^{+}-(\mathbf{G}^{-1}\mathbf{S}\mathbf{G}^{-1})^{+}\big\|\big\|\mathbf{G}^{-1}T^{-\frac{1}{2}-\frac{\tau}{2}}\widehat{\mathbf{F}}^{0\prime}\mathbf{W}\big\|

\displaystyle=O_{p}(N^{-\frac{1}{2}}T^{-\frac{\tau}{2}})+O_{p}(T^{-\frac{1}{2}}),

(A.44)

due to the independence of
\mathbf{W}
and
\overline{\mathbf{E}}^{0}
together with (
A.41
). Since
T^{-1-\tau}{\mathbf{W}^{\prime}}\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{\mathbf{F}}^{0}}{\mathbf{W}}
and
T^{-1-\tau}{\mathbf{W}^{\prime}}\mathbf{M}_{\mathbf{F}^{0}}{\mathbf{W}}
are both positive definite wp1, we also have that

\displaystyle\big\|(T^{-1-\tau}{\mathbf{W}^{\prime}}\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{\mathbf{F}}^{0}}{\mathbf{W}})^{+}-(T^{-1-\tau}{\mathbf{W}^{\prime}}\mathbf{M}_{\mathbf{F}^{0}}{\mathbf{W}})^{+}\big\|=O_{p}(N^{-\frac{1}{2}}T^{-\frac{\tau}{2}})+O_{p}(T^{-\frac{1}{2}})

(A.45)

so that

\displaystyle\big\|(T^{-1-\tau}{\mathbf{W}^{\prime}}\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{\mathbf{F}}^{0}}{\mathbf{W}})^{+}\big\|=O_{p}(1).

(A.46)

A second important implication of (
A.41
) is that

\displaystyle\big\|T^{-1-\tau}\mathbf{W}^{\prime}\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{\mathbf{F}}^{0}}\overline{\mathbf{E}}_{r}^{0}\big\|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\big\|T^{-1-\tau}\mathbf{W}^{\prime}\overline{\mathbf{E}}_{r}^{0}\big\|+\big\|T^{-\frac{1}{2}-\frac{\tau}{2}}\mathbf{W}^{\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{F}}^{0}\mathbf{G}^{-1}\big\|\big\|(\mathbf{G}^{-1}\widehat{\mathbf{F}}^{0\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{F}}^{0}\mathbf{G}^{-1})^{+}\big\|\big\|\mathbf{G}^{-1}T^{-\frac{1}{2}-\frac{\tau}{2}}\widehat{\mathbf{F}}^{0\prime}\overline{\mathbf{E}}_{r}^{0}\big\|

(A.47)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\big\|T^{-1-\tau}\mathbf{W}^{\prime}\overline{\mathbf{E}}_{r}^{0}\big\|+\big\|T^{-\frac{1}{2}-\frac{\tau}{2}}\mathbf{W}^{\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{F}}^{0}\mathbf{G}^{-1}\big\|\big\|(\mathbf{G}^{-1}\widehat{\mathbf{F}}^{0\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{F}}^{0}\mathbf{G}^{-1})^{+}\big\|\big\|\mathbf{G}^{-1}T^{-\frac{1}{2}-\frac{\tau}{2}}\mathbf{F}^{0\prime}\overline{\mathbf{E}}_{r}^{0}\big\|

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\big\|T^{-\frac{1}{2}-\frac{\tau}{2}}\mathbf{W}^{\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{F}}^{0}\mathbf{G}^{-1}\big\|\big\|(\mathbf{G}^{-1}\widehat{\mathbf{F}}^{0\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{F}}^{0}\mathbf{G}^{-1})^{+}\big\|\big\|\mathbf{G}^{-1}\big\|\big\|T^{-\frac{1}{2}-\frac{\tau}{2}}\overline{\mathbf{E}}^{0\prime}\overline{\mathbf{E}}_{r}^{0}\big\|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\big\|T^{-1-\tau}\mathbf{W}^{\prime}\overline{\mathbf{E}}_{r}^{0}\big\|+\big\|T^{-\frac{1}{2}-\frac{\tau}{2}}\mathbf{W}^{\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{F}}^{0}\mathbf{G}^{-1}\big\|\big\|(\mathbf{G}^{-1}\widehat{\mathbf{F}}^{0\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{F}}^{0}\mathbf{G}^{-1})^{+}\big\|\big\|{\mathbf{G}^{-1}}T^{-\frac{1}{2}-\frac{\tau}{2}}\mathbf{F}^{0\prime}\overline{\mathbf{E}}_{r}^{0}\big\|

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle M{T^{-\frac{\tau}{2}}}\big\|T^{-\frac{1}{2}-\frac{\tau}{2}}\mathbf{W}^{\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{F}}^{0}\mathbf{G}^{-1}\big\|\big\|(\mathbf{G}^{-1}\widehat{\mathbf{F}}^{0\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{F}}^{0}\mathbf{G}^{-1})^{+}\big\|\big\|{T^{-1}}\overline{\mathbf{E}}^{0\prime}\overline{\mathbf{E}}_{r}^{0}\big\|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle O_{p}(N^{-\frac{1}{2}}T^{-\frac{1}{2}-\frac{\tau}{2}})+O_{p}(N^{-\frac{1}{2}}T^{-\frac{1}{2}-\frac{\tau}{2}})+O_{p}(N^{-\frac{1}{2}}T^{-\frac{\tau}{2}})=O_{p}(N^{-\frac{1}{2}}T^{-\frac{\tau}{2}})

similarly to (
A.26
). As a result of (
A.25
), (
A.26
), (
A.46
) and (
A.47
), we have

\displaystyle\big\|({\mathbf{W}^{\prime}}\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{\mathbf{F}}^{0}}{\mathbf{W}})^{+}{\mathbf{W}^{\prime}}\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{\mathbf{F}}^{0}}\overline{\mathbf{E}}_{r}^{0}\big\|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\big\|(T^{-1-\tau}{\mathbf{W}^{\prime}}\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{\mathbf{F}}^{0}}{\mathbf{W}})^{+}\big\|\big\|T^{-1-\tau}{\mathbf{W}^{\prime}}\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{\mathbf{F}}^{0}}\overline{\mathbf{E}}_{r}^{0}\big\|

(A.48)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle O_{p}(N^{-\frac{1}{2}}T^{-\frac{\tau}{2}})

and

\displaystyle\big\|({\mathbf{W}^{\prime}}\mathbf{M}_{\mathbf{F}^{0}}{\mathbf{W}})^{+}{\mathbf{W}^{\prime}}\mathbf{M}_{\mathbf{F}^{0}}\overline{\mathbf{E}}_{r}^{0}\big\|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\big\|(T^{-1-\tau}{\mathbf{W}^{\prime}}\mathbf{M}_{\mathbf{F}^{0}}{\mathbf{W}})^{+}\big\|\big\|T^{-1-\tau}{\mathbf{W}^{\prime}}\mathbf{M}_{\mathbf{F}^{0}}\overline{\mathbf{E}}_{r}^{0}\big\|

(A.49)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle O_{p}(N^{-\frac{1}{2}}T^{-\frac{1}{2}-\frac{\tau}{2}}).

Using (
A.48
) and (
A.49
), we obtain

\displaystyle\big\|\mathrm{\mathbf{IV}}\big\|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle T^{\frac{1}{4}+\frac{\tau}{2}}\big\|\bigl[({\mathbf{W}^{\prime}}\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{\mathbf{F}}^{0}}{\mathbf{W}})^{+}{\mathbf{W}^{\prime}}\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{\mathbf{F}}^{0}}-({\mathbf{W}^{\prime}}\mathbf{M}_{\mathbf{F}}{\mathbf{W}})^{+}{\mathbf{W}^{\prime}}\mathbf{M}_{\mathbf{F}}\bigr]\overline{\mathbf{E}}_{r}^{0}\boldsymbol{\alpha}\big\|

(A.50)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\big\|({\mathbf{W}^{\prime}}\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{\mathbf{F}}^{0}}{\mathbf{W}})^{+}{\mathbf{W}^{\prime}}\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{\mathbf{F}}^{0}}\overline{\mathbf{E}}_{r}^{0}\big\|\big\|\boldsymbol{\alpha}^{0}\big\|+\big\|({\mathbf{W}^{\prime}}\mathbf{M}_{\mathbf{F}}{\mathbf{W}})^{+}{\mathbf{W}^{\prime}}\mathbf{M}_{\mathbf{F}}\overline{\mathbf{E}}_{r}^{0}\big\|\big\|\boldsymbol{\alpha}^{0}\big\|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle O_{p}(N^{-\frac{1}{2}}T^{-\frac{\tau}{2}}).

For
\mathrm{\mathbf{III}}
, recall from (
A.18
) that
\big\|(T^{-1-\tau}{\mathbf{F}^{\prime}}\mathbf{M}_{\mathbf{W}}{\mathbf{F}})^{+}T^{-1-\tau}{\mathbf{F}^{\prime}}\mathbf{M}_{\mathbf{W}}\overline{\mathbf{E}}_{r}^{0}\boldsymbol{\alpha}\big\|=O_{p}(N^{-\frac{1}{2}}T^{-\frac{1}{2}-\frac{\tau}{2}})
. Further, we use the fact that
\big\|\mathbf{D}_{\mathbf{F}}\mathbf{G}^{-1}(\mathbf{G}^{-1}\widehat{\mathbf{F}}^{0\prime}\mathbf{M}_{\mathbf{W}}{\widehat{\mathbf{F}}^{0}}\mathbf{G}^{-1})^{+}\mathbf{G}^{-1}\widehat{\mathbf{F}}^{0\prime}\mathbf{M}_{\mathbf{W}}\overline{\mathbf{E}}_{r}^{0}\boldsymbol{\alpha}\big\|=O_{p}(N^{-\frac{1}{2}}T^{-\tau/2})
. Indeed, given that
\mathbf{D}_{\mathbf{F}}\mathbf{G}^{-1}=T^{-\frac{1}{4}}\mathbf{I}_{m}
, we have

\displaystyle\big\|T^{-\frac{1}{4}}\mathbf{G}^{-1}\widehat{\mathbf{F}}^{0\prime}\mathbf{M}_{\mathbf{W}}\overline{\mathbf{E}}_{r}^{0}\boldsymbol{\alpha}\big\|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\big\|T^{-\frac{1}{2}-\frac{\tau}{2}}\mathbf{G}^{-1}\widehat{\mathbf{F}}^{0\prime}\mathbf{M}_{\mathbf{W}}\overline{\mathbf{E}}_{r}^{0}\boldsymbol{\alpha}^{0}\big\|

(A.51)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\big\|T^{-\frac{1}{2}-\frac{\tau}{2}}\mathbf{G}^{-1}\widehat{\mathbf{F}}^{0\prime}\overline{\mathbf{E}}_{r}^{0}\boldsymbol{\alpha}^{0}\big\|+\big\|T^{-1-\tau}\widehat{\mathbf{F}}^{0\prime}\mathbf{W}\big\|\big\|(T^{-1-\tau}\mathbf{W}^{\prime}\mathbf{W})^{+}\big\|\big\|T^{-\frac{1}{2}-\frac{\tau}{2}}\mathbf{G}^{-1}\mathbf{W}^{\prime}\overline{\mathbf{E}}_{r}^{0}\boldsymbol{\alpha}^{0}\big\|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\big\|T^{-(1+\tau)}\mathbf{F}^{\prime}\overline{\mathbf{E}}_{r}^{0}\boldsymbol{\alpha}^{0}\big\|+\big\|T^{-\frac{1}{2}-\frac{\tau}{2}}\mathbf{G}^{-1}\overline{\mathbf{E}}^{\prime}\overline{\mathbf{E}}_{r}^{0}\boldsymbol{\alpha}^{0}\big\|

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\big\|T^{-1-\tau}\widehat{\mathbf{F}}^{0\prime}\mathbf{W}\big\|\big\|(T^{-1-\tau}\mathbf{W}^{\prime}\mathbf{W})^{+}\big\|\big\|T^{-\frac{1}{2}-\frac{\tau}{2}}\mathbf{G}^{-1}\mathbf{W}^{\prime}\overline{\mathbf{E}}_{r}^{0}\boldsymbol{\alpha}^{0}\big\|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\big\|T^{-(1+\tau)}\mathbf{F}^{\prime}\overline{\mathbf{E}}_{r}^{0}\big\|\big\|\boldsymbol{\alpha}^{0}\big\|+MT^{-\frac{\tau}{2}}\big\|T^{-1}\overline{\mathbf{E}}^{\prime}\overline{\mathbf{E}}_{r}^{0}\big\|\big\|\boldsymbol{\alpha}^{0}\big\|

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\big\|T^{-1-\tau}\widehat{\mathbf{F}}^{0\prime}\mathbf{W}\big\|\big\|(T^{-1-\tau}\mathbf{W}^{\prime}\mathbf{W})^{+}\big\|\big\|T^{-\frac{1}{2}-\frac{\tau}{2}}\mathbf{G}^{-1}\mathbf{W}^{\prime}\overline{\mathbf{E}}_{r}^{0}\big\|\big\|\boldsymbol{\alpha}^{0}\big\|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle O_{p}(N^{-\frac{1}{2}}T^{-\frac{\tau}{2}})+O_{p}(N^{-1}T^{-\frac{\tau}{2}})+O_{p}(N^{-\frac{1}{2}}T^{-\frac{1}{2}-\frac{\tau}{2}})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle O_{p}(N^{-\frac{1}{2}}T^{-\frac{\tau}{2}}).

With respect to
(\mathbf{G}^{-1}\widehat{\mathbf{F}}^{0\prime}\mathbf{M}_{\mathbf{W}}{\widehat{\mathbf{F}}^{0}}\mathbf{G}^{-1})^{+}
, we proceed analogously to (
A.38
). Let
\mathbf{S}_{\mathbf{W}}
be defined as

\displaystyle\mathbf{S}_{\mathbf{M}_{\mathbf{W}}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\begin{bmatrix}\mathbf{F}^{\prime}\mathbf{M}_{\mathbf{W}}{\mathbf{F}}&\mathbf{0}_{r\times(m-r)}\\
\mathbf{0}_{(m-r)\times r}&\overline{\mathbf{E}}_{-r}^{0\prime}\mathbf{M}_{\mathbf{W}}\overline{\mathbf{E}}_{-r}^{0}\end{bmatrix}

(A.52)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\begin{bmatrix}\mathbf{F}^{\prime}{\mathbf{F}}&\mathbf{0}_{r\times(m-r)}\\
\mathbf{0}_{(m-r)\times r}&\overline{\mathbf{E}}_{-r}^{0\prime}\overline{\mathbf{E}}_{-r}^{0}\end{bmatrix}-\begin{bmatrix}\mathbf{F}^{\prime}\mathbf{W}(\mathbf{W}^{\prime}\mathbf{W})^{+}\mathbf{W}^{\prime}{\mathbf{F}}&\mathbf{0}_{r\times(m-r)}\\
\mathbf{0}_{(m-r)\times r}&\overline{\mathbf{E}}_{-r}^{0\prime}\mathbf{W}(\mathbf{W}^{\prime}\mathbf{W})^{+}\mathbf{W}^{\prime}\overline{\mathbf{E}}_{-r}^{0}\end{bmatrix}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathbf{S}-\mathbf{S}_{\mathbf{W}}.

and exploiting the definition of
\mathbf{M}_{\mathbf{W}}
yields the decomposition

\displaystyle\mathbf{G}^{-1}\widehat{\mathbf{F}}^{0\prime}\mathbf{M}_{\mathbf{W}}{\widehat{\mathbf{F}}^{0}}\mathbf{G}^{-1}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathbf{G}^{-1}\widehat{\mathbf{F}}^{0\prime}{\widehat{\mathbf{F}}^{0}}\mathbf{G}^{-1}-\mathbf{G}^{-1}\widehat{\mathbf{F}}^{0\prime}\mathbf{W}(\mathbf{W}^{\prime}\mathbf{W})^{+}\mathbf{W}^{\prime}{\widehat{\mathbf{F}}^{0}}\mathbf{G}^{-1}.

(A.53)

Thus,

\displaystyle\big\|\mathbf{G}^{-1}\widehat{\mathbf{F}}^{0\prime}\mathbf{M}_{\mathbf{W}}{\widehat{\mathbf{F}}^{0}}\mathbf{G}^{-1}-\mathbf{G}^{-1}\mathbf{S}_{\mathbf{M}_{\mathbf{W}}}\mathbf{G}^{-1}\big\|

(A.54)

\displaystyle\leq\big\|\mathbf{G}^{-1}\widehat{\mathbf{F}}^{0\prime}{\widehat{\mathbf{F}}^{0}}\mathbf{G}^{-1}-\mathbf{G}^{-1}\mathbf{S}\mathbf{G}^{-1}\big\|+\big\|\mathbf{G}^{-1}\widehat{\mathbf{F}}^{0\prime}\mathbf{W}(\mathbf{W}^{\prime}\mathbf{W})^{+}\mathbf{W}^{\prime}{\widehat{\mathbf{F}}^{0}}\mathbf{G}^{-1}-\mathbf{G}^{-1}\mathbf{S}_{\mathbf{W}}\mathbf{G}^{-1}\big\|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle O_{p}(N^{-\frac{1}{2}}T^{-\frac{\tau}{2}})+O_{p}(T^{-\frac{1}{2}}),

using the fact that
\big\|T^{-1-\tau}\mathbf{F}^{0\prime}\overline{\mathbf{E}}^{0}\big\|
and
\big\|T^{-1-\tau}\mathbf{W}^{\prime}\overline{\mathbf{E}}^{0}\big\|
are both of order
O_{p}(T^{-\frac{1}{2}-\frac{\tau}{2}})
, while
\big\|T^{-1}\overline{\mathbf{E}}^{0\prime}\overline{\mathbf{E}}^{0}\big\|
is of order
O_{p}(1)
, which ensures that the first term on the right-hand side of (
A.54
) drives the order (i.e. the second one does not dominate). Indeed the slowest decaying component of the second term is

\displaystyle\left\|T^{-1-\tau}\mathbf{F}^{\prime}\mathbf{W}(T^{-(1+\tau)}\mathbf{W}^{\prime}\mathbf{W})^{+}T^{-1-\tau/2}\mathbf{W}^{\prime}\overline{\mathbf{E}}_{-r}^{0}\right\|

(A.55)

\displaystyle\leq T^{-1/2}\left\|T^{-(1+\tau)}\mathbf{F}^{\prime}\mathbf{W}\right\|\left\|(T^{-(1+\tau)}\mathbf{W}^{\prime}\mathbf{W})^{+}\right\|\left\|T^{-(1+\tau)/2}\mathbf{W}^{\prime}\overline{\mathbf{E}}_{-r}^{0}\right\|

\displaystyle=O_{p}(T^{-1/2}).

(A.56)

Therefore, we obtain

\displaystyle\mathbf{G}^{-1}\widehat{\mathbf{F}}^{0\prime}\mathbf{M}_{\mathbf{W}}{\widehat{\mathbf{F}}^{0}}\mathbf{G}^{-1}=\mathbf{G}^{-1}\mathbf{S}_{\mathbf{M}_{\mathbf{W}}}\mathbf{G}^{-1}+O_{p}(N^{-\frac{1}{2}}T^{-\frac{\tau}{2}})+O_{p}(T^{-\frac{1}{2}}).

(A.57)

Since
\mathbf{G}^{-1}\mathbf{S}\mathbf{G}^{-1}
and
\mathbf{G}^{-1}\mathbf{S}_{\mathbf{W}}\mathbf{G}^{-1}
are positive definite wp1, then so are
\mathbf{G}^{-1}\mathbf{S}_{\mathbf{M}_{\mathbf{W}}}\mathbf{G}^{-1}
and
\mathbf{G}^{-1}\widehat{\mathbf{F}}^{0\prime}\mathbf{M}_{\mathbf{W}}{\widehat{\mathbf{F}}^{0}}\mathbf{G}^{-1}
. Further, the rank of
\mathbf{G}^{-1}\mathbf{S}_{\mathbf{M}_{\mathbf{W}}}\mathbf{G}^{-1}
is
m
, which is also the rank of
\mathbf{G}^{-1}\widehat{\mathbf{F}}^{0\prime}\mathbf{M}_{\mathbf{W}}{\widehat{\mathbf{F}}^{0}}\mathbf{G}^{-1}
. It then follows that as
N,T\to\infty
,

\displaystyle rk[\mathbf{G}^{-1}\widehat{\mathbf{F}}^{0\prime}\mathbf{M}_{\mathbf{W}}{\widehat{\mathbf{F}}^{0}}\mathbf{G}^{-1}]\to rk\mathbf{G}^{-1}\mathbf{S}_{\mathbf{M}_{\mathbf{W}}}\mathbf{G}^{-1}

(A.58)

almost surely. Following
Karabiyik et al. (2017)
, (
A.57
) and (
A.58
) lead to the key result

\displaystyle\big\|(\mathbf{G}^{-1}\widehat{\mathbf{F}}^{0\prime}\mathbf{M}_{\mathbf{W}}{\widehat{\mathbf{F}}^{0}}\mathbf{G}^{-1})^{+}-(\mathbf{G}^{-1}\mathbf{S}_{\mathbf{M}_{\mathbf{W}}}\mathbf{G}^{-1})^{+}\big\|=O_{p}(N^{-\frac{1}{2}}T^{-\frac{\tau}{2}})+O_{p}(T^{-\frac{1}{2}}).

(A.59)

An important implication of this result is that

\displaystyle\big\|(\mathbf{G}^{-1}\widehat{\mathbf{F}}^{0\prime}\mathbf{M}_{\mathbf{W}}{\widehat{\mathbf{F}}^{0}}\mathbf{G}^{-1})^{+}\big\|=O_{p}(1).

(A.60)

Combining (
A.60
) and (
A.57
) directly implies that

\displaystyle\big\|\mathbf{D}_{\mathbf{F}}(\widehat{\mathbf{F}}^{0\prime}\mathbf{M}_{\mathbf{W}}{\widehat{\mathbf{F}}^{0}})^{+}\widehat{\mathbf{F}}^{0\prime}\mathbf{M}_{\mathbf{W}}\overline{\mathbf{E}}_{r}^{0}\boldsymbol{\alpha}\big\|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\big\|(\mathbf{G}^{-1}\widehat{\mathbf{F}}^{0\prime}\mathbf{M}_{\mathbf{W}}{\widehat{\mathbf{F}}^{0}}\mathbf{G}^{-1})^{+}\big\|\big\|T^{-\frac{1}{4}}\mathbf{G}^{-1}\widehat{\mathbf{F}}^{0\prime}\mathbf{M}_{\mathbf{W}}\overline{\mathbf{E}}_{r}^{0}\boldsymbol{\alpha}\big\|

(A.61)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle O_{p}(N^{-\frac{1}{2}}T^{-\tau/2}),

as required. Finally, we are able to obtain the order
\mathrm{\mathbf{I}II}
by using the definition of
\boldsymbol{\alpha}
and
\mathbf{D}_{\mathbf{F}}\mathbf{G}^{-1}=\mathbf{I}_{m}T^{-1/4}
:

\displaystyle\big\|\mathrm{\mathbf{III}}\big\|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\bigg\|\mathbf{D}_{\mathbf{F}}\left((\widehat{\mathbf{F}}^{0\prime}\mathbf{M}_{\mathbf{W}}{\widehat{\mathbf{F}}^{0}})^{+}\widehat{\mathbf{F}}^{0\prime}\mathbf{M}_{\mathbf{W}}-\begin{bmatrix}({\mathbf{F}^{\prime}}\mathbf{M}_{\mathbf{W}}{\mathbf{F}})^{+}{\mathbf{F}^{\prime}}\mathbf{M}_{\mathbf{W}}\\
\mathbf{0}_{(m-r)\times(t-1)}\end{bmatrix}\right)\overline{\mathbf{E}}_{r}^{0}\boldsymbol{\alpha}\bigg\|

(A.62)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\bigg\|(\mathbf{G}^{-1}\widehat{\mathbf{F}}^{0\prime}\mathbf{M}_{\mathbf{W}}{\widehat{\mathbf{F}}^{0}}\mathbf{G}^{-1})^{+}\mathbf{G}^{-1}T^{-\frac{1}{2}-\frac{\tau}{2}}\widehat{\mathbf{F}}^{0\prime}\mathbf{M}_{\mathbf{W}}\overline{\mathbf{E}}_{r}^{0}\bigg\|\big\|\boldsymbol{\alpha}^{0}\big\|+\bigg\|\begin{bmatrix}(T^{-(1+\tau)}{\mathbf{F}^{\prime}}\mathbf{M}_{\mathbf{W}}{\mathbf{F}})^{+}T^{-(1+\tau)}{\mathbf{F}^{\prime}}\mathbf{M}_{\mathbf{W}}\\
\mathbf{0}_{(m-r)\times(t-1)}\end{bmatrix}\overline{\mathbf{E}}_{r}^{0}\bigg\|\big\|\boldsymbol{\alpha}^{0}\big\|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle O_{p}((NT)^{-\frac{1}{2}})+O_{p}(N^{-\frac{1}{2}}T^{-\tau}).

Moving on to
\mathrm{\mathbf{V}}
, consider the expansion

\displaystyle\mathrm{\mathbf{V}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathbf{D}_{\mathbf{F}}\bigl[(\widehat{\mathbf{F}}^{0\prime}\mathbf{M}_{\mathbf{W}}{\widehat{\mathbf{F}}^{0}})^{+}-\mathbf{S}_{\mathbf{M}_{\mathbf{W}}}^{+}\bigr](\widehat{\mathbf{F}}^{0\prime}-{\mathbf{F}}^{0\prime})\mathbf{M}_{\mathbf{W}}\mathbf{u}+\mathbf{D}_{\mathbf{F}}\bigl[(\widehat{\mathbf{F}}^{0\prime}\mathbf{M}_{\mathbf{W}}{\widehat{\mathbf{F}}^{0}})^{+}-\mathbf{S}_{\mathbf{M}_{\mathbf{W}}}^{+}\bigr]{\mathbf{F}}^{0\prime}\mathbf{M}_{\mathbf{W}}\mathbf{u}

(A.63)

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\mathbf{D}_{\mathbf{F}}\mathbf{S}_{\mathbf{M}_{\mathbf{W}}}^{+}(\widehat{\mathbf{F}}^{0\prime}-{\mathbf{F}}^{0\prime})\mathbf{M}_{\mathbf{W}}\mathbf{u}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathbf{D}_{\mathbf{F}}\bigl[(\widehat{\mathbf{F}}^{0\prime}\mathbf{M}_{\mathbf{W}}{\widehat{\mathbf{F}}^{0}})^{+}-\mathbf{S}_{\mathbf{M}_{\mathbf{W}}}^{+}\bigr](\widehat{\mathbf{F}}^{0\prime}-{\mathbf{F}}^{0\prime})\mathbf{M}_{\mathbf{W}}\mathbf{u}+\mathbf{D}_{\mathbf{F}}\bigl[(\widehat{\mathbf{F}}^{0\prime}\mathbf{M}_{\mathbf{W}}{\widehat{\mathbf{F}}^{0}})^{+}-\mathbf{S}_{\mathbf{M}_{\mathbf{W}}}^{+}\bigr]{\mathbf{F}}^{0\prime}\mathbf{M}_{\mathbf{W}}\mathbf{u}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\mathbf{D}_{\mathbf{F}}\mathbf{S}_{\mathbf{M}_{\mathbf{W}}}^{+}\overline{\mathbf{E}}^{0\prime}\mathbf{M}_{\mathbf{W}}\mathbf{u},

where
\mathbf{S}_{\mathbf{M}_{\mathbf{W}}}
is defined as in (
A.52
). Beginning from the third term on the right-hand side of (
A.63
),

\displaystyle\mathbf{D}_{\mathbf{F}}\mathbf{S}_{\mathbf{M}_{\mathbf{W}}}^{+}\overline{\mathbf{E}}^{0\prime}\mathbf{M}_{\mathbf{W}}\mathbf{u}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathbf{D}_{\mathbf{F}}\mathbf{G}^{-1}\begin{bmatrix}(T^{-1-\tau}\mathbf{F}^{\prime}\mathbf{M}_{\mathbf{W}}{\mathbf{F}})^{+}&\mathbf{0}_{r\times(m-r)}\\
\mathbf{0}_{(m-r)\times r}&(T^{-1}\overline{\mathbf{E}}_{-r}^{0\prime}\mathbf{M}_{\mathbf{W}}\overline{\mathbf{E}}_{-r}^{0})^{+}\end{bmatrix}\mathbf{G}^{-1}\begin{bmatrix}\overline{\mathbf{E}}_{r}^{0\prime}\mathbf{M}_{\mathbf{W}}\mathbf{u}\\
\overline{\mathbf{E}}_{-r}^{0\prime}\mathbf{M}_{\mathbf{W}}\mathbf{u}\end{bmatrix}

(A.64)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\begin{bmatrix}(T^{-1-\tau}\mathbf{F}^{\prime}\mathbf{M}_{\mathbf{W}}{\mathbf{F}})^{+}T^{-\frac{3}{4}-\frac{\tau}{2}}\overline{\mathbf{E}}_{r}^{0\prime}\mathbf{M}_{\mathbf{W}}\mathbf{u}\\
(T^{-1}\overline{\mathbf{E}}_{-r}^{0\prime}\mathbf{M}_{\mathbf{W}}\overline{\mathbf{E}}_{-r}^{0})^{+}T^{-\frac{3}{4}}\overline{\mathbf{E}}_{-r}^{0\prime}\mathbf{M}_{\mathbf{W}}\mathbf{u}\end{bmatrix}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle O_{p}(T^{-\frac{1}{4}}),

where the order of the remainder is due to the fact that

\displaystyle\big\|T^{-\frac{3}{4}-\frac{\tau}{2}}\overline{\mathbf{E}}_{r}^{0\prime}\mathbf{M}_{\mathbf{W}}\mathbf{u}\big\|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\big\|T^{-\frac{3}{4}-\frac{\tau}{2}}\overline{\mathbf{E}}_{r}^{0\prime}\mathbf{u}\big\|+\big\|T^{-1-\tau}\overline{\mathbf{E}}_{r}^{0\prime}\mathbf{W}\big\|\big\|(T^{-1-\tau}\mathbf{W}^{\prime}\mathbf{W})^{+}\big\|\big\|T^{-\frac{3}{4}-\frac{\tau}{2}}\mathbf{W}^{\prime}\mathbf{u}\big\|

(A.65)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle O_{p}(N^{-\frac{1}{2}}T^{-\frac{1}{4}-\frac{\tau}{2}}),

and
\big\|(T^{-1}\overline{\mathbf{E}}_{-r}^{0\prime}\mathbf{M}_{\mathbf{W}}\overline{\mathbf{E}}_{-r}^{0})^{+}T^{-\frac{1}{2}}\overline{\mathbf{E}}_{-r}^{0\prime}\mathbf{M}_{\mathbf{W}}\mathbf{u}\big\|=O_{p}(1)
from
Stauskas and Westerlund (2022)
. Note that it can be simplified slightly. Firstly,

\displaystyle\left\|T^{-1/2}\overline{\mathbf{E}}_{-r}^{0\prime}\mathbf{M}_{\mathbf{W}}\mathbf{u}\right\|\leq\left\|T^{-1/2}\overline{\mathbf{E}}_{-r}^{0\prime}\mathbf{u}\right\|+

\displaystyle T^{-1/2}\left\|T^{-(1+\tau)/2}\overline{\mathbf{E}}_{-r}^{0\prime}\mathbf{W}\right\|\left\|\left(T^{-(1+\tau)}\mathbf{W}^{\prime}\mathbf{W}\right)^{+}\right\|

\displaystyle\times\left\|T^{-(1+\tau)/2}\mathbf{W}^{\prime}\mathbf{u}\right\|

\displaystyle=\left\|T^{-1/2}\overline{\mathbf{E}}_{-r}^{0\prime}\mathbf{u}\right\|+O_{p}(T^{-1/2}),

(A.66)

and

\displaystyle\left\|T^{-1}\overline{\mathbf{E}}_{-r}^{0\prime}\mathbf{M}_{\mathbf{W}}\overline{\mathbf{E}}_{-r}^{0}\right\|

\displaystyle\leq\left\|T^{-1}\overline{\mathbf{E}}_{-r}^{0\prime}\overline{\mathbf{E}}_{-r}^{0}\right\|+T^{-1}\left\|T^{-(1+\tau)/2}\overline{\mathbf{E}}_{-r}^{0\prime}\mathbf{W}\right\|^{2}\left\|\left(T^{-(1+\tau)}\mathbf{W}^{\prime}\mathbf{W}\right)^{+}\right\|

\displaystyle=\left\|T^{-1}\overline{\mathbf{E}}_{-r}^{0\prime}\overline{\mathbf{E}}_{-r}^{0}\right\|+O_{p}(T^{-1}).

(A.67)

This leads to

\displaystyle\mathbf{D}_{\mathbf{F}}\mathbf{S}_{\mathbf{M}_{\mathbf{W}}}^{+}\overline{\mathbf{E}}^{0\prime}\mathbf{M}_{\mathbf{W}}\mathbf{u}

\displaystyle=\begin{bmatrix}(T^{-1-\tau}\mathbf{F}^{\prime}\mathbf{M}_{\mathbf{W}}{\mathbf{F}})^{+}T^{-\frac{3}{4}-\frac{\tau}{2}}\overline{\mathbf{E}}_{r}^{0\prime}\mathbf{M}_{\mathbf{W}}\mathbf{u}\\
(T^{-1}\overline{\mathbf{E}}_{-r}^{0\prime}\mathbf{M}_{\mathbf{W}}\overline{\mathbf{E}}_{-r}^{0})^{+}T^{-\frac{3}{4}}\overline{\mathbf{E}}_{-r}^{0\prime}\mathbf{M}_{\mathbf{W}}\mathbf{u}\end{bmatrix}

\displaystyle=\begin{bmatrix}\mathbf{0}_{r}\\
(T^{-1}\overline{\mathbf{E}}_{-r}^{0\prime}\mathbf{M}_{\mathbf{W}}\overline{\mathbf{E}}_{-r}^{0})^{+}T^{-\frac{3}{4}}\overline{\mathbf{E}}_{-r}^{0\prime}\mathbf{u}\end{bmatrix}+O_{p}(T^{-3/4})

\displaystyle=\begin{bmatrix}\mathbf{0}_{r}\\
T^{-1/4}(T^{-1}\overline{\mathbf{E}}^{0\prime}_{-r}\overline{\mathbf{E}}_{-r}^{0})^{+}T^{-1/2}\overline{\mathbf{E}}^{0\prime}_{-r}\mathbf{u}\end{bmatrix}+O_{p}(T^{-1/2})

(A.68)

\displaystyle=\begin{bmatrix}\mathbf{0}_{r}\\
T^{-1/4}\mathbf{v}_{t}\end{bmatrix}+O_{p}(T^{-1/2})=O_{p}(T^{-1/4}).

(A.69)

Here, we define

\displaystyle\mathbf{v}_{t}=(T^{-1}\overline{\mathbf{E}}^{0\prime}_{-r}\overline{\mathbf{E}}_{-r}^{0})^{+}T^{-1/2}\overline{\mathbf{E}}^{0\prime}_{-r}\mathbf{u}

\displaystyle=\left(\frac{1}{T}\sum_{s=1}^{t-1}\overline{\mathbf{e}}_{-r,s}^{0}\overline{\mathbf{e}}_{-r,s}^{0\prime}\right)^{+}\frac{1}{\sqrt{T}}\sum_{s=1}^{t-1}\overline{\mathbf{e}}_{-r,s}^{0}u_{s+1}

\displaystyle=\left(\frac{1}{T}\sum_{s=1}^{t-1}\overline{\mathbf{e}}_{-r,s}^{0}\overline{\mathbf{e}}_{-r,s}^{0\prime}\right)^{-1}\frac{1}{\sqrt{T}}\sum_{s=1}^{t-1}\overline{\mathbf{e}}_{-r,s}^{0}u_{s+1},

(A.70)

which can be interpreted as a least squares regression coefficient of the forecast error on the error arising from the excess
m-r
CAs. With respect to the first term on the right-hand side of (
A.63
), we proceed as follows. Because of the results in (
A.59
) and (
A.64
), we then have

\displaystyle\big\|\mathbf{D}_{\mathbf{F}}\bigl[(\widehat{\mathbf{F}}^{0\prime}\mathbf{M}_{\mathbf{W}}{\widehat{\mathbf{F}}^{0}})^{+}-\mathbf{S}_{\mathbf{M}_{\mathbf{W}}}^{+}\bigr](\widehat{\mathbf{F}}^{0\prime}-{\mathbf{F}}^{0\prime})\mathbf{M}_{\mathbf{W}}\mathbf{u}\big\|

\displaystyle\leq\underbrace{\big\|(\mathbf{G}^{-1}\widehat{\mathbf{F}}^{0\prime}\mathbf{M}_{\mathbf{W}}{\widehat{\mathbf{F}}^{0}}\mathbf{G}^{-1})^{+}-(\mathbf{G}^{-1}\mathbf{S}_{\mathbf{M}_{\mathbf{W}}}\mathbf{G}^{-1})^{+}\big\|}_{O_{p}(N^{-\frac{1}{2}}T^{-\frac{\tau}{2}})+O_{p}(T^{-\frac{1}{2}})}\big\|T^{-\frac{1}{4}}\mathbf{G}^{-1}\overline{\mathbf{E}}^{0\prime}\mathbf{M}_{\mathbf{W}}\mathbf{u}\big\|

\displaystyle=O_{p}(N^{-\frac{1}{2}}T^{-\frac{1}{4}-\frac{\tau}{2}})+O_{p}(T^{-\frac{3}{4}}).

(A.71)

Finally, the order of the remaining term in (
A.63
) can be shown to be

\displaystyle\big\|\mathbf{D}_{\mathbf{F}}\bigl[\mathbf{G}^{-1}(\mathbf{G}^{-1}\widehat{\mathbf{F}}^{0\prime}\mathbf{M}_{\mathbf{W}}{\widehat{\mathbf{F}}^{0}}\mathbf{G}^{-1})^{+}-(\mathbf{G}^{-1}\mathbf{S}_{\mathbf{M}_{\mathbf{W}}}^{+}\mathbf{G}^{-1})\bigr]\mathbf{G}^{-1}{\mathbf{F}}^{0\prime}\mathbf{M}_{\mathbf{W}}\mathbf{u}\big\|

\displaystyle\leq\big\|(\mathbf{G}^{-1}\widehat{\mathbf{F}}^{0\prime}\mathbf{M}_{\mathbf{W}}{\widehat{\mathbf{F}}^{0}}\mathbf{G}^{-1})^{+}-(\mathbf{G}^{-1}\mathbf{S}_{\mathbf{M}_{\mathbf{W}}}^{+}\mathbf{G}^{-1})\big\|\big\|T^{-\frac{1}{4}}\mathbf{G}^{-1}{\mathbf{F}}^{0\prime}\mathbf{M}_{\mathbf{W}}\mathbf{u}\big\|

\displaystyle\leq\big\|(\mathbf{G}^{-1}\widehat{\mathbf{F}}^{0\prime}\mathbf{M}_{\mathbf{W}}{\widehat{\mathbf{F}}^{0}}\mathbf{G}^{-1})^{+}-(\mathbf{G}^{-1}\mathbf{S}_{\mathbf{M}_{\mathbf{W}}}^{+}\mathbf{G}^{-1})\big\|\big\|T^{-\frac{3}{4}-\frac{\tau}{2}}{\mathbf{F}}^{0\prime}\mathbf{u}\big\|

\displaystyle+\big\|(\mathbf{G}^{-1}\widehat{\mathbf{F}}^{0\prime}\mathbf{M}_{\mathbf{W}}{\widehat{\mathbf{F}}^{0}}\mathbf{G}^{-1})^{+}-(\mathbf{G}^{-1}\mathbf{S}_{\mathbf{M}_{\mathbf{W}}}^{+}\mathbf{G}^{-1})\big\|\big\|T^{-1-\tau}{\mathbf{F}}^{0\prime}\mathbf{W}\big\|\big\|(T^{-1-\tau}\mathbf{W}^{\prime}\mathbf{W})^{+}\big\|\big\|T^{-\frac{3}{4}-\frac{\tau}{2}}\mathbf{W}^{\prime}\mathbf{u}\big\|

\displaystyle=O_{p}(N^{-\frac{1}{2}}T^{-\frac{1}{4}-\frac{\tau}{2}})+O_{p}(T^{-\frac{3}{4}}).

(A.72)

Hence, putting together the results in (
A.64
), (
A.71
) and (
A.72
) gives the order of
\mathrm{\mathbf{V}}
:

\displaystyle\big\|\mathrm{\mathbf{V}}\big\|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle O_{p}(T^{-\frac{1}{4}}).

(A.73)

It remains to address the term
\mathrm{\mathbf{V}\mathbf{I}}
. Recall the decomposition

\displaystyle\mathrm{\mathbf{V}\mathbf{I}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle T^{\frac{1}{4}+\frac{\tau}{2}}\bigl[({\mathbf{W}^{\prime}}\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{\mathbf{F}}^{0}}{\mathbf{W}})^{+}-({\mathbf{W}^{\prime}}\mathbf{M}_{\mathbf{F}^{0}}{\mathbf{W}})^{+}\bigr]{\mathbf{W}^{\prime}}(\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{\mathbf{F}}^{0}}-\mathbf{M}_{\mathbf{F}^{0}})\mathbf{u}

(A.74)

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle T^{\frac{1}{4}+\frac{\tau}{2}}\bigl[({\mathbf{W}^{\prime}}\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{\mathbf{F}}^{0}}{\mathbf{W}})^{+}-({\mathbf{W}^{\prime}}\mathbf{M}_{\mathbf{F}^{0}}{\mathbf{W}})^{+}\bigr]{\mathbf{W}^{\prime}}\mathbf{M}_{\mathbf{F}^{0}}\mathbf{u}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle T^{\frac{1}{4}+\frac{\tau}{2}}({\mathbf{W}^{\prime}}\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{\mathbf{F}}^{0}}{\mathbf{W}})^{+}{\mathbf{W}^{\prime}}(\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{\mathbf{F}}^{0}}-\mathbf{M}_{\mathbf{F}^{0}})\mathbf{u}.

Using (A.77) in the Supplement of
Stauskas and Westerlund (2022)
, note that

\displaystyle\big\|T^{-\frac{1}{2}-\frac{\tau}{2}}{\mathbf{W}^{\prime}}(\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{\mathbf{F}}^{0}}-\mathbf{M}_{\mathbf{F}^{0}})\mathbf{u}\big\|

\displaystyle\leq\big\|T^{-\frac{1}{2}-\frac{\tau}{2}}{\mathbf{W}^{\prime}}(\overline{\mathbf{E}}_{-r}^{0}(\overline{\mathbf{E}}_{-r}^{0\prime}\overline{\mathbf{E}}_{-r}^{0})^{+}\overline{\mathbf{E}}_{-r}^{0\prime}+\overline{\mathbf{E}}_{r}^{0}(\mathbf{F}^{\prime}\mathbf{F})^{+}\overline{\mathbf{E}}_{r}^{0\prime}+\overline{\mathbf{E}}_{r}^{0}(\mathbf{F}^{\prime}\mathbf{F})^{+}\mathbf{F}^{\prime}+\mathbf{F}(\mathbf{F}^{\prime}\mathbf{F})^{+}\overline{\mathbf{E}}_{r}^{0\prime}+\widehat{\mathbf{F}}^{0}[(\widehat{\mathbf{F}}^{0\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{F}}^{0})^{+}+\mathbf{S}^{+}]\widehat{\mathbf{F}}^{0\prime})\mathbf{u}\big\|

\displaystyle\leq\big\|T^{-1-\frac{\tau}{2}}{\mathbf{W}^{\prime}}\overline{\mathbf{E}}_{-r}^{0}\big\|\big\|(T^{-1}\overline{\mathbf{E}}_{-r}^{0\prime}\overline{\mathbf{E}}_{-r}^{0})^{+}\big\|\big\|T^{-\frac{1}{2}}\overline{\mathbf{E}}_{-r}^{0\prime}\mathbf{u}\big\|+T^{-\tau}\big\|T^{-1-\frac{\tau}{2}}{\mathbf{W}^{\prime}}\overline{\mathbf{E}}_{r}^{0}\big\|\big\|(T^{-1-\tau}\mathbf{F}^{\prime}\mathbf{F})^{+}\big\|\big\|T^{-\frac{1}{2}}\overline{\mathbf{E}}_{r}^{0\prime}\mathbf{u}\big\|

\displaystyle+T^{-\frac{\tau}{2}}\big\|T^{-1-\frac{\tau}{2}}{\mathbf{W}^{\prime}}\overline{\mathbf{E}}_{r}^{0}\big\|\big\|(T^{-1-\tau}\mathbf{F}^{\prime}\mathbf{F})^{+}\big\|\big\|T^{-\frac{1}{2}-\frac{\tau}{2}}\mathbf{F}^{\prime}\mathbf{u}\big\|+T^{-\frac{\tau}{2}}\big\|T^{-1-\tau}{\mathbf{W}^{\prime}}\mathbf{F}\big\|\big\|(T^{-1-\tau}\mathbf{F}^{\prime}\mathbf{F})^{+}\big\|\big\|T^{-\frac{1}{2}}\overline{\mathbf{E}}_{r}^{0\prime}\mathbf{u}\big\|

\displaystyle+\big\|T^{-\frac{1}{2}-\frac{\tau}{2}}{\mathbf{W}^{\prime}}\widehat{\mathbf{F}}^{0}\mathbf{G}^{-1}\big\|\big\|(\mathbf{G}^{-1}\widehat{\mathbf{F}}^{0\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{F}}^{0}\mathbf{G}^{-1})^{+}+(\mathbf{G}^{-1}\mathbf{S}\mathbf{G}^{-1})^{+}\big\|\big\|\mathbf{G}^{-1}\widehat{\mathbf{F}}^{0\prime}\mathbf{u}\big\|

\displaystyle=O_{p}(T^{-\frac{1}{2}})+O_{p}(N^{-1}T^{-\frac{\tau}{2}})+O_{p}(N^{-\frac{1}{2}}T^{-\frac{1}{2}-\frac{\tau}{2}})+O_{p}(N^{-\frac{1}{2}}T^{-\frac{\tau}{2}})+O_{p}(N^{-\frac{1}{2}}T^{-\frac{\tau}{2}})+O_{p}(T^{-\frac{1}{2}})

\displaystyle=O_{p}(T^{-\frac{1}{2}})+O_{p}(N^{-\frac{1}{2}}T^{-\frac{\tau}{2}}),

(A.75)

by (
A.41
) and the independence of
\mathbf{W}
and
\overline{\mathbf{E}}
. By putting this latter result together with
\big\|(T^{-1-\tau}{\mathbf{W}^{\prime}}\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{\mathbf{F}}^{0}}{\mathbf{W}})^{+}-(T^{-1-\tau}{\mathbf{W}^{\prime}}\mathbf{M}_{\mathbf{F}^{0}}{\mathbf{W}})^{+}\big\|=O_{p}(N^{-\frac{1}{2}}T^{-\frac{\tau}{2}})+O_{p}(T^{-\frac{1}{2}})
, we can show that (
A.74
) reduces to

\displaystyle\big\|\mathrm{\mathbf{V}\mathbf{I}}\big\|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle T^{-\frac{1}{4}}\big\|(T^{-1-\tau}{\mathbf{W}^{\prime}}\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{\mathbf{F}}^{0}}{\mathbf{W}})^{+}-(T^{-1-\tau}{\mathbf{W}^{\prime}}\mathbf{M}_{\mathbf{F}^{0}}{\mathbf{W}})^{+}\big\|\big\|T^{-\frac{1}{2}-\frac{\tau}{2}}{\mathbf{W}^{\prime}}(\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{\mathbf{F}}^{0}}-\mathbf{M}_{\mathbf{F}^{0}})\mathbf{u}\big\|

(A.76)

\displaystyle+
