\displaystyle T^{-\frac{1}{4}}\big\|(T^{-1-\tau}{\mathbf{W}^{\prime}}\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{\mathbf{F}}^{0}}{\mathbf{W}})^{+}-(T^{-1-\tau}{\mathbf{W}^{\prime}}\mathbf{M}_{\mathbf{F}^{0}}{\mathbf{W}})^{+}\big\|\big\|T^{-\frac{1}{2}-\frac{\tau}{2}}{\mathbf{W}^{\prime}}\mathbf{M}_{\mathbf{F}^{0}}\mathbf{u}\big\|

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle T^{-\frac{1}{4}}\big\|(T^{-1-\tau}{\mathbf{W}^{\prime}}\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{\mathbf{F}}^{0}}{\mathbf{W}})^{+}\big\|\big\|T^{-\frac{1}{2}-\frac{\tau}{2}}{\mathbf{W}^{\prime}}(\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{\mathbf{F}}^{0}}-\mathbf{M}_{\mathbf{F}^{0}})\mathbf{u}\big\|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle O_{p}(T^{-\frac{3}{4}})+O_{p}(N^{-\frac{1}{2}}T^{-\frac{1}{4}-\frac{\tau}{2}}).

With the results of
\mathrm{\mathbf{I}}-\mathrm{\mathbf{V}\mathbf{I}}
in mind, we finally obtain

\displaystyle\mathbf{D}_{T}(\mathbf{Q}_{N}^{-1}\boldsymbol{\widehat{\delta}}_{t}-\boldsymbol{\widetilde{\delta}_{t}^{0}})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\begin{bmatrix}\mathrm{\mathbf{I}}\\
\mathrm{\mathbf{I}\mathbf{I}}\end{bmatrix}-\begin{bmatrix}\mathrm{\mathbf{I}\mathbf{I}\mathbf{I}}\\
\mathrm{\mathbf{I}\mathbf{V}}\end{bmatrix}+\begin{bmatrix}\mathrm{\mathbf{V}}\\
\mathrm{\mathbf{V}\mathbf{I}}\end{bmatrix}

(A.77)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle O_{p}(N^{-\frac{1}{2}}T^{-\frac{\tau}{2}})+O_{p}(T^{-\frac{1}{4}}),

thus concluding the lemma.
\blacksquare

7.3.2
Proof of Lemma 2

We now prove Lemma 2. For the reference, we will demonstrate the following statements that constitute
|\xi_{N,T,j}|=o_{p}(1)
for
j=1,\ldots,4
:

(a)

\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{t=k_{0}}^{k_{0}+m_{0}-1}\widetilde{u}_{1,t+1}(\widetilde{u}_{2,t+1}-\widehat{u}_{2,t+1})=o_{p}(1)

(b)

\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{t=k_{0}}^{k_{0}+l_{2}^{0}-1}(\widetilde{u}_{2,t+1}-\widehat{u}_{2,t+1})^{2}=o_{p}(1)

(c)

\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{t=k_{0}}^{k_{0}+l_{2}^{0}-1}\widetilde{u}_{2,t+1}(\widetilde{u}_{2,t+1}-\widehat{u}_{2,t+1})=o_{p}(1)

(d)

\frac{1}{n}\sum_{l_{2}}\frac{n}{l_{2}}\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{t=k_{0}}^{k_{0}+l_{2}-1}(\widetilde{u}_{2,t+1}-\widehat{u}_{2,t+1})^{2}=o_{p}(1)

(e)

\frac{1}{n}\sum_{l_{2}}\frac{n}{l_{2}}\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{t=k_{0}}^{k_{0}+l_{2}-1}\widetilde{u}_{2,t+1}(\widetilde{u}_{2,t+1}-\widehat{u}_{2,t+1})=o_{p}(1)

(f)

|\widetilde{\phi}^{2}-\widehat{\phi}^{2}|=o_{p}(1)
.

Consider (a). Beginning from the definitions of
\widetilde{u}_{1,t+1}
and
\widehat{u}_{2,t+1}
and the fact that
\boldsymbol{\widetilde{\delta}}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{z}_{t}=\boldsymbol{\widetilde{\delta}}_{t}^{0\prime}\mathbf{Q}_{N}^{\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{z}}_{t}^{0}-\boldsymbol{\widetilde{\alpha}_{t}}^{\prime}\overline{\mathbf{e}}_{r,t}^{0}
:

\displaystyle\widetilde{u}_{2,t+1}-\widehat{u}_{2,t+1}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\boldsymbol{\widehat{\delta}}_{t}^{\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{z}}_{t}-\boldsymbol{\widetilde{\delta}}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{z}_{t}

(A.78)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\boldsymbol{\widetilde{\delta}}_{t}^{\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{z}}_{t}-\boldsymbol{\widetilde{\delta}}_{t}^{0\prime\textasciiacute}\mathbf{Q}_{N}^{\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{z}}_{t}+\boldsymbol{\widetilde{\alpha}}_{t}^{\prime}\overline{\mathbf{e}}_{r,t}^{0}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\boldsymbol{\widehat{\delta}}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{Q}_{N}^{-1\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{z}}_{t}^{0}-\boldsymbol{\widetilde{\delta}_{t}^{0\prime\textasciiacute}}\widehat{\mathbf{z}}_{t}^{0}+\boldsymbol{\widetilde{\alpha}}_{t}^{\prime}\overline{\mathbf{e}}_{r,t}^{0}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle(\mathbf{Q}_{N}^{-1}\boldsymbol{\widehat{\delta}}_{t}-\boldsymbol{\widetilde{\delta}}_{t}^{0})^{\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{z}}_{t}^{0}+\boldsymbol{\widetilde{\alpha}}_{t}^{\prime}\overline{\mathbf{e}}_{r,t}^{0},

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle(\mathbf{Q}_{N}^{-1}\boldsymbol{\widehat{\delta}}_{t}-\boldsymbol{\widetilde{\delta}}_{t}^{0})^{\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{z}}_{t}^{0}+(\boldsymbol{\widetilde{\alpha}}_{t}-\boldsymbol{\alpha})^{\prime}\overline{\mathbf{e}}_{r,t}^{0}+\boldsymbol{\alpha}^{\prime}\overline{\mathbf{e}}_{r,t}^{0}

the first term of which Lemma 1 is concerned with. With respect to the last term on the right-hand side of (
A.78
), whereas, recall that
\big\|\overline{\mathbf{e}}_{r,t}^{0}\big\|=O_{p}(N^{-\frac{1}{2}})
and observe that

\displaystyle\big\|\boldsymbol{\widetilde{\alpha}_{t}}-\boldsymbol{\alpha}\big\|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\big\|(t^{-1-\tau}\mathbf{F}^{\prime}\mathbf{M}_{\mathbf{W}}\mathbf{F})^{-1}\big\|\big\|t^{-1-\tau}\mathbf{F}^{\prime}\mathbf{M}_{\mathbf{W}}\mathbf{u}\big\|

(A.79)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle O_{p}(T^{-\frac{1}{2}-\frac{\tau}{2}}).

Then it follows that

\displaystyle\big\|\boldsymbol{\widetilde{\alpha}_{t}}^{\prime}\overline{\mathbf{e}}_{r,t}^{0}\big\|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\big\|(\boldsymbol{\widetilde{\alpha}_{t}}-\boldsymbol{\alpha})^{\prime}\overline{\mathbf{e}}_{r,t}^{0}+\boldsymbol{\alpha}^{\prime}\overline{\mathbf{e}}_{r,t}^{0}\big\|

(A.80)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\big\|(\boldsymbol{\widetilde{\alpha}_{t}}-\boldsymbol{\alpha})^{\prime}\big\|\big\|\overline{\mathbf{e}}_{r,t}^{0}\big\|+\big\|\boldsymbol{\alpha}^{\prime}\big\|\big\|\overline{\mathbf{e}}_{r,t}^{0}\big\|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle O_{p}(N^{-\frac{1}{2}}T^{-\frac{1}{2}-\frac{\tau}{2}})

which holds uniformly in
t
, thus meaning that the last term on the right-hand side of (
A.78
) is negligible. Let us consider the last two summations in (3.3). First, using the definition
\widetilde{u}_{1,t+1}=u_{t+1}-(\boldsymbol{\widetilde{\theta}}_{t}-\boldsymbol{\theta})^{\prime}\mathbf{w}_{t}+\boldsymbol{\alpha}^{\prime}\mathrm{\mathbf{f}}_{t}
and the result in (
A.78
) gives

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{t=k_{0}}^{k_{0}+m_{0}-1}\widetilde{u}_{1,t+1}(\widetilde{u}_{2,t+1}-\widehat{u}_{2,t+1})

(A.81)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{t=k_{0}}^{k_{0}+m_{0}-1}u_{t+1}(\widetilde{u}_{2,t+1}-\widehat{u}_{2,t+1})-\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{t=k_{0}}^{k_{0}+m_{0}-1}(\boldsymbol{\widetilde{\theta}}_{t}-\boldsymbol{\theta})^{\prime}\mathbf{w}_{t}(\widetilde{u}_{2,t+1}-\widehat{u}_{2,t+1})

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{t=k_{0}}^{k_{0}+m_{0}-1}\boldsymbol{\alpha}^{\prime}\mathrm{\mathbf{f}}_{t}(\widetilde{u}_{2,t+1}-\widehat{u}_{2,t+1})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{t=k_{0}}^{k_{0}+m_{0}-1}u_{t+1}((\mathbf{Q}_{N}^{-1}\boldsymbol{\widehat{\delta}}_{t}-\boldsymbol{\widetilde{\delta}}_{t}^{0})^{\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{z}}_{t}^{0}+(\boldsymbol{\widetilde{\alpha}}_{t}-\boldsymbol{\alpha})^{\prime}\overline{\mathbf{e}}_{r,t}^{0}+\boldsymbol{\alpha}^{\prime}\overline{\mathbf{e}}_{r,t}^{0})-\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{t=k_{0}}^{k_{0}+m_{0}-1}(\boldsymbol{\widetilde{\theta}}_{t}-\boldsymbol{\theta})^{\prime}\mathbf{w}_{t}(\widetilde{u}_{2,t+1}-\widehat{u}_{2,t+1})

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{t=k_{0}}^{k_{0}+m_{0}-1}\boldsymbol{\alpha}^{\prime}\mathrm{\mathbf{f}}_{t}(\widetilde{u}_{2,t+1}-\widehat{u}_{2,t+1})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{t=k_{0}}^{k_{0}+m_{0}-1}u_{t+1}(\mathbf{Q}_{N}^{-1}\boldsymbol{\widehat{\delta}}_{t}-\boldsymbol{\widetilde{\delta}}_{t}^{0})^{\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{z}}_{t}^{0}+\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{t=k_{0}}^{k_{0}+m_{0}-1}(\boldsymbol{\widetilde{\alpha}_{t}}-\boldsymbol{\alpha})^{\prime}\overline{\mathbf{e}}_{r,t}^{0}u_{t+1}+\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{t=k_{0}}^{k_{0}+m_{0}-1}\boldsymbol{\alpha}^{\prime}\overline{\mathbf{e}}_{r,t}^{0}u_{t+1}

\displaystyle-

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{t=k_{0}}^{k_{0}+m_{0}-1}(\boldsymbol{\widetilde{\theta}}_{t}-\boldsymbol{\theta})^{\prime}\mathbf{w}_{t}(\widetilde{u}_{2,t+1}-\widehat{u}_{2,t+1})+\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{t=k_{0}}^{k_{0}+m_{0}-1}\boldsymbol{\alpha}^{\prime}\mathrm{\mathbf{f}}_{t}(\widetilde{u}_{2,t+1}-\widehat{u}_{2,t+1})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathrm{\mathbf{I}}+\mathrm{\mathbf{I}I}+\mathrm{\mathbf{I}II}-\mathrm{\mathbf{I}V}+\mathrm{\mathbf{V}},

where the definitions of
\mathrm{\mathbf{I}}
-
\mathrm{\mathbf{V}}
are implicit. Begining from
\mathrm{\mathbf{I}I}
, and using the fact that
\sqrt{\frac{T}{n}}=\frac{1}{\sqrt{1-\pi_{0}}}+O(T^{-1/2})
, we have

\displaystyle\left|\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{t=k_{0}}^{k_{0}+m_{0}-1}(\boldsymbol{\widetilde{\alpha}_{t}}-\boldsymbol{\alpha})^{\prime}\overline{\mathbf{e}}_{r,t}^{0}u_{t+1}\right|

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\sqrt{N}}\left|\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\frac{1}{T^{1/2+\tau/2}}\sum_{t=k_{0}}^{k_{0}+m_{0}-1}T^{1/2+\tau/2}(\boldsymbol{\widetilde{\alpha}_{t}}-\boldsymbol{\alpha})^{\prime}(\sqrt{N}\overline{\mathbf{e}}_{r,t}^{0})u_{t+1}\right|

\displaystyle=T^{-\tau/2}\frac{1}{\sqrt{N}}\left|\frac{T}{\sqrt{nT}}\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=k_{0}}^{k_{0}+m_{0}-1}T^{1/2+\tau/2}(\boldsymbol{\widetilde{\alpha}_{t}}-\boldsymbol{\alpha})^{\prime}(\sqrt{N}\overline{\mathbf{e}}_{r,t}^{0})u_{t+1}\right|

\displaystyle\leq T^{-\tau/2}\frac{1}{\sqrt{N}}\sqrt{\frac{T}{n}}\sup_{t}\left\|T^{1/2+\tau/2}(\boldsymbol{\widetilde{\alpha}_{t}}-\boldsymbol{\alpha})\right\|\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=k_{0}}^{k_{0}+m_{0}-1}|(\sqrt{N}\overline{\mathbf{e}}_{r,t}^{0})u_{t+1}|

\displaystyle=O_{p}(N^{-\frac{1}{2}}T^{-\frac{\tau}{2}}),

(A.82)

because
\sup_{t}\|T^{\frac{1}{2}+\frac{\tau}{2}}(\boldsymbol{\widetilde{\alpha}_{t}}-\boldsymbol{\alpha})\|=O_{p}(1)
as in (38) and (46) of
Pitarakis, 2023
, and the term with the summation is of the same order. In a similar manner, the order of
\mathrm{\mathbf{I}II}
is obtained as follows:

\displaystyle\left|\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{t=k_{0}}^{k_{0}+m_{0}-1}\boldsymbol{\alpha}^{\prime}\overline{\mathbf{e}}_{r,t}^{0}u_{t+1}\right|

(A.83)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\sqrt{N}T^{\frac{1}{4}+\frac{\tau}{2}}}\sqrt{\frac{T}{n}}\big\|\boldsymbol{\alpha}^{0}\big\|\bigg\|\frac{1}{\sqrt{T}}\sum_{t=k_{0}}^{k_{0}+m_{0}-1}(\sqrt{N}\overline{\mathbf{e}}_{r,t}^{0})u_{t+1}\bigg\|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle O_{p}(N^{-\frac{1}{2}}T^{-\frac{1}{4}-\frac{\tau}{2}})

under the same logic. Let us now turn our attention to
\mathrm{\mathbf{I}}
:

\displaystyle\left|\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{t=k_{0}}^{k_{0}+m_{0}-1}u_{t+1}(\mathbf{Q}_{N}^{-1}\boldsymbol{\widehat{\delta}}_{t}-\boldsymbol{\widetilde{\delta}}_{t}^{0})^{\prime}\mathbf{D}_{T}\mathbf{D}_{T}^{-1}\widehat{\mathbf{z}}_{t}^{0}\right|

\displaystyle=T^{-\frac{1}{4}}\left|\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{t=k_{0}}^{k_{0}+m_{0}-1}(\mathbf{Q}_{N}^{-1}\boldsymbol{\widehat{\delta}}_{t}-\boldsymbol{\widetilde{\delta}}_{t}^{0})^{\prime}\mathbf{D}_{T}T^{\frac{1}{4}}\mathbf{D}_{T}^{-1}\widehat{\mathbf{z}}_{t}^{0}u_{t+1}\right|

\displaystyle=T^{\frac{\tau}{2}-\frac{1}{2}}\left|\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{t=k_{0}}^{k_{0}+m_{0}-1}\left[(\mathbf{Q}_{N}^{-1}\boldsymbol{\widehat{\delta}}_{t}-\boldsymbol{\widetilde{\delta}}_{t}^{0})^{\prime}\mathbf{D}_{T}T^{1/4}\right](T^{1/4}\mathbf{D}_{T}^{-1})T^{-\tau/2}\widehat{\mathbf{z}}_{t}^{0}u_{t+1}\right|

\displaystyle=O_{p}(T^{(\tau-1)/2}),

(A.84)

which holds for all
\tau\in(0,1)
by noting that the summand,
\left[(\mathbf{Q}_{N}^{-1}\boldsymbol{\widehat{\delta}}_{t}-\boldsymbol{\widetilde{\delta}}_{t}^{0})^{\prime}\mathbf{D}_{T}T^{1/4}\right](T^{1/4}\mathbf{D}_{T}^{-1})T^{-\tau/2}\widehat{\mathbf{z}}_{t}^{0}u_{t+1}
, is a heterogeneous martingale difference process by Assumption 1. In particular,
\left\|(\mathbf{Q}_{N}^{-1}\boldsymbol{\widehat{\delta}}_{t}-\boldsymbol{\widetilde{\delta}}_{t}^{0})^{\prime}\mathbf{D}_{T}T^{1/4}\right\|=O_{p}(1)
by Lemma 1, while

\displaystyle\sup_{t}\left\|T^{-\tau/2}\widehat{\mathbf{z}}_{t}^{0}\right\|\leq\sup_{t}\left\|T^{-\tau/2}\mathbf{z}_{t}\right\|+\sup_{t}\left\|T^{-\tau/2}\overline{\mathbf{e}}_{t}^{0}\right\|=O_{p}(1),

(A.85)

as
sup_{t}\left\|T^{-\tau/2}\mathbf{z}_{t}\right\|=O_{p}(1)
based on
Magdalinos and Phillips (2009)
. For
\mathrm{\mathbf{I}V}
, whereas, we can start by showing that, as
T\to\infty
, we have

\displaystyle T^{\frac{1}{4}+\frac{\tau}{2}}(\boldsymbol{\widetilde{\theta}}_{t}-\boldsymbol{\theta})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle T^{\frac{1}{4}+\frac{\tau}{2}}(T^{-1-\tau}\mathbf{W}^{\prime}\mathbf{W})^{-1}T^{-1-\tau}\mathbf{W}^{\prime}(\mathbf{F}\boldsymbol{\alpha}+\mathbf{u})

(A.86)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle(t^{-1-\tau}\mathbf{W}^{\prime}\mathbf{W})^{-1}t^{-1-\tau}\mathbf{W}^{\prime}\mathbf{F}\boldsymbol{\alpha}^{0}+(T^{1+\tau}t^{-1-\tau})T^{\frac{1}{4}+\frac{\tau}{2}}(t^{-1-\tau}\mathbf{W}^{\prime}\mathbf{W})^{-1}T^{-1-\tau}\mathbf{W}^{\prime}\mathbf{u}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle(t^{-1-\tau}\mathbf{W}^{\prime}\mathbf{W})^{-1}t^{-1-\tau}\mathbf{W}^{\prime}\mathbf{F}\boldsymbol{\alpha}^{0}+T^{-\frac{1}{4}}(T^{1+\tau}t^{-1-\tau})(t^{-1-\tau}\mathbf{W}^{\prime}\mathbf{W})^{-1}T^{-\frac{1}{2}-\frac{\tau}{2}}\mathbf{W}^{\prime}\mathbf{u}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle(t^{-1-\tau}\mathbf{W}^{\prime}\mathbf{W})^{-1}t^{-1-\tau}\mathbf{W}^{\prime}\mathbf{F}\boldsymbol{\alpha}^{0}+O_{p}(T^{-\frac{1}{4}})

\displaystyle\to_{p}

\displaystyle\boldsymbol{\Sigma}_{ww}^{-1}\boldsymbol{\Sigma}_{wf}\boldsymbol{\alpha}^{0},

because of the result in (
A.23
) and the fact that
\big\|(t^{-1-\tau}\mathbf{W}^{\prime}\mathbf{W})^{-1}T^{-\frac{1}{2}-\frac{\tau}{2}}\mathbf{W}^{\prime}\mathbf{u}\big\|=O_{p}(1)
. Hence, we have that
\big\|T^{\frac{1}{4}+\frac{\tau}{2}}(\boldsymbol{\widetilde{\theta}}_{t}-\boldsymbol{\theta})\big\|=O_{p}(1)
. To be more precise, we have that
\sup_{t}\big\|T^{\frac{1}{4}+\frac{\tau}{2}}(\boldsymbol{\widetilde{\theta}}_{t}-\boldsymbol{\theta})\big\|=O_{p}(1)
. Further, substituting (
A.78
) in
\mathrm{\mathbf{I}V}
yields

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{t=k_{0}}^{k_{0}+m_{0}-1}(\boldsymbol{\widetilde{\theta}}_{t}-\boldsymbol{\theta})^{\prime}\mathbf{w}_{t}(\widetilde{u}_{2,t+1}-\widehat{u}_{2,t+1})

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{t=k_{0}}^{k_{0}+m_{0}-1}(\boldsymbol{\widetilde{\theta}}_{t}-\boldsymbol{\theta})^{\prime}\mathbf{w}_{t}((\mathbf{Q}_{N}^{-1}\boldsymbol{\widehat{\delta}}_{t}-\boldsymbol{\widetilde{\delta}}_{t}^{0})^{\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{z}}_{t}^{0}+(\boldsymbol{\widetilde{\alpha}}_{t}-\boldsymbol{\alpha})^{\prime}\overline{\mathbf{e}}_{r,t}^{0}+\boldsymbol{\alpha}^{\prime}\overline{\mathbf{e}}_{r,t}^{0})

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{t=k_{0}}^{k_{0}+m_{0}-1}(\boldsymbol{\widetilde{\theta}}_{t}-\boldsymbol{\theta})^{\prime}\mathbf{w}_{t}(\mathbf{Q}_{N}^{-1}\boldsymbol{\widehat{\delta}}_{t}-\boldsymbol{\widetilde{\delta}}_{t}^{0})^{\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{z}}_{t}^{0}+\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{t=k_{0}}^{k_{0}+m_{0}-1}(\boldsymbol{\widetilde{\theta}}_{t}-\boldsymbol{\theta})^{\prime}\mathbf{w}_{t}(\boldsymbol{\widetilde{\alpha}}_{t}-\boldsymbol{\alpha})^{\prime}\overline{\mathbf{e}}_{r,t}^{0}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{t=k_{0}}^{k_{0}+m_{0}-1}(\boldsymbol{\widetilde{\theta}}_{t}-\boldsymbol{\theta})^{\prime}\mathbf{w}_{t}\boldsymbol{\alpha}^{\prime}\overline{\mathbf{e}}_{r,t}^{0}.

(A.87)

Here, the third term in this decomposition becomes

\displaystyle\left|\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{t=k_{0}}^{k_{0}+m_{0}-1}(\boldsymbol{\widetilde{\theta}}_{t}-\boldsymbol{\theta})^{\prime}\mathbf{w}_{t}\boldsymbol{\alpha}^{\prime}\overline{\mathbf{e}}_{r,t}^{0}\right|

\displaystyle=\left|T^{-\frac{1}{4}-\frac{\tau}{2}}\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{t=k_{0}}^{k_{0}+m_{0}-1}T^{\frac{1}{4}+\frac{\tau}{2}}(\boldsymbol{\widetilde{\theta}}_{t}-\boldsymbol{\theta})^{\prime}T^{-\frac{1}{4}-\frac{\tau}{2}}\mathbf{w}_{t}T^{\frac{1}{4}+\frac{\tau}{2}}\boldsymbol{\alpha}^{\prime}\overline{\mathbf{e}}_{r,t}^{0}\right|

\displaystyle\leq T^{-\frac{1}{4}-\frac{\tau}{2}}\big\|\boldsymbol{\alpha}^{0}\big\|\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq k_{0}+m_{0}-1}\big\|T^{\frac{1}{4}+\frac{\tau}{2}}(\boldsymbol{\widetilde{\theta}}_{t}-\boldsymbol{\theta})\big\|\left|\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{t=k_{0}}^{k_{0}+m_{0}-1}T^{-\frac{1}{4}-\frac{\tau}{2}}\mathbf{w}_{t}\overline{\mathbf{e}}_{r,t}^{0\prime}\right|

\displaystyle\leq\big\|\boldsymbol{\alpha}^{0}\big\|\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq k_{0}+m_{0}-1}\big\|T^{\frac{1}{4}+\frac{\tau}{2}}(\boldsymbol{\widetilde{\theta}}_{t}-\boldsymbol{\theta})\big\|\sqrt{\frac{T}{n}}\frac{1}{T^{1+\tau}}\sum_{t=k_{0}}^{k_{0}+m_{0}-1}\big\|\mathbf{w}_{t}\big\|\big\|\overline{\mathbf{e}}_{r,t}^{0}\big\|

\displaystyle\leq T^{-\frac{\tau}{2}}\big\|\boldsymbol{\alpha}^{0}\big\|\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq k_{0}+m_{0}-1}\big\|T^{\frac{1}{4}+\frac{\tau}{2}}(\boldsymbol{\widetilde{\theta}}_{t}-\boldsymbol{\theta})\big\|\sqrt{\frac{T}{n}}\left(\frac{1}{T^{1+\tau}}\sum_{t=k_{0}}^{k_{0}+m_{0}-1}\big\|\mathbf{w}_{t}\big\|^{2}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\left(\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=k_{0}}^{k_{0}+m_{0}-1}\big\|\overline{\mathbf{e}}_{r,t}^{0}\big\|^{2}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle=O_{p}(N^{-\frac{1}{2}}T^{-\frac{\tau}{2}}).

(A.88)

Moving on to the second term, we follow a similar argument:

\displaystyle\left|\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{t=k_{0}}^{k_{0}+m_{0}-1}(\boldsymbol{\widetilde{\theta}}_{t}-\boldsymbol{\theta})^{\prime}\mathbf{w}_{t}(\boldsymbol{\widetilde{\alpha}}_{t}-\boldsymbol{\alpha})^{\prime}\overline{\mathbf{e}}_{r,t}^{0}\right|

\displaystyle=\left|T^{-\frac{1}{4}-\frac{\tau}{2}}\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{t=k_{0}}^{k_{0}+m_{0}-1}T^{\frac{1}{4}+\frac{\tau}{2}}(\boldsymbol{\widetilde{\theta}}_{t}-\boldsymbol{\theta})^{\prime}T^{-\frac{1}{2}-\frac{\tau}{2}}\mathbf{w}_{t}T^{\frac{1}{2}+\frac{\tau}{2}}(\boldsymbol{\widetilde{\alpha}}_{t}-\boldsymbol{\alpha})^{\prime}\overline{\mathbf{e}}_{r,t}^{0}\right|

\displaystyle\leq T^{-\frac{1}{4}}\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq k_{0}+m_{0}-1}\big\|T^{\frac{1}{2}+\frac{\tau}{2}}(\boldsymbol{\widetilde{\alpha}}_{t}-\boldsymbol{\alpha})\big\|\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq k_{0}+m_{0}-1}\big\|T^{\frac{1}{4}+\frac{\tau}{2}}(\boldsymbol{\widetilde{\theta}}_{t}-\boldsymbol{\theta})\big\|\left|\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{t=k_{0}}^{k_{0}+m_{0}-1}T^{-\frac{1}{2}-\tau}\mathbf{w}_{t}\overline{\mathbf{e}}_{r,t}^{0\prime}\right|

\displaystyle\leq T^{-\frac{1}{4}}\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq k_{0}+m_{0}-1}\big\|T^{\frac{1}{2}+\frac{\tau}{2}}(\boldsymbol{\widetilde{\alpha}}_{t}-\boldsymbol{\alpha})\big\|\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq k_{0}+m_{0}-1}\big\|T^{\frac{1}{4}+\frac{\tau}{2}}(\boldsymbol{\widetilde{\theta}}_{t}-\boldsymbol{\theta})\big\|\sqrt{\frac{T}{n}}\frac{1}{T^{1+\tau}}\sum_{t=k_{0}}^{k_{0}+m_{0}-1}\big\|\mathbf{w}_{t}\big\|\big\|\overline{\mathbf{e}}_{r,t}^{0}\big\|

\displaystyle\leq T^{-\frac{1}{4}-\frac{\tau}{2}}\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq k_{0}+m_{0}-1}\big\|T^{\frac{1}{2}+\frac{\tau}{2}}(\boldsymbol{\widetilde{\alpha}}_{t}-\boldsymbol{\alpha})\big\|\times\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq k_{0}+m_{0}-1}\big\|T^{\frac{1}{4}+\frac{\tau}{2}}(\boldsymbol{\widetilde{\theta}}_{t}-\boldsymbol{\theta})\big\|\sqrt{\frac{T}{n}}\left(\frac{1}{T^{1+\tau}}\sum_{t=k_{0}}^{k_{0}+m_{0}-1}\big\|\mathbf{w}_{t}\big\|^{2}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\times\left(\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=k_{0}}^{k_{0}+m_{0}-1}\big\|\overline{\mathbf{e}}_{r,t}^{0}\big\|^{2}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle=O_{p}(N^{-\frac{1}{2}}T^{-\frac{1}{4}-\frac{\tau}{2}}).

(A.89)

It remains to look at the first term, and to obtain an order result sufficient for our analysis, we cannot use rough bounds straight away. By letting
\mathbf{R}_{T}=T^{1/4}\mathbf{D}_{T}^{-1}=\mathrm{diag}(\mathbf{0}_{r\times r},\mathbf{I}_{m-r},\mathbf{0}_{q\times 1})+O(T^{-\tau/2})=O(1)
, we have

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{t=k_{0}}^{k_{0}+m_{0}-1}(\boldsymbol{\widetilde{\theta}}_{t}-\boldsymbol{\theta})^{\prime}\mathbf{w}_{t}\widehat{\mathbf{z}}_{t}^{0\prime}(\mathbf{Q}_{N}^{-1}\boldsymbol{\widehat{\delta}}_{t}-\boldsymbol{\widetilde{\delta}}_{t}^{0})

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{t=k_{0}}^{k_{0}+m_{0}-1}T^{\frac{1}{4}+\frac{\tau}{2}}(\boldsymbol{\widetilde{\theta}}_{t}-\boldsymbol{\theta})^{\prime}T^{-\frac{1}{4}-\frac{\tau}{2}}\mathbf{w}_{t}\widehat{\mathbf{z}}_{t}^{0\prime}\mathbf{D}_{T}^{-1}\mathbf{D}_{T}(\mathbf{Q}_{N}^{-1}\boldsymbol{\widehat{\delta}}_{t}-\boldsymbol{\widetilde{\delta}}_{t}^{0})

\displaystyle=\sqrt{\frac{T}{n}}\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=k_{0}}^{k_{0}+m_{0}-1}T^{\frac{1}{4}+\frac{\tau}{2}}(\boldsymbol{\widetilde{\theta}}_{t}-\boldsymbol{\theta})^{\prime}T^{-\tau}\mathbf{w}_{t}\widehat{\mathbf{z}}_{t}^{0\prime}\mathbf{R}_{T}T^{\tau/2}\mathbf{D}_{T}(\mathbf{Q}_{N}^{-1}\boldsymbol{\widehat{\delta}}_{t}-\boldsymbol{\widetilde{\delta}}_{t}^{0})

\displaystyle=\sqrt{\frac{T}{n}}\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=k_{0}}^{k_{0}+m_{0}-1}\left(\mathbf{R}_{T}T^{\tau/2}\mathbf{D}_{T}(\mathbf{Q}_{N}^{-1}\boldsymbol{\widehat{\delta}}_{t}-\boldsymbol{\widetilde{\delta}}_{t}^{0})\otimes T^{\frac{1}{4}+\frac{\tau}{2}}(\boldsymbol{\widetilde{\theta}}_{t}-\boldsymbol{\theta})\right)^{\prime}\mathrm{vec}\left(T^{-\tau}\mathbf{w}_{t}\widehat{\mathbf{z}}_{t}^{0\prime}\right)

\displaystyle=\sqrt{\frac{T}{n}}\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=k_{0}}^{k_{0}+m_{0}-1}\left(T^{\tau/2-1/4}\mathbf{v}_{t}^{0}\otimes T^{\frac{1}{4}+\frac{\tau}{2}}(\boldsymbol{\widetilde{\theta}}_{t}-\boldsymbol{\theta})\right)^{\prime}\mathrm{vec}\left(T^{-\tau}\mathbf{w}_{t}\widehat{\mathbf{z}}_{t}^{0\prime}\right)

\displaystyle+O_{p}(N^{-1/2})+O_{p}(T^{(\tau-1)/2}).

(A.90)

This simplification requires an additional explanation. Here, we use our Lemma 1, where we find that
\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq k_{0}+m_{0}-1}\left\|\mathbf{D}_{T}(\mathbf{Q}_{N}^{-1}\boldsymbol{\widehat{\delta}}_{t}-\boldsymbol{\widetilde{\delta}}_{t}^{0})\right\|=O_{p}(N^{-1/2}T^{-\tau/2})+O_{p}(T^{-1/4})
. Alternatively, this can be represented as

\displaystyle\mathbf{D}_{T}(\mathbf{Q}_{N}^{-1}\boldsymbol{\widehat{\delta}}_{t}-\boldsymbol{\widetilde{\delta}}_{t}^{0})

\displaystyle=[\mathbf{0}_{r}^{\prime},T^{-1/4}\mathbf{v}_{t}^{\prime},\mathbf{0}_{q}^{\prime}]^{\prime}+O_{p}(N^{-1/2}T^{-\tau/2})+O_{p}(T^{-1/2})

(A.91)

\displaystyle=T^{-1/4}\mathbf{v}_{t}^{0}+O_{p}(N^{-1/2}T^{-\tau/2})++O_{p}(T^{-1/2}),

where indeed the slowest decaying (
O_{p}(T^{-1/4})
) term is
T^{-1/4}\mathbf{v}_{t}
, and the second slowest term is
O_{p}(T^{-1/2})
, as per thorough inspection of Lemma 1. Therefore,

\displaystyle T^{\tau/2}\mathbf{D}_{T}(\mathbf{Q}_{N}^{-1}\boldsymbol{\widehat{\delta}}_{t}-\boldsymbol{\widetilde{\delta}}_{t}^{0})=T^{\tau/2-1/4}\mathbf{v}_{t}^{0}+O_{p}(N^{-1/2})+O_{p}(T^{(\tau-1)/2}).

(A.92)

Eventually, by using the structure of
\mathbf{R}_{T}
, we have that

\displaystyle T^{\tau/2}\mathbf{R}_{T}\mathbf{D}_{T}(\mathbf{Q}_{N}^{-1}\boldsymbol{\widehat{\delta}}_{t}-\boldsymbol{\widetilde{\delta}}_{t}^{0})=T^{\tau/2-1/4}\mathbf{v}_{t}^{0}+O_{p}(N^{-1/2})+O_{p}(T^{(\tau-1)/2}),

(A.93)

as well. We can implement this substitution, because
\left\|T^{-\tau}\mathbf{w}_{t}\widehat{\mathbf{z}}_{t}^{0\prime}\right\|=O_{p}(1)
uniformly in
t
, according to Lemma 3.1 of
Magdalinos and Phillips (2009)
, and also
\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq k_{0}+m_{0}-1}\left\|T^{\frac{1}{4}+\frac{\tau}{2}}(\boldsymbol{\widetilde{\theta}}_{t}-\boldsymbol{\theta})\right\|=O_{p}(1)
. Therefore, we obtain

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{t=k_{0}}^{k_{0}+m_{0}-1}(\boldsymbol{\widetilde{\theta}}_{t}-\boldsymbol{\theta})^{\prime}\mathbf{w}_{t}\widehat{\mathbf{z}}_{t}^{0\prime}(\mathbf{Q}_{N}^{-1}\boldsymbol{\widehat{\delta}}_{t}-\boldsymbol{\widetilde{\delta}}_{t}^{0})

\displaystyle=\sqrt{\frac{T}{n}}\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=k_{0}}^{k_{0}+m_{0}-1}\left(T^{\tau/2-1/4}\mathbf{v}_{t}^{0}\otimes T^{\frac{1}{4}+\frac{\tau}{2}}(\boldsymbol{\widetilde{\theta}}_{t}-\boldsymbol{\theta})\right)^{\prime}\mathrm{vec}\left(T^{-\tau}\mathbf{w}_{t}\widehat{\mathbf{z}}_{t}^{0\prime}\right)+O_{p}(N^{-1/2})+O_{p}(T^{(\tau-1)/2})

\displaystyle=\sqrt{\frac{T}{n}}\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=k_{0}}^{k_{0}+m_{0}-1}\left(T^{\tau/2-1/4}\mathbf{v}_{t}^{0}\otimes T^{\frac{1}{4}+\frac{\tau}{2}}(\boldsymbol{\widetilde{\theta}}_{t}-\boldsymbol{\theta})\right)^{\prime}\mathrm{vec}\left(T^{-\tau}\mathbf{w}_{t}\mathbf{z}_{t}^{0\prime}\right)

\displaystyle+\sqrt{\frac{T}{n}}\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=k_{0}}^{k_{0}+m_{0}-1}\left(T^{\tau/2-1/4}\mathbf{v}_{t}^{0}\otimes T^{\frac{1}{4}+\frac{\tau}{2}}(\boldsymbol{\widetilde{\theta}}_{t}-\boldsymbol{\theta})\right)^{\prime}\mathrm{vec}\left(T^{-\tau}\mathbf{w}_{t}\widehat{\mathbf{z}}_{t}^{0\prime}-T^{-\tau}\mathbf{w}_{t}\mathbf{z}_{t}^{0\prime}\right)+O_{p}(N^{-1/2})+O_{p}(T^{(\tau-1)/2})

\displaystyle=\sqrt{\frac{T}{n}}\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=k_{0}}^{k_{0}+m_{0}-1}\left(T^{\tau/2-1/4}\mathbf{v}_{t}^{0}\otimes T^{\frac{1}{4}+\frac{\tau}{2}}(\boldsymbol{\widetilde{\theta}}_{t}-\boldsymbol{\theta})\right)^{\prime}\mathrm{vec}\left(T^{-\tau}\mathbf{w}_{t}\mathbf{z}_{t}^{0\prime}\right)+O_{p}(N^{-1/2})+O_{p}(T^{(\tau-1)/2})+O_{p}(T^{-1/4})

\displaystyle=T^{\tau/2-1/4}\sqrt{\frac{T}{n}}\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=k_{0}}^{k_{0}+m_{0}-1}T^{\frac{1}{4}+\frac{\tau}{2}}(\boldsymbol{\widetilde{\theta}}_{t}-\boldsymbol{\theta})^{\prime}T^{-\tau}\mathbf{w}_{t}\mathbf{z}_{t}^{0\prime}\mathbf{v}_{t}^{0}+O_{p}(N^{-1/2})+O_{p}(T^{(\tau-1)/2})+O_{p}(T^{-1/4})

\displaystyle=O_{p}(N^{-1/2})++O_{p}(T^{(\tau-1)/2})+O_{p}(T^{-1/4}),

(A.94)

because
\mathbf{z}_{t}^{0\prime}\mathbf{v}_{t}^{0}=0
algebraically. Notice that

\displaystyle\left|\sqrt{\frac{T}{n}}\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=k_{0}}^{k_{0}+m_{0}-1}\left(T^{\tau/2-1/4}\mathbf{v}_{t}^{0}\otimes T^{\frac{1}{4}+\frac{\tau}{2}}(\boldsymbol{\widetilde{\theta}}_{t}-\boldsymbol{\theta})\right)^{\prime}\mathrm{vec}\left(T^{-\tau}\mathbf{w}_{t}\widehat{\mathbf{z}}_{t}^{0\prime}-T^{-\tau}\mathbf{w}_{t}\mathbf{z}_{t}^{0\prime}\right)\right|

\displaystyle\leq T^{\tau/2-1/4}\underbrace{\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq k_{0}+m_{0}-1}\left\|T^{-\tau}\mathbf{w}_{t}\widehat{\mathbf{z}}_{t}^{0\prime}-T^{-\tau}\mathbf{w}_{t}\mathbf{z}_{t}^{0\prime}\right\|}_{O_{p}(T^{-\tau/2})}\sqrt{\frac{T}{n}}\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=k_{0}}^{k_{0}+m_{0}-1}\left\|\left(\mathbf{v}_{t}^{0}\otimes T^{\frac{1}{4}+\frac{\tau}{2}}(\boldsymbol{\widetilde{\theta}}_{t}-\boldsymbol{\theta})\right)\right\|

\displaystyle=O_{p}(T^{-1/4}),

(A.95)

since
T^{-\tau/2}\widehat{\mathbf{z}}_{t}^{0}=T^{-\tau/2}\mathbf{z}_{t}^{0}+O_{p}(T^{-\tau/2})
uniformly in
t
by Lemma 3.1 in
Magdalinos and Phillips (2009)
, because the idiosyncratic component is stationary. Hence,
\mathrm{\mathbf{I}V}
reduces to

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{t=k_{0}}^{k_{0}+m_{0}-1}(\boldsymbol{\widetilde{\theta}}_{t}-\boldsymbol{\theta})^{\prime}\mathbf{w}_{t}(\widetilde{u}_{2,t+1}-\widehat{u}_{2,t+1})=O_{p}(N^{-\frac{1}{2}})+O_{p}(T^{(\tau-1)/2})+O_{p}(T^{-\frac{1}{4}}),

(A.96)

which is negligible for all
\tau\in(0,1)
. Finally, the order of
\mathrm{\mathbf{V}}
can be obtained by employing an argument similar
\mathrm{\mathbf{I}V}
. That is,

\displaystyle\left|\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{t=k_{0}}^{k_{0}+m_{0}-1}\boldsymbol{\alpha}^{\prime}\mathrm{\mathbf{f}}_{t}(\widetilde{u}_{2,t+1}-\widehat{u}_{2,t+1})\right|

\displaystyle=\left|\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{t=k_{0}}^{k_{0}+m_{0}-1}\boldsymbol{\alpha}^{\prime}\mathrm{\mathbf{f}}_{t}(\mathbf{Q}_{N}^{-1}\boldsymbol{\widehat{\delta}}_{t}-\boldsymbol{\widetilde{\delta}}_{t}^{0})^{\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{z}}_{t}^{0}\right|+\left|\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{t=k_{0}}^{k_{0}+m_{0}-1}\boldsymbol{\alpha}^{\prime}\mathrm{\mathbf{f}}_{t}(\boldsymbol{\widetilde{\alpha}}_{t}-\boldsymbol{\alpha})^{\prime}\overline{\mathbf{e}}_{r,t}^{0}\right|

\displaystyle+\left|\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{t=k_{0}}^{k_{0}+m_{0}-1}\boldsymbol{\alpha}^{\prime}\mathrm{\mathbf{f}}_{t}\boldsymbol{\alpha}^{\prime}\overline{\mathbf{e}}_{r,t}^{0}\right|

\displaystyle=O_{p}(N^{-\frac{1}{2}})+O_{p}(T^{(\tau-1)/2})+O_{p}(T^{-\frac{1}{4}}).

(A.97)

To derive this order, again, we need more work than relying on rough bounds. Notice that we can re-write the first term, because it is a scalar. By letting
\mathbf{R}_{T}=T^{1/4}\mathbf{D}_{T}^{-1}
, we obtain

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{t=k_{0}}^{k_{0}+m_{0}-1}\boldsymbol{\alpha}^{\prime}\mathrm{\mathbf{f}}_{t}(\mathbf{Q}_{N}^{-1}\boldsymbol{\widehat{\delta}}_{t}-\boldsymbol{\widetilde{\delta}}_{t}^{0})^{\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{z}}_{t}^{0}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{t=k_{0}}^{k_{0}+m_{0}-1}(\mathbf{Q}_{N}^{-1}\boldsymbol{\widehat{\delta}}_{t}-\boldsymbol{\widetilde{\delta}}_{t}^{0})^{\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{z}}_{t}^{0}\mathbf{f}_{t}^{\prime}\boldsymbol{\alpha}

\displaystyle=T^{-\frac{1}{4}-\frac{\tau}{2}}\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{t=k_{0}}^{k_{0}+m_{0}-1}(\mathbf{Q}_{N}^{-1}\boldsymbol{\widehat{\delta}}_{t}-\boldsymbol{\widetilde{\delta}}_{t}^{0})^{\prime}\mathbf{D}_{T}\mathbf{D}_{T}^{-1}\widehat{\mathbf{z}}_{t}^{0}\mathbf{f}_{t}^{\prime}\boldsymbol{\alpha}^{0}

\displaystyle=T^{-1/2}T^{-\tau/2}\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{t=k_{0}}^{k_{0}+m_{0}-1}(\mathbf{Q}_{N}^{-1}\boldsymbol{\widehat{\delta}}_{t}-\boldsymbol{\widetilde{\delta}}_{t}^{0})^{\prime}\mathbf{D}_{T}\mathbf{R}_{T}\widehat{\mathbf{z}}_{t}^{0}\mathbf{f}_{t}^{\prime}\boldsymbol{\alpha}^{0}

\displaystyle=\sqrt{\frac{T}{n}}\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=k_{0}}^{k_{0}+m_{0}-1}\left(\boldsymbol{\alpha}^{0}\otimes T^{\tau/2}(\mathbf{Q}_{N}^{-1}\boldsymbol{\widehat{\delta}}_{t}-\boldsymbol{\widetilde{\delta}}_{t}^{0})^{\prime}\mathbf{D}_{T}\mathbf{R}_{T}\right)^{\prime}\mathrm{vec}\left(T^{-\tau}\widehat{\mathbf{z}}_{t}^{0}\mathbf{f}_{t}^{\prime}\right)

\displaystyle=\sqrt{\frac{T}{n}}\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=k_{0}}^{k_{0}+m_{0}-1}\left(\boldsymbol{\alpha}^{0}\otimes T^{\tau/2-1/4}\mathbf{v}_{t}^{0}\right)^{\prime}\mathrm{vec}\left(T^{-\tau}\widehat{\mathbf{z}}_{t}^{0}\mathbf{f}_{t}^{\prime}\right)+O_{p}(N^{-1/2})+O_{p}(T^{(\tau-1)/2}),

(A.98)

where
\left\|T^{-\tau}\widehat{\mathbf{z}}_{t}^{0}\mathbf{f}_{t}^{\prime}\right\|=O_{p}(1)
uniformly in
t
by Lemma 3.1 in
Magdalinos and Phillips (2009)
. Again, we used
\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq k_{0}+m_{0}-1}\left\|\mathbf{D}_{T}(\mathbf{Q}_{N}^{-1}\boldsymbol{\widehat{\delta}}_{t}-\boldsymbol{\widetilde{\delta}}_{t}^{0})\right\|=O_{p}(N^{-1/2}T^{-\tau/2})+O_{p}(T^{-1/4})
to uncover the leading term, and
\mathbf{R}_{T}=\mathrm{diag}(\mathbf{0}_{r\times r},\mathbf{I}_{m-r},\mathbf{0}_{q\times q})+O(T^{-\tau/2})
. Therefore,

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{t=k_{0}}^{k_{0}+m_{0}-1}\boldsymbol{\alpha}^{\prime}\mathrm{\mathbf{f}}_{t}(\mathbf{Q}_{N}^{-1}\boldsymbol{\widehat{\delta}}_{t}-\boldsymbol{\widetilde{\delta}}_{t}^{0})^{\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{z}}_{t}^{0}

\displaystyle=\sqrt{\frac{T}{n}}\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=k_{0}}^{k_{0}+m_{0}-1}\left(\boldsymbol{\alpha}^{0}\otimes T^{\tau/2-1/4}\mathbf{v}_{t}^{0}\right)^{\prime}\mathrm{vec}\left(T^{-\tau}\widehat{\mathbf{z}}_{t}^{0}\mathbf{f}_{t}^{\prime}\right)+O_{p}(N^{-1/2})+O_{p}(T^{(\tau-1)/2})

\displaystyle=\sqrt{\frac{T}{n}}\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=k_{0}}^{k_{0}+m_{0}-1}\left(\boldsymbol{\alpha}^{0}\otimes T^{\tau/2-1/4}\mathbf{v}_{t}^{0}\right)^{\prime}\mathrm{vec}\left(T^{-\tau}\mathbf{z}_{t}^{0}\mathbf{f}_{t}^{\prime}\right)

\displaystyle+\sqrt{\frac{T}{n}}\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=k_{0}}^{k_{0}+m_{0}-1}\left(\boldsymbol{\alpha}^{0}\otimes T^{\tau/2-1/4}\mathbf{v}_{t}^{0}\right)^{\prime}\mathrm{vec}\left(T^{-\tau}\widehat{\mathbf{z}}_{t}^{0}\mathbf{f}_{t}^{\prime}-T^{-\tau}\mathbf{z}_{t}^{0}\mathbf{f}_{t}^{\prime}\right)+O_{p}(N^{-1/2})+O_{p}(T^{(\tau-1)/2})

\displaystyle=\sqrt{\frac{T}{n}}\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=k_{0}}^{k_{0}+m_{0}-1}\left(\boldsymbol{\alpha}^{0}\otimes T^{\tau/2-1/4}\mathbf{v}_{t}^{0}\right)^{\prime}\mathrm{vec}\left(T^{-\tau}\mathbf{z}_{t}^{0}\mathbf{f}_{t}^{\prime}\right)+O_{p}(N^{-1/2})+O_{p}(T^{(\tau-1)/2})+O_{p}(T^{-1/4})

\displaystyle=T^{\tau/2-1/4}\sqrt{\frac{T}{n}}\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=k_{0}}^{k_{0}+m_{0}-1}\mathbf{v}_{t}^{0\prime}T^{-\tau}\mathbf{z}_{t}^{0}\mathbf{f}_{t}^{\prime}\boldsymbol{\alpha}+O_{p}(N^{-1/2})+O_{p}(T^{(\tau-1)/2})+O_{p}(T^{-1/4})

\displaystyle=O_{p}(N^{-1/2})+O_{p}(T^{(\tau-1)/2})+O_{p}(T^{-1/4}),

(A.99)

because, again
\mathbf{v}_{t}^{0\prime}\mathbf{z}_{t}^{0}=0
algebraically, and which is negligible for all
\tau\in(0,1)
. The term
O_{p}(T^{-1/4})
is obtained from

\displaystyle\left|\sqrt{\frac{T}{n}}\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=k_{0}}^{k_{0}+m_{0}-1}\left(\boldsymbol{\alpha}^{0}\otimes T^{\tau/2-1/4}\mathbf{v}_{t}^{0}\right)^{\prime}\mathrm{vec}\left(T^{-\tau}\widehat{\mathbf{z}}_{t}^{0}\mathbf{f}_{t}^{\prime}-T^{-\tau}\mathbf{z}_{t}^{0}\mathbf{f}_{t}^{\prime}\right)\right|

\displaystyle\leq T^{\tau/2-1/4}\underbrace{\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq k_{0}+m_{0}-1}\left\|T^{-\tau}\widehat{\mathbf{z}}_{t}^{0}\mathbf{f}_{t}^{\prime}-T^{-\tau}\mathbf{z}_{t}^{0}\mathbf{f}_{t}^{\prime}\right\|}_{O_{p}(T^{-\tau/2})}\sqrt{\frac{T}{n}}\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=k_{0}}^{k_{0}+m_{0}-1}\left\|\left(\boldsymbol{\alpha}^{0}\otimes\mathbf{v}_{t}^{0}\right)\right\|=O_{p}(T^{-1/4}),

(A.100)

as expected from the fact that
T^{-\tau/2}\widehat{\mathbf{z}}^{0}_{t}=T^{-\tau/2}\mathbf{z}_{t}^{0}+O_{p}(T^{-\tau/2})
uniformly in
t
. The remaining components follow:

\displaystyle\left|\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{t=k_{0}}^{k_{0}+m_{0}-1}\boldsymbol{\alpha}^{\prime}\mathrm{\mathbf{f}}_{t}(\boldsymbol{\widetilde{\alpha}}_{t}-\boldsymbol{\alpha})^{\prime}\overline{\mathbf{e}}_{r,t}^{0}\right|

\displaystyle=\left|\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{t=k_{0}}^{k_{0}+m_{0}-1}T^{\frac{1}{4}+\frac{\tau}{2}}\boldsymbol{\alpha}^{\prime}T^{-\frac{3}{4}-\tau}\mathrm{\mathbf{f}}_{t}T^{\frac{1}{2}+\frac{\tau}{2}}(\boldsymbol{\widetilde{\alpha}}_{t}-\boldsymbol{\alpha})^{\prime}\overline{\mathbf{e}}_{r,t}^{0\prime}\right|

\displaystyle\leq\big\|\boldsymbol{\alpha}^{0}\big\|\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq k_{0}+m_{0}-1}\big\|T^{\frac{1}{2}+\frac{\tau}{2}}(\boldsymbol{\widetilde{\alpha}}_{t}-\boldsymbol{\alpha})\big\|\left|\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{t=k_{0}}^{k_{0}+m_{0}-1}T^{-\frac{3}{4}-\tau}\mathrm{\mathbf{f}}_{t}\overline{\mathbf{e}}_{r,t}^{0}\right|

\displaystyle\leq T^{-\frac{1}{4}}\big\|\boldsymbol{\alpha}^{0}\big\|\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq k_{0}+m_{0}-1}\big\|T^{\frac{1}{2}+\frac{\tau}{2}}(\boldsymbol{\widetilde{\alpha}}_{t}-\boldsymbol{\alpha})\big\|\sqrt{\frac{T}{n}}\frac{1}{T^{1+\tau}}\sum_{t=k_{0}}^{k_{0}+m_{0}-1}\big\|\mathrm{\mathbf{f}}_{t}\big\|\big\|\overline{\mathbf{e}}_{r,t}^{0}\big\|

\displaystyle\leq T^{-\frac{1}{4}-\frac{\tau}{2}}\big\|\boldsymbol{\alpha}^{0}\big\|\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq k_{0}+m_{0}-1}\big\|T^{\frac{1}{2}+\frac{\tau}{2}}(\boldsymbol{\widetilde{\alpha}}_{t}-\boldsymbol{\alpha})\big\|\sqrt{\frac{T}{n}}\left(\frac{1}{T^{1+\tau}}\sum_{t=k_{0}}^{k_{0}+m_{0}-1}\big\|\mathrm{\mathbf{f}}_{t}\big\|^{2}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\left(\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=k_{0}}^{k_{0}+m_{0}-1}\big\|\overline{\mathbf{e}}_{r,t}^{0}\big\|^{2}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle=O_{p}(N^{-\frac{1}{2}}T^{-\frac{1}{4}-\frac{\tau}{2}}),

(A.101)

and

\displaystyle\left|\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{t=k_{0}}^{k_{0}+m_{0}-1}\boldsymbol{\alpha}^{\prime}\mathrm{\mathbf{f}}_{t}\boldsymbol{\alpha}^{\prime}\overline{\mathbf{e}}_{r,t}^{0}\right|

\displaystyle=\left|\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{t=k_{0}}^{k_{0}+m_{0}-1}T^{\frac{1}{4}+\frac{\tau}{2}}\boldsymbol{\alpha}^{\prime}T^{-\frac{1}{2}-\tau}\mathrm{\mathbf{f}}_{t}T^{\frac{1}{4}+\frac{\tau}{2}}\boldsymbol{\alpha}^{\prime}\overline{\mathbf{e}}_{r,t}^{0}\right|

\displaystyle\leq\big\|\boldsymbol{\alpha}^{0}\big\|^{2}\left|\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{t=k_{0}}^{k_{0}+m_{0}-1}T^{-\frac{1}{2}-\tau}\mathrm{\mathbf{f}}_{t}\overline{\mathbf{e}}_{r,t}^{0}\right|

\displaystyle\leq\big\|\boldsymbol{\alpha}^{0}\big\|^{2}\sqrt{\frac{T}{n}}\frac{1}{T^{1+\tau}}\sum_{t=k_{0}}^{k_{0}+m_{0}-1}\big\|\mathrm{\mathbf{f}}_{t}\big\|\big\|\overline{\mathbf{e}}_{r,t}^{0}\big\|

\displaystyle\leq T^{-\frac{\tau}{2}}\big\|\boldsymbol{\alpha}^{0}\big\|^{2}\sqrt{\frac{T}{n}}\left(\frac{1}{T^{1+\tau}}\sum_{t=k_{0}}^{k_{0}+m_{0}-1}\big\|\mathrm{\mathbf{f}}_{t}\big\|^{2}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\left(\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=k_{0}}^{k_{0}+m_{0}-1}\big\|\overline{\mathbf{e}}_{r,t}^{0}\big\|^{2}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle=O_{p}(N^{-\frac{1}{2}}T^{-\frac{\tau}{2}}).

(A.102)

Therefore, combining the orders of
\mathrm{\mathbf{I}}
-
\mathrm{\mathbf{V}}
gives

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{t=k_{0}}^{k_{0}+m_{0}-1}\widetilde{u}_{1,t+1}(\widetilde{u}_{2,t+1}-\widehat{u}_{2,t+1})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle O_{p}(N^{-\frac{1}{2}})++O_{p}(T^{(\tau-1)/2})+O_{p}(T^{-\frac{1}{4}}).

(A.103)

which is negligible for all
\tau\in\left(0,1\right)
.

Now, let us turn our attention to Lemma 2 (b) by recalling that
\widetilde{u}_{2,t+1}-\widehat{u}_{2,t+1}=(\mathbf{Q}_{N}^{-1}\boldsymbol{\widehat{\delta}}_{t}-\boldsymbol{\widetilde{\delta}}_{t}^{0})^{\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{z}}_{t}^{0}+(\boldsymbol{\widetilde{\alpha}}_{t}-\boldsymbol{\alpha})^{\prime}\overline{\mathbf{e}}_{r,t}^{0}+\boldsymbol{\alpha}^{\prime}\overline{\mathbf{e}}_{r,t}^{0}
and proceeding as follows:

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{t=k_{0}}^{k_{0}+l_{2}^{0}-1}(\widetilde{u}_{2,t+1}-\widehat{u}_{2,t+1})^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{3}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{t=k_{0}}^{k_{0}+m_{0}-1}((\mathbf{Q}_{N}^{-1}\boldsymbol{\widehat{\delta}}_{t}-\boldsymbol{\widetilde{\delta}}_{t}^{0})^{\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{z}}_{t}^{0})^{2}+\frac{3}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{t=k_{0}}^{k_{0}+m_{0}-1}((\boldsymbol{\widetilde{\alpha}}_{t}-\boldsymbol{\alpha})^{\prime}\overline{\mathbf{e}}_{r,t}^{0})^{2}

(A.104)

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\frac{3}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{t=k_{0}}^{k_{0}+m_{0}-1}(\boldsymbol{\alpha}^{\prime}\overline{\mathbf{e}}_{r,t}^{0})^{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle 3(U_{1}+U_{2}+U_{3}).

Starting from
U_{1}
, and letting
\mathbf{r}_{t}=O_{p}(N^{-1/2})+O_{p}(T^{(\tau-1)/2})
for short-hand notation, we obtain

\displaystyle U_{1}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{t=k_{0}}^{k_{0}+l_{2}^{0}-1}((\mathbf{Q}_{N}^{-1}\boldsymbol{\widehat{\delta}}_{t}-\boldsymbol{\widetilde{\delta}}_{t}^{0})^{\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{z}}_{t}^{0})^{2}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{t=k_{0}}^{k_{0}+l_{2}^{0}-1}((\mathbf{Q}_{N}^{-1}\boldsymbol{\widehat{\delta}}_{t}-\boldsymbol{\widetilde{\delta}}_{t}^{0})^{\prime}\mathbf{D}_{T}\mathbf{D}_{T}^{-1}\widehat{\mathbf{z}}_{t}^{0})^{2}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{t=k_{0}}^{k_{0}+l_{2}^{0}-1}\left(\mathbf{D}_{T}(\mathbf{Q}_{N}^{-1}\boldsymbol{\widehat{\delta}}_{t}-\boldsymbol{\widetilde{\delta}}_{t}^{0})\otimes\mathbf{D}_{T}(\mathbf{Q}_{N}^{-1}\boldsymbol{\widehat{\delta}}_{t}-\boldsymbol{\widetilde{\delta}}_{t}^{0})\right)^{\prime}\mathrm{vec}(\mathbf{D}_{T}^{-1}\widehat{\mathbf{z}}_{t}^{0}\widehat{\mathbf{z}}_{t}^{0\prime}\mathbf{D}_{T}^{-1})

\displaystyle=\sqrt{\frac{T}{n}}\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=k_{0}}^{k_{0}+l_{2}^{0}-1}\left(T^{\tau/2}\mathbf{D}_{T}(\mathbf{Q}_{N}^{-1}\boldsymbol{\widehat{\delta}}_{t}-\boldsymbol{\widetilde{\delta}}_{t}^{0})\otimes T^{\tau/2}\mathbf{D}_{T}(\mathbf{Q}_{N}^{-1}\boldsymbol{\widehat{\delta}}_{t}-\boldsymbol{\widetilde{\delta}}_{t}^{0})\right)^{\prime}\left(\mathbf{R}_{T}\otimes\mathbf{R}_{T}\right)

\displaystyle\times\mathrm{vec}\left(T^{-\tau}\widehat{\mathbf{z}}_{t}^{0}\widehat{\mathbf{z}}_{t}^{0\prime}\right)

\displaystyle=\sqrt{\frac{T}{n}}\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=k_{0}}^{k_{0}+l_{2}^{0}-1}\left(T^{\tau/2-1/4}\mathbf{v}_{t}^{0}\otimes T^{\tau/2}\mathbf{D}_{T}(\mathbf{Q}_{N}^{-1}\boldsymbol{\widehat{\delta}}_{t}-\boldsymbol{\widetilde{\delta}}_{t}^{0})\right)^{\prime}\left(\mathbf{R}_{T}\otimes\mathbf{R}_{T}\right)\mathrm{vec}\left(T^{-\tau}\widehat{\mathbf{z}}_{t}^{0}\widehat{\mathbf{z}}_{t}^{0\prime}\right)

\displaystyle+\sqrt{\frac{T}{n}}\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=k_{0}}^{k_{0}+l_{2}^{0}-1}\left(\mathbf{r}_{t}\otimes T^{\tau/2}\mathbf{D}_{T}(\mathbf{Q}_{N}^{-1}\boldsymbol{\widehat{\delta}}_{t}-\boldsymbol{\widetilde{\delta}}_{t}^{0})\right)^{\prime}\left(\mathbf{R}_{T}\otimes\mathbf{R}_{T}\right)\times\mathrm{vec}\left(T^{-\tau}\widehat{\mathbf{z}}_{t}^{0}\widehat{\mathbf{z}}_{t}^{0\prime}\right)

\displaystyle=\sqrt{\frac{T}{n}}\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=k_{0}}^{k_{0}+l_{2}^{0}-1}\left(T^{\tau/2-1/4}\mathbf{v}_{t}^{0}\otimes T^{\tau/2}\mathbf{D}_{T}(\mathbf{Q}_{N}^{-1}\boldsymbol{\widehat{\delta}}_{t}-\boldsymbol{\widetilde{\delta}}_{t}^{0})\right)^{\prime}\left(\mathbf{R}_{T}\otimes\mathbf{R}_{T}\right)\mathrm{vec}\left(T^{-\tau}\widehat{\mathbf{z}}_{t}^{0}\widehat{\mathbf{z}}_{t}^{0\prime}\right)

\displaystyle+\sqrt{\frac{T}{n}}\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=k_{0}}^{k_{0}+l_{2}^{0}-1}\left(\mathbf{r}_{t}\otimes T^{\tau/2}\mathbf{D}_{T}(\mathbf{Q}_{N}^{-1}\boldsymbol{\widehat{\delta}}_{t}-\boldsymbol{\widetilde{\delta}}_{t}^{0})\right)^{\prime}\left(\mathbf{R}_{T}\otimes\mathbf{R}_{T}\right)\times\mathrm{vec}\left(T^{-\tau}\mathbf{z}_{t}^{0}\mathbf{z}_{t}^{0\prime}\right)

\displaystyle+\sqrt{\frac{T}{n}}\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=k_{0}}^{k_{0}+l_{2}^{0}-1}\left(\mathbf{r}_{t}\otimes\mathbf{D}_{T}(\mathbf{Q}_{N}^{-1}\boldsymbol{\widehat{\delta}}_{t}-\boldsymbol{\widetilde{\delta}}_{t}^{0})\right)^{\prime}\left(\mathbf{R}_{T}\otimes\mathbf{R}_{T}\right)\times T^{\tau/2}\mathrm{vec}\left(T^{-\tau}\widehat{\mathbf{z}}_{t}^{0}\widehat{\mathbf{z}}_{t}^{0\prime}-T^{-\tau}\mathbf{z}_{t}^{0}\mathbf{z}_{t}^{0\prime}\right)

\displaystyle=\sqrt{\frac{T}{n}}\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=k_{0}}^{k_{0}+l_{2}^{0}-1}\left(T^{\tau/2-1/4}\mathbf{v}_{t}^{0}\otimes T^{\tau/2}\mathbf{D}_{T}(\mathbf{Q}_{N}^{-1}\boldsymbol{\widehat{\delta}}_{t}-\boldsymbol{\widetilde{\delta}}_{t}^{0})\right)^{\prime}\left(\mathbf{R}_{T}\otimes\mathbf{R}_{T}\right)\mathrm{vec}\left(T^{-\tau}\widehat{\mathbf{z}}_{t}^{0}\widehat{\mathbf{z}}_{t}^{0\prime}\right)

\displaystyle+\sqrt{\frac{T}{n}}\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=k_{0}}^{k_{0}+l_{2}^{0}-1}\left(\mathbf{r}_{t}\otimes T^{\tau/2}\mathbf{D}_{T}(\mathbf{Q}_{N}^{-1}\boldsymbol{\widehat{\delta}}_{t}-\boldsymbol{\widetilde{\delta}}_{t}^{0})\right)^{\prime}\left(\mathbf{R}_{T}\otimes\mathbf{R}_{T}\right)\times\mathrm{vec}\left(T^{-\tau}\mathbf{z}_{t}^{0}\mathbf{z}_{t}^{0\prime}\right)

\displaystyle+O_{p}(N^{-1}T^{-\tau/2})+O_{p}((NT)^{-1/2})+O_{p}(N^{-1/2}T^{-1/4})+O_{p}(T^{\tau/2-3/4})

\displaystyle=\sqrt{\frac{T}{n}}\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=k_{0}}^{k_{0}+l_{2}^{0}-1}\left(T^{\tau/2-1/4}\mathbf{v}_{t}^{0}\otimes T^{\tau/2}\mathbf{D}_{T}(\mathbf{Q}_{N}^{-1}\boldsymbol{\widehat{\delta}}_{t}-\boldsymbol{\widetilde{\delta}}_{t}^{0})\right)^{\prime}\left(\mathbf{R}_{T}\otimes\mathbf{R}_{T}\right)\mathrm{vec}\left(T^{-\tau}\widehat{\mathbf{z}}_{t}^{0}\widehat{\mathbf{z}}_{t}^{0\prime}\right)

\displaystyle+\underbrace{\sqrt{\frac{T}{n}}\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=k_{0}}^{k_{0}+l_{2}^{0}-1}\left(\mathbf{r}_{t}\otimes T^{\tau/2-1/4}\mathbf{v}_{t}^{0}\right)^{\prime}\left(\mathbf{R}_{T}\otimes\mathbf{R}_{T}\right)\times\mathrm{vec}\left(T^{-\tau}\mathbf{z}_{t}^{0}\mathbf{z}_{t}^{0\prime}\right)}_{=0}

\displaystyle+\sqrt{\frac{T}{n}}\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=k_{0}}^{k_{0}+l_{2}^{0}-1}\left(\mathbf{r}_{t}\otimes\mathbf{r}_{t}\right)^{\prime}\left(\mathbf{R}_{T}\otimes\mathbf{R}_{T}\right)\times\mathrm{vec}\left(T^{-\tau}\mathbf{z}_{t}^{0}\mathbf{z}_{t}^{0\prime}\right)

\displaystyle+O_{p}(N^{-1}T^{-\tau/2})+O_{p}((NT)^{-1/2})+O_{p}(N^{-1/2}T^{-1/4})+O_{p}(T^{\tau/2-3/4})

\displaystyle=\sqrt{\frac{T}{n}}\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=k_{0}}^{k_{0}+l_{2}^{0}-1}\left(T^{\tau/2-1/4}\mathbf{v}_{t}^{0}\otimes T^{\tau/2}\mathbf{D}_{T}(\mathbf{Q}_{N}^{-1}\boldsymbol{\widehat{\delta}}_{t}-\boldsymbol{\widetilde{\delta}}_{t}^{0})\right)^{\prime}\left(\mathbf{R}_{T}\otimes\mathbf{R}_{T}\right)\mathrm{vec}\left(T^{-\tau}\widehat{\mathbf{z}}_{t}^{0}\widehat{\mathbf{z}}_{t}^{0\prime}\right)

\displaystyle+O_{p}(N^{-1})+O_{p}(T^{\tau-1})

\displaystyle=\sqrt{\frac{T}{n}}\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=k_{0}}^{k_{0}+l_{2}^{0}-1}\left(T^{\tau/2-1/4}\mathbf{v}_{t}^{0}\otimes T^{\tau/2-1/4}\mathbf{v}_{t}^{0}\right)^{\prime}\left(\mathbf{R}_{T}\otimes\mathbf{R}_{T}\right)\mathrm{vec}\left(T^{-\tau}\widehat{\mathbf{z}}_{t}^{0}\widehat{\mathbf{z}}_{t}^{0\prime}\right)

\displaystyle+\sqrt{\frac{T}{n}}\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=k_{0}}^{k_{0}+l_{2}^{0}-1}\left(T^{\tau/2-1/4}\mathbf{v}_{t}^{0}\otimes\mathbf{r}_{t}\right)^{\prime}\left(\mathbf{R}_{T}\otimes\mathbf{R}_{T}\right)\mathrm{vec}\left(T^{-\tau}\widehat{\mathbf{z}}_{t}^{0}\widehat{\mathbf{z}}_{t}^{0\prime}\right)+O_{p}(N^{-1})+O_{p}(T^{\tau-1})

\displaystyle=\sqrt{\frac{T}{n}}\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=k_{0}}^{k_{0}+l_{2}^{0}-1}\left(T^{\tau/2-1/4}\mathbf{v}_{t}^{0}\otimes T^{\tau/2-1/4}\mathbf{v}_{t}^{0}\right)^{\prime}\left(\mathbf{R}_{T}\otimes\mathbf{R}_{T}\right)\mathrm{vec}\left(T^{-\tau}\widehat{\mathbf{z}}_{t}^{0}\widehat{\mathbf{z}}_{t}^{0\prime}\right)

\displaystyle+\underbrace{\sqrt{\frac{T}{n}}\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=k_{0}}^{k_{0}+l_{2}^{0}-1}\left(T^{\tau/2-1/4}\mathbf{v}_{t}^{0}\otimes\mathbf{r}_{t}\right)^{\prime}\left(\mathbf{R}_{T}\otimes\mathbf{R}_{T}\right)\mathrm{vec}\left(T^{-\tau}\mathbf{z}_{t}^{0}\mathbf{z}_{t}^{0\prime}\right)}_{=0}

\displaystyle+\sqrt{\frac{T}{n}}\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=k_{0}}^{k_{0}+l_{2}^{0}-1}\left(T^{-1/4}\mathbf{v}_{t}^{0}\otimes\mathbf{r}_{t}\right)^{\prime}\left(\mathbf{R}_{T}\otimes\mathbf{R}_{T}\right)T^{\tau/2}\mathrm{vec}\left(T^{-\tau}\widehat{\mathbf{z}}_{t}^{0}\widehat{\mathbf{z}}_{t}^{0\prime}-T^{-\tau}\mathbf{z}_{t}^{0}\mathbf{z}_{t}^{0\prime}\right)

\displaystyle+O_{p}(N^{-1})+O_{p}(T^{\tau-1})

\displaystyle=\sqrt{\frac{T}{n}}\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=k_{0}}^{k_{0}+l_{2}^{0}-1}\left(T^{\tau/2-1/4}\mathbf{v}_{t}^{0}\otimes T^{\tau/2-1/4}\mathbf{v}_{t}^{0}\right)^{\prime}\left(\mathbf{R}_{T}\otimes\mathbf{R}_{T}\right)\mathrm{vec}\left(T^{-\tau}\widehat{\mathbf{z}}_{t}^{0}\widehat{\mathbf{z}}_{t}^{0\prime}\right)

\displaystyle+O_{p}(N^{-1})+O_{p}(N^{-1/2}T^{-1/4})+O_{p}(T^{\tau-1})

(A.105)

where we used the same approximation arguments as in (
A.90
) and (
A.99
) iteratively in the substitutions along the fact that
\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq k_{0}+m_{0}-1}T^{\tau/2}\left\|T^{-\tau}\widehat{\mathbf{z}}_{t}^{0}\widehat{\mathbf{z}}_{t}^{0\prime}-T^{-\tau}\mathbf{z}_{t}^{0}\mathbf{z}_{t}^{0\prime}\right\|=O_{p}(1)
, because now we use squares of the approximating terms. Subsequently,

\displaystyle U_{1}=\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{t=k_{0}}^{k_{0}+l_{2}^{0}-1}((\mathbf{Q}_{N}^{-1}\boldsymbol{\widehat{\delta}}_{t}-\boldsymbol{\widetilde{\delta}}_{t}^{0})^{\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{z}}_{t}^{0})^{2}

\displaystyle=\sqrt{\frac{T}{n}}\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=k_{0}}^{k_{0}+l_{2}^{0}-1}\left(T^{\tau/2-1/4}\mathbf{v}_{t}^{0}\otimes T^{\tau/2-1/4}\mathbf{v}_{t}^{0}\right)^{\prime}\mathrm{vec}\left(T^{-\tau}\mathbf{z}_{t}^{0}\mathbf{z}_{t}^{0\prime}\right)

\displaystyle+\sqrt{\frac{T}{n}}\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=k_{0}}^{k_{0}+l_{2}^{0}-1}\left(T^{\tau/2-1/4}\mathbf{v}_{t}^{0}\otimes T^{\tau/2-1/4}\mathbf{v}_{t}^{0}\right)^{\prime}\mathrm{vec}\left(T^{-\tau}\widehat{\mathbf{z}}_{t}^{0}\widehat{\mathbf{z}}_{t}^{0\prime}-\mathbf{z}_{t}^{0}\mathbf{z}_{t}^{0\prime}\right)

\displaystyle+O_{p}(N^{-1})+O_{p}(N^{-1/2}T^{-1/4})+O_{p}(T^{\tau-1})

\displaystyle=\sqrt{\frac{T}{n}}\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=k_{0}}^{k_{0}+l_{2}^{0}-1}\left(T^{\tau/2-1/4}\mathbf{v}_{t}^{0}\otimes T^{\tau/2-1/4}\mathbf{v}_{t}^{0}\right)^{\prime}\mathrm{vec}\left(T^{-\tau}\mathbf{z}_{t}^{0}\mathbf{z}_{t}^{0\prime}\right)+O_{p}(N^{-1})+O_{p}(N^{-1/2}T^{-1/4})+O_{p}(T^{(\tau-1)/2})

\displaystyle=T^{\tau-1/2}\sqrt{\frac{T}{n}}\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=k_{0}}^{k_{0}+l_{2}^{0}-1}\mathbf{v}_{t}^{0\prime}T^{-\tau}\mathbf{z}_{t}^{0}\mathbf{z}_{t}^{0\prime}\mathbf{v}_{t}^{0}+O_{p}(N^{-1})+O_{p}(N^{-1/2}T^{-1/4})+O_{p}(T^{(\tau-1)/2})

\displaystyle=O_{p}(N^{-1})+O_{p}(N^{-1/2}T^{-1/4})+O_{p}(T^{(\tau-1)/2}),

(A.106)

since
\mathbf{v}_{t}^{0\prime}\mathbf{z}_{t}^{0}=0
exactly. The slowest decaying term, which dominates
O_{p}(T^{\tau-1})
is obtained by

\displaystyle\left|\sqrt{\frac{T}{n}}\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=k_{0}}^{k_{0}+l_{2}^{0}-1}\left(T^{\tau/2-1/4}\mathbf{v}_{t}^{0}\otimes T^{\tau/2-1/4}\mathbf{v}_{t}^{0}\right)^{\prime}\mathrm{vec}\left(T^{-\tau}\widehat{\mathbf{z}}_{t}^{0}\widehat{\mathbf{z}}_{t}^{0\prime}-\mathbf{z}_{t}^{0}\mathbf{z}_{t}^{0\prime}\right)\right|

\displaystyle\leq T^{\tau-1/2}\underbrace{\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq k_{0}+l_{2}^{0}-1}\left\|T^{-\tau}\widehat{\mathbf{z}}_{t}^{0}\widehat{\mathbf{z}}_{t}^{0\prime}-\mathbf{z}_{t}^{0}\mathbf{z}_{t}^{0\prime}\right\|}_{O_{p}(T^{-\tau/2})}\sqrt{\frac{T}{n}}\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=k_{0}}^{k_{0}+l_{2}^{0}-1}\left\|\left(\mathbf{v}_{t}^{0}\otimes\mathbf{v}_{t}^{0}\right)\right\|

\displaystyle=O_{p}(T^{\tau/2-1/2})=o_{p}(1)

(A.107)

as required for any
\tau\in(0,1)
. Note terms of this nature can decay very slowly for
\tau\approx 1
. Next,

\displaystyle U_{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{t=k_{0}}^{k_{0}+l_{2}^{0}-1}(T^{\frac{1}{2}+\frac{\tau}{2}}(\boldsymbol{\widetilde{\alpha}}_{t}-\boldsymbol{\alpha})^{\prime}T^{-\frac{1}{2}-\frac{\tau}{2}}\overline{\mathbf{e}}_{r,t}^{0})^{2}

(A.108)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle T^{-\frac{1}{2}-\tau}\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq k_{0}+m_{0}-1}\big\|T^{\frac{1}{2}+\frac{\tau}{2}}(\boldsymbol{\widetilde{\alpha}}_{t}-\boldsymbol{\alpha})\big\|^{2}\sqrt{\frac{T}{n}}\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=k_{0}}^{k_{0}+l_{2}^{0}-1}\big\|\overline{\mathbf{e}}_{r,t}^{0}\big\|^{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle O_{p}(N^{-1}T^{-\frac{1}{2}-\tau}).

Moving on to
U_{3}
,

\displaystyle U_{3}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{t=k_{0}}^{k_{0}+l_{2}^{0}-1}(T^{\frac{1}{4}+\frac{\tau}{2}}\boldsymbol{\alpha}^{\prime}T^{-\frac{1}{4}-\frac{\tau}{2}}\overline{\mathbf{e}}_{r,t}^{0})^{2}

(A.109)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle T^{-\tau}\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq k_{0}+l_{2}^{0}-1}\big\|\boldsymbol{\alpha}^{0}\big\|^{2}\sqrt{\frac{T}{n}}\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=k_{0}}^{k_{0}+m_{0}-1}\big\|\overline{\mathbf{e}}_{r,t}^{0}\big\|^{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle O_{p}(N^{-1}T^{-\tau}).

Combining (
A.105
)-(
A.109
) yields

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{t=k_{0}}^{k_{0}+l_{2}^{0}-1}(\widetilde{u}_{2,t+1}-\widehat{u}_{2,t+1})^{2}=O_{p}(N^{-1})+O_{p}(N^{-1/2}T^{-1/4})+O_{p}(T^{(\tau-1)/2}).

(A.110)

Then we show that the term in (c) is negligible. Indeed, using the definition
\widetilde{u}_{2,t+1}=u_{t+1}-(\boldsymbol{\widetilde{\delta}}_{t}-\boldsymbol{\delta})^{\prime}\mathbf{z}_{t}
and the result in (
A.78
) gives

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{t=k_{0}}^{k_{0}+l_{2}^{0}-1}\widetilde{u}_{2,t+1}(\widetilde{u}_{2,t+1}-\widehat{u}_{2,t+1})

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{t=k_{0}}^{k_{0}+l_{2}^{0}-1}u_{t+1}((\mathbf{Q}_{N}^{-1}\boldsymbol{\widehat{\delta}}_{t}-\boldsymbol{\widetilde{\delta}}_{t}^{0})^{\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{z}}_{t}^{0}+(\boldsymbol{\widetilde{\alpha}}_{t}-\boldsymbol{\alpha})^{\prime}\overline{\mathbf{e}}_{r,t}^{0}+\boldsymbol{\alpha}^{\prime}\overline{\mathbf{e}}_{r,t}^{0})

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{t=k_{0}}^{k_{0}+l_{2}^{0}-1}(\boldsymbol{\widetilde{\delta}}_{t}-\boldsymbol{\delta})^{\prime}\mathbf{z}_{t}((\mathbf{Q}_{N}^{-1}\boldsymbol{\widehat{\delta}}_{t}-\boldsymbol{\widetilde{\delta}}_{t}^{0})^{\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{z}}_{t}^{0}+(\boldsymbol{\widetilde{\alpha}}_{t}-\boldsymbol{\alpha})^{\prime}\overline{\mathbf{e}}_{r,t}^{0}+\boldsymbol{\alpha}^{\prime}\overline{\mathbf{e}}_{r,t}^{0})

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{t=k_{0}}^{k_{0}+l_{2}^{0}-1}(\mathbf{Q}_{N}^{-1}\boldsymbol{\widehat{\delta}}_{t}-\boldsymbol{\widetilde{\delta}}_{t}^{0})^{\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{z}}_{t}^{0}u_{t+1}+\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{t=k_{0}}^{k_{0}+l_{2}^{0}-1}(\boldsymbol{\widetilde{\alpha}}_{t}-\boldsymbol{\alpha})^{\prime}\overline{\mathbf{e}}_{r,t}^{0}u_{t+1}+\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{t=k_{0}}^{k_{0}+l_{2}^{0}-1}\boldsymbol{\alpha}^{\prime}\overline{\mathbf{e}}_{r,t}^{0}u_{t+1}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{t=k_{0}}^{k_{0}+l_{2}^{0}-1}(\boldsymbol{\widetilde{\delta}}_{t}-\boldsymbol{\delta})^{\prime}\mathbf{z}_{t}(\mathbf{Q}_{N}^{-1}\boldsymbol{\widehat{\delta}}_{t}-\boldsymbol{\widetilde{\delta}}_{t}^{0})^{\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{z}}_{t}^{0}+\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{t=k_{0}}^{k_{0}+l_{2}^{0}-1}(\boldsymbol{\widetilde{\delta}}_{t}-\boldsymbol{\delta})^{\prime}\mathbf{z}_{t}(\boldsymbol{\widetilde{\alpha}}_{t}-\boldsymbol{\alpha})^{\prime}\overline{\mathbf{e}}_{r,t}^{0}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{t=k_{0}}^{k_{0}+l_{2}^{0}-1}(\boldsymbol{\widetilde{\delta}}_{t}-\boldsymbol{\delta})^{\prime}\mathbf{z}_{t}\boldsymbol{\alpha}^{\prime}\overline{\mathbf{e}}_{r,t}^{0}

(A.111)

Note that the first three terms in this expression are the same as in (
A.81
), so we only address the other three terms. Before looking at the fourth term, observe that

\displaystyle\big\|\boldsymbol{\widetilde{\delta}}_{t}-\boldsymbol{\delta}\big\|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\big\|(t^{-1-\tau}\mathbf{Z}^{\prime}\mathbf{Z})^{-1}\big\|\big\|t^{-1-\tau}\mathbf{Z}^{\prime}\mathbf{u}\big\|

(A.112)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle O_{p}(T^{-\frac{1}{2}-\frac{\tau}{2}}).

due to fact that
t^{-1-\tau}\mathbf{Z}^{\prime}\mathbf{Z}\to_{p}\boldsymbol{\Sigma}_{ZZ}
which is positive definite wp1 (Assumption 2). Hence,

\displaystyle\left|\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{t=k_{0}}^{k_{0}+l_{2}^{0}-1}(\boldsymbol{\widetilde{\delta}}_{t}-\boldsymbol{\delta})^{\prime}\mathbf{z}_{t}(\mathbf{Q}_{N}^{-1}\boldsymbol{\widehat{\delta}}_{t}-\boldsymbol{\widetilde{\delta}}_{t}^{0})^{\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{z}}_{t}^{0}\right|

\displaystyle=\left|\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{t=k_{0}}^{k_{0}+l_{2}^{0}-1}T^{\frac{1}{2}+\frac{\tau}{2}}(\boldsymbol{\widetilde{\delta}}_{t}-\boldsymbol{\delta})^{\prime}T^{-\frac{1}{2}-\frac{\tau}{2}}\mathbf{z}_{t}\mathbf{D}_{T}^{-1}\mathbf{D}_{T}(\mathbf{Q}_{N}^{-1}\boldsymbol{\widehat{\delta}}_{t}-\boldsymbol{\widetilde{\delta}}_{t}^{0})^{\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{z}}_{t}^{0}\right|

\displaystyle\leq\big\|\mathbf{D}_{T}^{-1}\big\|\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq k_{0}+l_{2}^{0}-1}\big\|T^{\frac{1}{2}+\frac{\tau}{2}}(\boldsymbol{\widetilde{\delta}}_{t}-\boldsymbol{\delta})\big\|\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq k_{0}+l_{2}^{0}-1}\big\|T^{\frac{\tau}{2}}\mathbf{D}_{T}(\mathbf{Q}_{N}^{-1}\boldsymbol{\widehat{\delta}}_{t}-\boldsymbol{\widetilde{\delta}}_{t}^{0})\big\|\sqrt{\frac{T}{n}}\frac{1}{T^{1+\tau}}\sum_{t=k_{0}}^{k_{0}+l_{2}^{0}-1}\big\|\mathbf{z}_{t}\big\|\big\|\widehat{\mathbf{z}}_{t}^{0}\big\|

\displaystyle\leq MT^{-\frac{1}{4}}\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq k_{0}+l_{2}^{0}-1}\big\|T^{\frac{1}{2}+\frac{\tau}{2}}(\boldsymbol{\widetilde{\delta}}_{t}-\boldsymbol{\delta})\big\|\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq k_{0}+l_{2}^{0}-1}\big\|T^{\frac{\tau}{2}}\mathbf{D}_{T}(\mathbf{Q}_{N}^{-1}\boldsymbol{\widehat{\delta}}_{t}-\boldsymbol{\widetilde{\delta}}_{t}^{0})\big\|\sqrt{\frac{T}{n}}\left(\frac{1}{T^{1+\tau}}\sum_{t=k_{0}}^{k_{0}+l_{2}^{0}-1}\big\|\mathbf{z}_{t}\big\|^{2}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\times\left(\frac{1}{T^{1+\tau}}\sum_{t=k_{0}}^{k_{0}+l_{2}^{0}-1}\big\|\widehat{\mathbf{z}}_{t}^{0}\big\|^{2}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle=O_{p}(N^{-\frac{1}{2}}T^{-\frac{1}{4}})+O_{p}(T^{\frac{\tau}{2}-\frac{1}{2}}),

(A.113)

which is negligible for all values of
\tau\in\left(0,1\right)
. With respect to the fifth term in (
A.111
), by using the results in (
A.79
) and (
A.112
), we have that

\displaystyle\left|\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{t=k_{0}}^{k_{0}+l_{2}^{0}-1}(\boldsymbol{\widetilde{\delta}}_{t}-\boldsymbol{\delta})^{\prime}\mathbf{z}_{t}(\boldsymbol{\widetilde{\alpha}}_{t}-\boldsymbol{\alpha})^{\prime}\overline{\mathbf{e}}_{r,t}^{0}\right|

\displaystyle=\left|\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{t=k_{0}}^{k_{0}+l_{2}^{0}-1}T^{\frac{1}{2}+\frac{\tau}{2}}(\boldsymbol{\widetilde{\delta}}_{t}-\boldsymbol{\delta})^{\prime}T^{-1-\tau}\mathbf{z}_{t}T^{\frac{1}{2}+\frac{\tau}{2}}(\boldsymbol{\widetilde{\alpha}}_{t}-\boldsymbol{\alpha})^{\prime}\overline{\mathbf{e}}_{r,t}^{0}\right|

\displaystyle\leq T^{-\frac{1}{2}}\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq k_{0}+l_{2}^{0}-1}\big\|T^{\frac{1}{2}+\frac{\tau}{2}}(\boldsymbol{\widetilde{\delta}}_{t}-\boldsymbol{\delta})\big\|\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq k_{0}+l_{2}^{0}-1}\big\|T^{\frac{1}{2}+\frac{\tau}{2}}(\boldsymbol{\widetilde{\alpha}}_{t}-\boldsymbol{\alpha})\big\|\sqrt{\frac{T}{n}}\frac{1}{T^{1+\tau}}\sum_{t=k_{0}}^{k_{0}+l_{2}^{0}-1}\big\|\mathbf{z}_{t}\big\|\big\|\overline{\mathbf{e}}_{r,t}^{0}\big\|

\displaystyle\leq T^{-\frac{1}{2}-\frac{\tau}{2}}\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq k_{0}+l_{2}^{0}-1}\big\|T^{\frac{1}{2}+\frac{\tau}{2}}(\boldsymbol{\widetilde{\delta}}_{t}-\boldsymbol{\delta})\big\|\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq k_{0}+l_{2}^{0}-1}\big\|T^{\frac{1}{2}+\frac{\tau}{2}}(\boldsymbol{\widetilde{\alpha}}_{t}-\boldsymbol{\alpha})\big\|\sqrt{\frac{T}{n}}\left(\frac{1}{T^{1+\tau}}\sum_{t=k_{0}}^{k_{0}+l_{2}^{0}-1}\big\|\mathbf{z}_{t}\big\|^{2}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\times\left(\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=k_{0}}^{k_{0}+l_{2}^{0}-1}\big\|\overline{\mathbf{e}}_{r,t}^{0}\big\|^{2}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle=O_{p}(N^{-\frac{1}{2}}T^{-\frac{1}{2}-\frac{\tau}{2}}).

(A.114)

In a similar manner, the last term is

\displaystyle\left|\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{t=k_{0}}^{k_{0}+l_{2}^{0}-1}(\boldsymbol{\widetilde{\delta}}_{t}-\boldsymbol{\delta})^{\prime}\mathbf{z}_{t}\boldsymbol{\alpha}^{\prime}\overline{\mathbf{e}}_{r,t}^{0}\right|

\displaystyle=T^{-\frac{3}{4}-\tau}\left|\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{t=k_{0}}^{k_{0}+l_{2}^{0}-1}T^{\frac{1}{2}+\frac{\tau}{2}}(\boldsymbol{\widetilde{\delta}}_{t}-\boldsymbol{\delta})^{\prime}\mathbf{z}_{t}T^{\frac{1}{4}+\frac{\tau}{2}}\boldsymbol{\alpha}^{\prime}\overline{\mathbf{e}}_{r,t}^{0}\right|

\displaystyle=T^{-\frac{1}{4}}\big\|\boldsymbol{\alpha}^{0}\big\|\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq k_{0}+l_{2}^{0}-1}\big\|T^{\frac{1}{2}+\frac{\tau}{2}}(\boldsymbol{\widetilde{\delta}}_{t}-\boldsymbol{\delta})\big\|\sqrt{\frac{T}{n}}\frac{1}{T^{1+\tau}}\sum_{t=k_{0}}^{k_{0}+l_{2}^{0}-1}\big\|\mathbf{z}_{t}\big\|\big\|\overline{\mathbf{e}}_{r,t}^{0}\big\|

\displaystyle\leq T^{-\frac{1}{4}-\frac{\tau}{2}}\big\|\boldsymbol{\alpha}^{0}\big\|\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq k_{0}+l_{2}^{0}-1}\big\|T^{\frac{1}{2}+\frac{\tau}{2}}(\boldsymbol{\widetilde{\delta}}_{t}-\boldsymbol{\delta})\big\|\sqrt{\frac{T}{n}}\left(\frac{1}{T^{1+\tau}}\sum_{t=k_{0}}^{k_{0}+l_{2}^{0}-1}\big\|\mathbf{z}_{t}\big\|^{2}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\left(\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=k_{0}}^{k_{0}+l_{2}^{0}-1}\big\|\overline{\mathbf{e}}_{r,t}^{0}\big\|^{2}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle=O_{p}(N^{-\frac{1}{2}}T^{-\frac{1}{4}-\frac{\tau}{2}})

(A.115)

Putting all of these results together reduces (
A.111
) to

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{t=k_{0}}^{k_{0}+l_{2}^{0}-1}\widetilde{u}_{2,t+1}(\widetilde{u}_{2,t+1}-\widehat{u}_{2,t+1})=O_{p}(N^{-\frac{1}{2}}T^{-\frac{1}{4}})+O_{p}(T^{\frac{\tau}{2}-\frac{1}{2}})+O_{p}(N^{-\frac{1}{2}}T^{-\frac{\tau}{2}}),

(A.116)

which is negligible for all values of
\tau\in\left(0,1\right)
.

Parts (d) and (e) follow directly based on the logic in
Pitarakis (2025)
, and in particular, the passage from (A.6) to (A.8), which demonstrates that the remainder still vanishes inside of the integral. That is

\displaystyle\left|\frac{1}{n}\sum_{l_{2}=\lfloor n\nu_{0}\rfloor}^{n}\frac{n}{l_{2}}\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{t=k_{0}}^{k_{0}+l_{2}-1}(\widetilde{u}_{2,t+1}-\widehat{u}_{2,t+1})^{2}\right|

\displaystyle\leq\sup_{\lfloor n\nu_{0}\rfloor\leq t\leq n}\left|\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{t=k_{0}}^{k_{0}+l_{2}-1}(\widetilde{u}_{2,t+1}-\widehat{u}_{2,t+1})^{2}\right|\frac{1}{n}\sum_{l_{2}=\lfloor n\nu_{0}\rfloor}^{n}\frac{n}{l_{2}}

\displaystyle\leq\sup_{\lfloor n\nu_{0}\rfloor\leq t\leq n}\left|\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{t=k_{0}}^{k_{0}+l_{2}-1}(\widetilde{u}_{2,t+1}-\widehat{u}_{2,t+1})^{2}\right|\int_{\lambda_{2}=\nu_{0}}^{1}\frac{1}{\lambda_{2}}d\lambda_{2}

\displaystyle+o_{p}(1)

\displaystyle=o_{p}(1),

(A.117)

and for (e) the argument is identical.

Finally, consider (f). Let us define
\phi^{2}=\lim_{T\to\infty}Var\left[\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{t=k_{0}}^{T-1}(u_{2,t+1}^{2}-\mathbb{E}(u_{2,t+1}^{2}))\right]
. By Assumption 1, the error process
u_{t}
is a martingale difference sequence so that

\displaystyle\phi^{2}=\lim_{T\to\infty}\frac{1}{n}\sum_{t=k_{0}}^{T-1}\mathbb{E}(u_{2,t+1}^{2}-\mathbb{E}(u_{2,t+1}^{2}))^{2},

(A.118)

Then the natural unfeasible and feasible estimators of (
A.118
) are obtained as

\displaystyle\widehat{\phi}^{2}=\frac{1}{n}\sum_{t=k_{0}}^{T-1}\left(\widehat{u}_{2,t+1}^{2}-\frac{1}{n}\sum_{t=k_{0}}^{T-1}\widehat{u}_{2,t+1}^{2}\right)^{2},

(A.119)

and

\displaystyle\widetilde{\phi}^{2}=\frac{1}{n}\sum_{t=k_{0}}^{T-1}\left(\widetilde{u}^{2}_{2,t+1}-\frac{1}{n}\sum_{t=k_{0}}^{T-1}\widetilde{u}_{2,t+1}^{2}\right)^{2}

(A.120)

respectively. To demonstrate (e), it is convenient to start from the decomposition of
Margaritella and Stauskas (2024)
:

\displaystyle\widetilde{\phi}^{2}-\widehat{\phi}^{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{2}{n}\sum_{t=k_{0}}^{T-1}\left(\widetilde{u}^{2}_{2,t+1}-\frac{1}{n}\sum_{t=k_{0}}^{T-1}\widetilde{u}_{2,t+1}^{2}\right)\left((\widetilde{u}^{2}_{2,t+1}-\widehat{u}^{2}_{2,t+1})+\left[\frac{1}{n}\sum_{t=k_{0}}^{T-1}(\widetilde{u}_{2,t+1}^{2}-\widehat{u}_{2,t+1}^{2})\right]\right)

(A.121)

\displaystyle-

\displaystyle\frac{1}{n}\sum_{t=k_{0}}^{T-1}\left((\widetilde{u}^{2}_{2,t+1}-\widehat{u}^{2}_{2,t+1})+\left[\frac{1}{n}\sum_{t=k_{0}}^{T-1}(\widetilde{u}_{2,t+1}^{2}-\widehat{u}_{2,t+1}^{2})\right]\right)^{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{2}{n}\sum_{t=k_{0}}^{T-1}\widetilde{u}^{2}_{2,t+1}(\widetilde{u}^{2}_{2,t+1}-\widehat{u}^{2}_{2,t+1})-2\left(\frac{1}{n}\sum_{t=k_{0}}^{T-1}\widetilde{u}_{2,t+1}^{2}\right)\frac{1}{n}\sum_{t=k_{0}}^{T-1}(\widetilde{u}^{2}_{2,t+1}-\widehat{u}^{2}_{2,t+1})

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle 2\left(\frac{1}{n}\sum_{t=k_{0}}^{T-1}(\widetilde{u}_{2,t+1}^{2}-\widehat{u}_{2,t+1}^{2})\right)\frac{1}{n}\sum_{t=k_{0}}^{T-1}\widetilde{u}^{2}_{2,t+1}-2\left(\frac{1}{n}\sum_{t=k_{0}}^{T-1}\widetilde{u}_{2,t+1}^{2}\right)\frac{1}{n}\sum_{t=k_{0}}^{T-1}(\widetilde{u}_{2,t+1}^{2}-\widehat{u}_{2,t+1}^{2})

\displaystyle-

\displaystyle\frac{1}{n}\sum_{t=k_{0}}^{T-1}\left(\widetilde{u}^{2}_{2,t+1}-\widehat{u}^{2}_{2,t+1}\right)^{2}-\left(\frac{1}{n}\sum_{t=k_{0}}^{T-1}(\widetilde{u}_{2,t+1}^{2}-\widehat{u}_{2,t+1}^{2})\right)^{2}

\displaystyle-

\displaystyle 2\left(\frac{1}{n}\sum_{t=k_{0}}^{T-1}(\widetilde{u}_{2,t+1}^{2}-\widehat{u}_{2,t+1}^{2})\right)\frac{1}{n}\sum_{t=k_{0}}^{T-1}(\widetilde{u}^{2}_{2,t+1}-\widehat{u}^{2}_{2,t+1})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{2}{n}\sum_{t=k_{0}}^{T-1}\widetilde{u}^{2}_{2,t+1}(\widetilde{u}^{2}_{2,t+1}-\widehat{u}^{2}_{2,t+1})-2\left(\frac{1}{n}\sum_{t=k_{0}}^{T-1}\widetilde{u}_{2,t+1}^{2}\right)\frac{1}{n}\sum_{t=k_{0}}^{T-1}(\widetilde{u}_{2,t+1}^{2}-\widehat{u}_{2,t+1}^{2})

\displaystyle-

\displaystyle\frac{1}{n}\sum_{t=k_{0}}^{T-1}\left(\widetilde{u}^{2}_{2,t+1}-\widehat{u}^{2}_{2,t+1}\right)^{2}-3\left(\frac{1}{n}\sum_{t=k_{0}}^{T-1}(\widetilde{u}_{2,t+1}^{2}-\widehat{u}_{2,t+1}^{2})\right)^{2}

which is consistent if the remainder is negligible. For this purpose, observe that the difference of squares can be rewritten as
\widetilde{u}^{2}_{2,t+1}-\widehat{u}^{2}_{2,t+1}=2\widetilde{u}_{2,t+1}(\widetilde{u}_{2,t+1}-\widehat{u}_{2,t+1})-(\widetilde{u}_{2,t+1}-\widehat{u}_{2,t+1})^{2}
so that the fourth term in (
A.121
) becomes

\displaystyle\left|\frac{1}{n}\sum_{t=k_{0}}^{T-1}(\widetilde{u}_{2,t+1}^{2}-\widehat{u}_{2,t+1}^{2})\right|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle 2\left|\frac{1}{n}\sum_{t=k_{0}}^{T-1}\widetilde{u}_{2,t+1}(\widetilde{u}_{2,t+1}-\widehat{u}_{2,t+1})\right|+\left|\frac{1}{n}\sum_{t=k_{0}}^{T-1}(\widetilde{u}_{2,t+1}-\widehat{u}_{2,t+1})^{2}\right|

(A.122)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle o_{p}(n^{-\frac{1}{2}})

by part (a) and (b) of this lemma. Next, we can show that
\widetilde{u}_{2,t+1}^{2}
is a consistent estimator of
u_{t+1}^{2}
uniformly in
t
:

\displaystyle\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\widetilde{u}_{2,t+1}^{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}(u_{t+1}-(\boldsymbol{\widetilde{\delta}}_{t}-\boldsymbol{\delta})^{\prime}\mathbf{z}_{t})^{2}

(A.123)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 2\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}u_{t+1}^{2}+2\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}((\boldsymbol{\widetilde{\delta}}_{t}-\boldsymbol{\delta})^{\prime}\mathbf{z}_{t})^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 2\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}u_{t+1}^{2}+2T^{-1}\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\big\|T^{\frac{1}{2}+\frac{\tau}{2}}(\boldsymbol{\widetilde{\delta}}_{t}-\boldsymbol{\delta})\big\|^{2}\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\big\|T^{-\frac{\tau}{2}}\mathbf{z}_{t}\big\|^{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle 2\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}u_{t+1}^{2}+O_{p}(T^{-1})=O_{p}(1),

where
\big\|T^{-\frac{\tau}{2}}\mathbf{z}_{t}\big\|^{2}=O_{p}(1)
follows from the results of Lemma 3.1 of
Magdalinos and Phillips (2009)
. Now, this expression implies that the first and second term in (
A.121
) are

\displaystyle\left|\frac{1}{n}\sum_{t=k_{0}}^{T-1}\widetilde{u}^{2}_{2,t+1}(\widetilde{u}^{2}_{2,t+1}-\widehat{u}^{2}_{2,t+1})\right|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 2\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\widetilde{u}_{2,t+1}^{2}\frac{1}{n}\sum_{t=k_{0}}^{T-1}(\widetilde{u}^{2}_{2,t+1}-\widehat{u}^{2}_{2,t+1})

(A.124)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle o_{p}(n^{-\frac{1}{2}})

and

\displaystyle\left|\left(\frac{1}{n}\sum_{t=k_{0}}^{T-1}\widetilde{u}_{2,t+1}^{2}\right)\frac{1}{n}\sum_{t=k_{0}}^{T-1}(\widetilde{u}_{2,t+1}^{2}-\widehat{u}_{2,t+1}^{2})\right|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\widetilde{u}_{2,t+1}^{2}\frac{1}{n}\sum_{t=k_{0}}^{T-1}(\widetilde{u}^{2}_{2,t+1}-\widehat{u}^{2}_{2,t+1})

(A.125)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle o_{p}(n^{-\frac{1}{2}}).

Hence, it remains to look at the third term. From previous results, we see that

\displaystyle\frac{1}{n}\sum_{t=k_{0}}^{T-1}\left(\widetilde{u}^{2}_{2,t+1}-\widehat{u}^{2}_{2,t+1}\right)^{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{n}\sum_{t=k_{0}}^{T-1}\left(2\widetilde{u}_{2,t+1}(\widetilde{u}_{2,t+1}-\widehat{u}_{2,t+1})-(\widetilde{u}_{2,t+1}-\widehat{u}_{2,t+1})^{2}\right)^{2}

(A.126)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{4}{n}\sum_{t=k_{0}}^{T-1}\widetilde{u}^{2}_{2,t+1}(\widetilde{u}^{2}_{2,t+1}-\widehat{u}^{2}_{2,t+1})-\frac{4}{n}\sum_{t=k_{0}}^{T-1}\widetilde{u}_{2,t+1}(\widetilde{u}_{2,t+1}-\widehat{u}_{2,t+1})^{3}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\frac{1}{n}\sum_{t=k_{0}}^{T-1}(\widetilde{u}_{2,t+1}-\widehat{u}_{2,t+1})^{4}

Here,

\displaystyle\left|\frac{1}{n}\sum_{t=k_{0}}^{T-1}\widetilde{u}^{2}_{2,t+1}(\widetilde{u}^{2}_{2,t+1}-\widehat{u}^{2}_{2,t+1})\right|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\widetilde{u}_{2,t+1}^{2}\left|\frac{1}{n}\sum_{t=k_{0}}^{T-1}(\widetilde{u}^{2}_{2,t+1}-\widehat{u}^{2}_{2,t+1})\right|

(A.127)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle o_{p}(n^{-\frac{1}{2}}),

while using the result in (
A.78
) gives

\displaystyle\left|\frac{1}{n}\sum_{t=k_{0}}^{T-1}\widetilde{u}_{2,t+1}(\widetilde{u}_{2,t+1}-\widehat{u}_{2,t+1})^{3}\right|

\displaystyle=\left|\frac{1}{n}\sum_{t=k_{0}}^{T-1}\widetilde{u}_{2,t+1}(\widetilde{u}_{2,t+1}-\widehat{u}_{2,t+1})^{2}((\mathbf{Q}_{N}^{-1}\boldsymbol{\widehat{\delta}}_{t}-\boldsymbol{\widetilde{\delta}}_{t}^{0})^{\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{z}}_{t}^{0}+(\boldsymbol{\widetilde{\alpha}}_{t}-\boldsymbol{\alpha})^{\prime}\overline{\mathbf{e}}_{r,t}^{0}+\boldsymbol{\alpha}^{\prime}\overline{\mathbf{e}}_{r,t}^{0})\right|

\displaystyle\leq\big\|\mathbf{D}_{T}^{-1}\big\|\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\left|\widetilde{u}_{2,t+1}\right|\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\big\|\mathbf{D}_{T}(\mathbf{Q}_{N}^{-1}\boldsymbol{\widehat{\delta}}_{t}-\boldsymbol{\widetilde{\delta}}_{t}^{0})\big\|\frac{T}{n}\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=k_{0}}^{T-1}(\widetilde{u}_{2,t+1}-\widehat{u}_{2,t+1})^{2}\big\|\widehat{\mathbf{z}}_{t}^{0}\big\|

\displaystyle+T^{-\frac{1}{2}-\frac{\tau}{2}}\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\left|\widetilde{u}_{2,t+1}\right|\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\big\|T^{\frac{1}{2}+\frac{\tau}{2}}(\boldsymbol{\widetilde{\alpha}}_{t}-\boldsymbol{\alpha})\big\|\frac{T}{n}\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=k_{0}}^{T-1}(\widetilde{u}_{2,t+1}-\widehat{u}_{2,t+1})^{2}\big\|\overline{\mathbf{e}}_{r,t}^{0}\big\|

\displaystyle+T^{-\frac{1}{4}-\frac{\tau}{2}}\big\|\boldsymbol{\alpha}^{0}\big\|\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\left|\widetilde{u}_{2,t+1}\right|\frac{T}{n}\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=k_{0}}^{T-1}(\widetilde{u}_{2,t+1}-\widehat{u}_{2,t+1})^{2}\big\|\overline{\mathbf{e}}_{r,t}^{0}\big\|

\displaystyle\leq MT^{-\frac{1}{4}}\frac{T}{n}\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\left|\widetilde{u}_{2,t+1}\right|\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\big\|T^{\frac{\tau}{2}}\mathbf{D}_{T}(\mathbf{Q}_{N}^{-1}\boldsymbol{\widehat{\delta}}_{t}-\boldsymbol{\widetilde{\delta}}_{t}^{0})\big\|\left(\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=k_{0}}^{T-1}(\widetilde{u}_{2,t+1}-\widehat{u}_{2,t+1})^{4}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\left(\frac{1}{T^{1+\tau}}\sum_{t=k_{0}}^{T-1}\big\|\widehat{\mathbf{z}}_{t}^{0}\big\|^{2}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle+T^{-\frac{1}{2}-\frac{\tau}{2}}\frac{T}{n}\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\left|\widetilde{u}_{2,t+1}\right|\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\big\|T^{\frac{1}{2}+\frac{\tau}{2}}(\boldsymbol{\widetilde{\alpha}}_{t}-\boldsymbol{\alpha})\big\|\left(\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=k_{0}}^{T-1}(\widetilde{u}_{2,t+1}-\widehat{u}_{2,t+1})^{4}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\left(\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=k_{0}}^{T-1}\big\|\overline{\mathbf{e}}_{r,t}^{0}\big\|^{2}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle+T^{-\frac{1}{4}-\frac{\tau}{2}}\frac{T}{n}\big\|\boldsymbol{\alpha}^{0}\big\|\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\left|\widetilde{u}_{2,t+1}\right|\left(\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=k_{0}}^{T-1}(\widetilde{u}_{2,t+1}-\widehat{u}_{2,t+1})^{4}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\left(\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=k_{0}}^{T-1}\big\|\overline{\mathbf{e}}_{r,t}^{0}\big\|^{2}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle=o_{p}(N^{-\frac{1}{2}}T^{-\frac{1}{4}})+o_{p}(T^{\frac{\tau}{2}-\frac{1}{2}})+o_{p}(N^{-\frac{1}{2}}T^{-\frac{1}{2}-\frac{\tau}{2}})+o_{p}(N^{-\frac{1}{2}}T^{-\frac{1}{4}-\frac{\tau}{2}})

\displaystyle=o_{p}(N^{-\frac{1}{2}}T^{-\frac{1}{4}})+o_{p}(T^{\frac{\tau}{2}-\frac{1}{2}}).

(A.128)

since one can show that
\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=k_{0}}^{T-1}(\widetilde{u}_{2,t+1}-\widehat{u}_{2,t+1})^{4}=o_{p}(1)
. Indeed, start by noticing that

\displaystyle\frac{1}{T^{2+2\tau}}\sum_{t=k_{0}}^{T-1}\big\|\widehat{\mathbf{z}}_{t}^{0}\big\|^{4}\leq\left(\frac{1}{T^{1+\tau}}\sum_{t=k_{0}}^{T-1}\big\|\widehat{\mathbf{z}}_{t}^{0}\big\|^{2}\right)^{2}=O_{p}(1).

(A.129)

Substituting the expression (
A.78
) into the quartic term of (
A.126
) then gives

\displaystyle\left|\frac{1}{n}\sum_{t=k_{0}}^{T-1}(\widetilde{u}_{2,t+1}-\widehat{u}_{2,t+1})^{4}\right|

(A.130)

\displaystyle=\left|\frac{1}{n}\sum_{t=k_{0}}^{T-1}((\mathbf{Q}_{N}^{-1}\boldsymbol{\widehat{\delta}}_{t}-\boldsymbol{\widetilde{\delta}}_{t}^{0})^{\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{z}}_{t}^{0}+(\boldsymbol{\widetilde{\alpha}}_{t}-\boldsymbol{\alpha})^{\prime}\overline{\mathbf{e}}_{r,t}^{0}+\boldsymbol{\alpha}^{\prime}\overline{\mathbf{e}}_{r,t}^{0})^{4}\right|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{4}{n}\sum_{t=k_{0}}^{T-1}\left|(\mathbf{Q}_{N}^{-1}\boldsymbol{\widehat{\delta}}_{t}-\boldsymbol{\widetilde{\delta}}_{t}^{0})^{\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{z}}_{t}^{0}\right|^{4}+\frac{4}{n}\sum_{t=k_{0}}^{T-1}\left|(\boldsymbol{\widetilde{\alpha}}_{t}-\boldsymbol{\alpha})^{\prime}\overline{\mathbf{e}}_{r,t}^{0}\right|^{4}+\frac{4}{n}\sum_{t=k_{0}}^{T-1}\left|\boldsymbol{\alpha}^{\prime}\overline{\mathbf{e}}_{r,t}^{0}\right|^{4}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{4}{n}\sum_{t=k_{0}}^{T-1}\big\|\mathbf{D}_{T}(\mathbf{Q}_{N}^{-1}\boldsymbol{\widehat{\delta}}_{t}-\boldsymbol{\widetilde{\delta}}_{t}^{0})\big\|^{4}\big\|\mathbf{D}_{T}^{-1}\widehat{\mathbf{z}}_{t}^{0}\big\|^{4}

\displaystyle+T^{-2-2\tau}\frac{4}{n}\sum_{t=k_{0}}^{T-1}\big\|T^{\frac{1}{2}+\frac{\tau}{2}}(\boldsymbol{\widetilde{\alpha}}_{t}-\boldsymbol{\alpha})\big\|^{4}\big\|\overline{\mathbf{e}}_{r,t}^{0}\big\|^{4}

\displaystyle+T^{-1-2\tau}\frac{4}{n}\sum_{t=k_{0}}^{T-1}\big\|T^{\frac{1}{4}+\frac{\tau}{2}}\boldsymbol{\alpha}\big\|^{4}\big\|\overline{\mathbf{e}}_{r,t}^{0}\big\|^{4}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 4\frac{T}{n}\big\|\mathbf{D}_{T}^{-1}\big\|^{4}\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\big\|T^{\frac{\tau}{2}}\mathbf{D}_{T}(\mathbf{Q}_{N}^{-1}\boldsymbol{\widehat{\delta}}_{t}-\boldsymbol{\widetilde{\delta}}_{t}^{0})\big\|^{4}\frac{1}{T^{1+2\tau}}\sum_{t=k_{0}}^{T-1}\big\|\widehat{\mathbf{z}}_{t}^{0}\big\|^{4}

\displaystyle+4T^{-2-2\tau}\frac{T}{n}\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\big\|T^{\frac{1}{2}+\frac{\tau}{2}}(\boldsymbol{\widetilde{\alpha}}_{t}-\boldsymbol{\alpha})\big\|^{4}\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=k_{0}}^{T-1}\big\|\overline{\mathbf{e}}_{r,t}^{0}\big\|^{4}

\displaystyle+T^{-1-2\tau}\frac{T}{n}\big\|\boldsymbol{\alpha}^{0}\big\|^{4}\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=k_{0}}^{T-1}\big\|\overline{\mathbf{e}}_{r,t}^{0}\big\|^{4}

\displaystyle\leq MT^{-1}\frac{T}{n}\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\big\|T^{\frac{\tau}{2}}\mathbf{D}_{T}(\mathbf{Q}_{N}^{-1}\boldsymbol{\widehat{\delta}}_{t}-\boldsymbol{\widetilde{\delta}}_{t}^{0})\big\|^{4}\frac{1}{T^{2+2\tau}}\sum_{t=k_{0}}^{T-1}\big\|\widehat{\mathbf{z}}_{t}^{0}\big\|^{4}+O_{p}(N^{-2}T^{-1-2\tau})

\displaystyle=O_{p}(N^{-2})+O_{p}(T^{2\tau-1})

\displaystyle=o_{p}(1),

which is negligible if
\tau\in\left(0,\frac{1}{2}\right)
. Hence,

\displaystyle\left|\frac{1}{n}\sum_{t=k_{0}}^{T-1}\left(\widetilde{u}^{2}_{2,t+1}-\widehat{u}^{2}_{2,t+1}\right)^{2}\right|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle o_{p}(1).

(A.131)

Therefore, combining all of the results in (
A.122
)-(
A.130
) leads to

\displaystyle\left|\widetilde{\phi}^{2}-\widehat{\phi}^{2}\right|=o_{p}(1),

(A.132)

as desired.
\blacksquare

7.4
Additional Results: Theory

7.4.1
A Comment on Multi-Step Forecast Errors

In
h
-step-ahead forecast context,
\mathbb{E}(u_{t+h}|\mathcal{F}_{t})=0
implies
MA(h-1)
dependence structure in
\{u_{t+h}\}
. In this section, we explore the rates of convergence of the key components in our proofs under multi-step ahead forecasts. The results provided below directly imply that the asymptotic equivalence result holds under
h
-step-ahead forecasts for
h>1
.

7.4.2
Convergence Rates

Lemma A.4

Under Assumptions 1-4 and forecast error following an
MA(h-1)
process, we have that

\displaystyle\left\|\frac{1}{T^{1+\tau}}\mathbf{Z}^{\prime}\mathbf{u}\right\|=O_{p}(T^{-(1+\tau)/2})

as
T\to\infty
.

Proof
. By Markov’s inequality,

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}

\displaystyle\left(\left\|\frac{1}{T^{1+\tau}}\sum_{t=1}^{T-h}\mathbf{z}_{t}u_{t+h}\right\|>\epsilon\right)\leq\epsilon^{-2}\mathbb{E}\left(\left\|\frac{1}{T^{1+\tau}}\sum_{t=1}^{T-h}\mathbf{z}_{t}u_{t+h}\right\|^{2}\right)

\displaystyle=\epsilon^{-2}\mathbb{E}\left(\frac{1}{T^{2+2\tau}}\sum_{t=1}^{T-h}\sum_{s=1}^{T-h}\mathbf{z}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{z}_{s}u_{t+h}u_{s+h}\right)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{T^{2+2\tau}}\sum_{t=1}^{T-h}\sum_{s=1}^{T-h}\mathbb{E}\left(\mathbf{z}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{z}_{s}\right)\mathbb{E}(u_{t+h}u_{s+h})

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{T^{2+2\tau}}\sum_{t=1}^{T-h}\sum_{s=\max\{1,t-h+1\}}^{\min\{T-h,t+h-1\}}\mathbb{E}\left(\mathbf{z}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{z}_{s}\right)\mathbb{C}ov(u_{t+h},u_{s+h})

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{T^{2+2\tau}}\sum_{t=1}^{T-h}\sum_{s=\max\{1,t-h+1\}}^{\min\{T-h,t+h-1\}}\mathbb{E}\left(|\mathbf{z}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{z}_{s}|\right)\left|\mathbb{C}ov(u_{t+h},u_{s+h})\right|

\displaystyle\leq\sup_{t,s}\left|\mathbb{C}ov(u_{t+h},u_{s+h})\right|\frac{1}{T^{2+2\tau}}\sum_{t=1}^{T-h}\sum_{s=\max\{1,t-h+1\}}^{\min\{T-h,t+h-1\}}\mathbb{E}\left(|\mathbf{z}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{z}_{s}|\right)

\displaystyle\leq\sup_{t,s}\left|\mathbb{C}ov(u_{t+h},u_{s+h})\right|\frac{1}{T^{2+2\tau}}\sum_{t=1}^{T-h}\sum_{s=\max\{1,t-h+1\}}^{\min\{T-h,t+h-1\}}\mathbb{E}\left(\left\|\mathbf{z}_{t}\right\|\left\|\mathbf{z}_{s}\right\|\right)

\displaystyle\leq\sup_{t,s}\left|\mathbb{C}ov(u_{t+h},u_{s+h})\right|\frac{1}{T^{2+2\tau}}\sum_{t=1}^{T-h}\left[\mathbb{E}\left(\left\|\mathbf{z}_{t}\right\|^{2}\right)\right]^{1/2}\sum_{s=\max\{1,t-h+1\}}^{\min\{T-h,t+h-1\}}\left[\mathbb{E}\left(\left\|\mathbf{z}_{s}\right\|^{2}\right)\right]^{1/2}

\displaystyle=\sup_{t,s}\left|\mathbb{C}ov(u_{t+h},u_{s+h})\right|\frac{1}{T^{2+\tau}}\sum_{t=1}^{T-h}\left[\mathbb{E}\left(\left\|T^{-\tau/2}\mathbf{z}_{t}\right\|^{2}\right)\right]^{1/2}\sum_{s=\max\{1,t-h+1\}}^{\min\{T-h,t+h-1\}}\left[\mathbb{E}\left(\left\|T^{-\tau/2}\mathbf{z}_{s}\right\|^{2}\right)\right]^{1/2}

\displaystyle=T^{-1-\tau}\sup_{t,s}\left|\mathbb{C}ov(u_{t+h},u_{s+h})\right|\sup_{t}\mathbb{E}\left(\left\|T^{-\tau/2}\mathbf{z}_{t}\right\|^{2}\right)C\frac{T-h-1}{T}

\displaystyle=O(T^{-1-\tau}),

(A.133)

for some positive
C
. Here we used the fact that
\sup_{t}\left[\mathbb{E}\left(\left\|T^{-\tau/2}\mathbf{z}_{t}\right\|^{2}\right)\right]^{1/2}\times\sup_{s}\left[\mathbb{E}\left(\left\|T^{-\tau/2}\mathbf{z}_{s}\right\|^{2}\right)\right]^{1/2}=\sup_{t}\mathbb{E}\left(\left\|T^{-\tau/2}\mathbf{z}_{t}\right\|^{2}\right)=O(1)
, and the fact that the sum over an index
s
is necessarily bounded in case of
MA(h-1)
process. This implies that
\left\|\frac{1}{T^{1+\tau}}\sum_{t=1}^{T-h}\mathbf{z}_{t}u_{t+h}\right\|=O_{p}(T^{-(1+\tau)/2})
.

7.4.3
Verification of Uniform Rates

In several steps of our proofs, especially that of Lemma 2, we need to have
\sup_{t}\left\|\widetilde{\boldsymbol{\delta}}_{t}-\boldsymbol{\delta}\right\|=O_{p}(T^{-(1+\tau)/2})
(and several similar least squares deviations) to demonstrate that the factor estimation error is negligible as
(N,T)\to\infty
. Clearly,
\left\|\widetilde{\boldsymbol{\delta}}_{\lfloor T\kappa\rfloor}-\boldsymbol{\delta}\right\|\leq\left\|\left(\frac{1}{T^{1+\tau}}\sum_{s=1}^{{\lfloor T\kappa\rfloor}-h}\mathbf{z}_{s}\mathbf{z}_{s}^{\prime}\right)^{-1}\right\|\left\|\frac{1}{T^{1+\tau}}\sum_{s=1}^{{\lfloor T\kappa\rfloor}-h}\mathbf{z}_{s}u_{s+h}\right\|
, where the denominator is bounded uniformly by Assumption 2 (c). For the numerator, we can use the same argument as in A.11 of
Magdalinos and Phillips (2009)
, where for all
t=1,\ldots,T
(equivalently,
\kappa\in(0,1)
), we create a bound:

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left(\left\|\frac{1}{T^{1+\tau}}\sum_{s=1}^{{\lfloor T\kappa\rfloor}-h}\mathbf{z}_{s}u_{s+h}\right\|^{2}\right)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{T^{2+2\tau}}\sum_{s=1}^{{\lfloor T\kappa\rfloor}-h}\sum_{j=1}^{\lfloor T\kappa\rfloor-h}\mathbb{E}(\mathbf{z}_{s}^{\prime}\mathbf{z}_{j})\mathbb{C}ov(u_{s+h},u_{j+h})

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{T^{2+2\tau}}\sum_{s=1}^{\lfloor T\kappa\rfloor-h}\sum_{j=\max\{1,s-h+1\}}^{\min\{\lfloor T\kappa\rfloor-h,s+h-1\}}\mathbb{E}(\mathbf{z}_{s}^{\prime}\mathbf{z}_{j})\mathbb{C}ov(u_{s+h},u_{j+h})

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{T^{2+2\tau}}\sum_{s=1}^{\lfloor T\kappa\rfloor-h}\sum_{j=\max\{1,s-h+1\}}^{\min\{\lfloor T\kappa\rfloor-h,s+h-1\}}\mathbb{E}(|\mathbf{z}_{s}^{\prime}\mathbf{z}_{j}|)|\mathbb{C}ov(u_{s+h},u_{j+h})|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{T^{2+2\tau}}\sum_{s=1}^{T-h}\sum_{j=\max\{1,s-h+1\}}^{\min\{T-h,s+h-1\}}\mathbb{E}(|\mathbf{z}_{s}^{\prime}\mathbf{z}_{j}|)|\mathbb{C}ov(u_{s+h},u_{j+h})|

\displaystyle=O(T^{-1-\tau}),

(A.134)

where we created the second inequality by simply summing more positive terms. The rest follows from (
A.133
). Similar least squares deviations can be checked in an analogous fashion. For example
\left\|\widetilde{\boldsymbol{\alpha}}_{\lfloor T\kappa\rfloor}-\boldsymbol{\alpha}\right\|\leq\left\|\left(\frac{1}{T^{1+\tau}}\mathbf{F}^{\prime}\mathbf{M}_{\mathbf{W}}\mathbf{F}\right)^{-1}\right\|\left\|\frac{1}{T^{1+\tau}}\mathbf{F}^{\prime}\mathbf{M}_{\mathbf{W}}\mathbf{u}\right\|
, where the denominator is bounded by our assumptions, while the numerator is negligible uniformly in
t
, as well, because

\displaystyle\left\|\frac{1}{T^{1+\tau}}\mathbf{F}^{\prime}\mathbf{M}_{\mathbf{W}}\mathbf{u}\right\|\leq\underbrace{\left\|\frac{1}{T^{1+\tau}}\sum_{s=1}^{\lfloor T\kappa\rfloor-h}\mathbf{f}_{s}u_{s+h}\right\|}_{O_{p}(T^{-(1+\tau)/2})}

\displaystyle+\left\|\frac{1}{T^{1+\tau}}\sum_{s=1}^{\lfloor T\kappa\rfloor-h}\mathbf{f}_{s}\mathbf{w}_{s}^{\prime}\right\|\left\|\left(\frac{1}{T^{1+\tau}}\sum_{s=1}^{\lfloor T\kappa\rfloor-h}\mathbf{w}_{s}\mathbf{w}_{s}^{\prime}\right)^{-1}\right\|

\displaystyle\times\underbrace{\left\|\frac{1}{T^{1+\tau}}\sum_{s=1}^{\lfloor T\kappa\rfloor-h}\mathbf{w}_{s}u_{s+h}\right\|}_{O_{p}(T^{-(1+\tau)/2})}=O_{p}(T^{-(1+\tau)/2}),

(A.135)

where the rates come immediately form
A.134
.

7.4.4
Power Enhancements

The statistics
s_{f,j}
for
j=2,3,4
can be power-enhanced, as argued in
Pitarakis (2025)
. The power adjustment is derived by replacing
\widetilde{u}_{2,t+1}^{2}
with
\breve{u}_{2,t+1}=\widetilde{u}_{2,t+1}^{2}-(\widetilde{u}_{1,t+1}-\widetilde{u}_{2,t+1})^{2}
. Clearly, we only have
\widehat{u}_{2,t+1}^{2}
, therefore, we work with the feasible power-adjustment term. For
s_{\widehat{f},2}
, for instance, this means that we need to add

\displaystyle\widehat{\triangle}_{2}=\frac{1}{\widehat{\omega}_{2}}\frac{1}{\lambda_{2}^{0}}\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{t=k_{0}}^{k_{0}+l_{2}^{0}-1}(\widetilde{u}_{1,t+1}-\widehat{u}_{2,t+1})^{2}

to the initial statistic to obtain
